Seeing Fast and Slow: Learning the Flow of Time in Videos
Authors: Yen-Siang Wu, Rundong Luo, Jingsen Zhu, Tao Tu, Ali Farhadi, Matthew Wallingford, Yu-Chiang Frank Wang, Steve Marschner, Wei-Chiu Ma
First: 2026-04-23T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:59:57+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://seeing-fast-and-slow.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
How can we tell whether a video has been sped up or slowed down? How can we generate videos at different speeds? Although videos have been central to modern computer vision research, little attention has been paid to perceiving and controlling the passage of time. In this paper, we study time as a learnable visual concept and develop models for reasoning about and manipulating the flow of time in videos. We first exploit the multimodal cues and temporal structure naturally present in videos to learn, in a self-supervised manner, to detect speed changes and estimate playback speed. We then show that these learned temporal reasoning models enable us to curate the largest slow-motion video dataset to date from noisy in-the-wild sources. Such slow-motion footage, typically filmed by high-speed cameras, contains substantially richer temporal detail than standard videos. Using this data, we further develop models capable of temporal control, including speed-conditioned video generation, which produces motion at specified playback speed, and temporal super-resolution, which tranforms low-FPS, blurry videos into high-FPS sequences with fine-grained temporal details. Our findings highlight time as a manipulable, perceptual dimension in video learning, opening doors to temporally controllable video generation, temporal forensics detection, and potentially richer world-models that understand how events unfold over time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：快与慢的视觉：学习视频中的时间流
我们如何判断视频是加速还是减速的？如何生成不同速度的视频？尽管视频一直是现代计算机视觉研究的核心，但对时间感知与控制的关注却很少。本文将时间视为可学习的视觉概念，开发了用于推理和操控视频中时间流的模型。我们首先利用视频中固有的多模态线索和时序结构，以自监督方式学习检测速度变化并估计播放速度。接着，我们证明这些学习到的时间推理模型能够从野外噪声数据中构建迄今最大的慢动作视频数据集。此类慢动作视频通常由高速摄像机拍摄，其时间细节远比标准视频丰富。基于此数据，我们进一步开发了具备时间控制能力的模型，包括速度条件视频生成（可生成指定播放速度的运动）和时序超分辨率（将低帧率、模糊视频转换为高帧率、细节丰富的时序序列）。我们的研究强调时间作为视频学习中可操控的感知维度，为时序可控视频生成、时间取证检测以及更深入理解事件随时间演化的世界模型开辟了新路径。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of perceiving and controlling time in videos, motivated by the lack of explicit modeling of temporal flow in computer vision. The authors develop self-supervised models that learn to detect speed changes and estimate playback rates by leveraging inherent multimodal and temporal cues in video data. These models are then applied to curate the largest slow-motion video dataset from real-world sources, enabling the training of temporal control systems. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of these models in generating videos at specified speeds and enhancing low-FPS footage into high-FPS sequences with fine-grained motion details, showcasing time as a learnable and manipulable dimension in video understanding.
该论文旨在解决视频中时间感知与控制这一长期被忽视的问题，动机源于对视频播放速度检测与调整的需求。作者构建了自监督模型，通过利用视频中的多模态线索和时序结构，学习检测速度变化并估计播放速率。这些模型进一步用于从真实世界噪声数据中构建迄今最大的慢动作视频数据集，该数据包含比普通视频更丰富的时序细节。基于此数据，作者开发了时序控制模型，包括速度条件视频生成和时序超分辨率技术，可将低帧率模糊视频转换为高帧率、细节丰富的时序序列。实验结果表明，时间可作为可学习、可操控的视觉维度，为可控视频生成、时序取证及理解事件演化过程的世界模型开辟了新路径。
Temporal Taskification in Streaming Continual Learning: A Source of Evaluation Instability
Authors: Nicolae Filat, Ahmed Hussain, Konstantinos Kalogiannis, Elena Burceanu
First: 2026-04-23T17:59:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:59:54+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Streaming Continual Learning (CL) typically converts a continuous stream into a sequence of discrete tasks through temporal partitioning. We argue that this temporal taskification step is not a neutral preprocessing choice, but a structural component of evaluation: different valid splits of the same stream can induce different CL regimes and therefore different benchmark conclusions. To study this effect, we introduce a taskification-level framework based on plasticity and stability profiles, a profile distance between taskifications, and Boundary-Profile Sensitivity (BPS), which diagnoses how strongly small boundary perturbations alter the induced regime before any CL model is trained. We evaluate continual finetuning, Experience Replay, Elastic Weight Consolidation, and Learning without Forgetting on network traffic forecasting with CESNET-Timeseries24, keeping the stream, model, and training budget fixed while varying only the temporal taskification. Across 9-, 30-, and 44-day splits, we observe substantial changes in forecasting error, forgetting, and backward transfer, showing that taskification alone can materially affect CL evaluation. We further find that shorter taskifications induce noisier distribution-level patterns, larger structural distances, and higher BPS, indicating greater sensitivity to boundary perturbations. These results show that benchmark conclusions in streaming CL depend not only on the learner and the data stream, but also on how that stream is taskified, motivating temporal taskification as a first-class evaluation variable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：流式持续学习中的时间任务化：评估不稳定性的来源
流式持续学习（CL）通常通过时间划分将连续数据流转换为一系列离散任务。我们认为，这一时间任务化步骤并非中性的预处理选择，而是评估结构的一部分：同一数据流的不同有效划分可诱导不同的CL范式，从而导致不同的基准结论。为研究此效应，我们引入基于可塑性与稳定性轮廓的任务化层级框架，包括任务化间轮廓距离及边界-轮廓敏感性（BPS），用于诊断在任何CL模型训练前，微小边界扰动对诱导范式的影响程度。我们在CESNET-Timeseries24网络流量预测任务上评估持续微调、经验回放、弹性权重固化及遗忘学习，保持数据流、模型与训练预算不变，仅改变时间任务化方式。在9天、30天和44天划分下，我们观察到预测误差、遗忘与反向迁移显著变化，表明任务化本身即可实质性影响CL评估。进一步发现，较短任务化诱导分布级模式更嘈杂、结构距离更大、BPS更高，表明对边界扰动更敏感。这些结果表明，流式CL基准结论不仅取决于学习器与数据流，还取决于数据流如何被任务化，从而将时间任务化确立为首要评估变量。
Summary / 总结
The paper argues that temporal taskification in streaming continual learning is not a neutral preprocessing step but a structural component that influences evaluation outcomes, as different task splits can induce distinct learning regimes. The authors introduce a taskification-level framework using plasticity-stability profiles, profile distance metrics, and Boundary-Profile Sensitivity (BPS) to quantify how task boundaries affect learning dynamics before model training. Evaluated across four continual learning methods on CESNET-Timeseries24 with fixed stream, model, and training budget, varying only taskification, they found significant differences in forecasting error, forgetting, and backward transfer across 9-, 30-, and 44-day splits. Shorter taskifications produced noisier distribution patterns, larger structural distances, and higher BPS, indicating greater sensitivity to boundary perturbations, demonstrating that taskification must be treated as a first-class variable in streaming CL evaluation.
该论文认为，流式持续学习中的时间任务划分并非中性预处理步骤，而是影响评估结果的结构性因素。研究提出一种基于可塑性-稳定性剖面、剖面距离和边界剖面敏感性（BPS）的任务划分层级框架，用于量化不同时间划分对持续学习范式的影响。在CESNET-Timeseries24数据集上，对四种持续学习方法进行实验，保持数据流、模型和训练预算不变，仅改变时间划分，结果发现9天、30天和44天划分下预测误差、遗忘率和反向迁移显著不同。较短划分导致分布模式更嘈杂、结构距离更大、BPS更高，表明其对边界扰动更敏感，证明持续学习评估结果不仅取决于学习器和数据流，还取决于如何划分任务，应将任务划分视为评估中的核心变量。
Evaluation of Automatic Speech Recognition Using Generative Large Language Models
Authors: Thibault Bañeras-Roux, Shashi Kumar, Driss Khalil, Sergio Burdisso, Petr Motlicek, Shiran Liu, Mickael Rouvier, Jane Wottawa, Richard Dufour
First: 2026-04-23T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:59:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) is traditionally evaluated using Word Error Rate (WER), a metric that is insensitive to meaning. Embedding-based semantic metrics are better correlated with human perception, but decoder-based Large Language Models (LLMs) remain underexplored for this task. This paper evaluates their relevance through three approaches: (1) selecting the best hypothesis between two candidates, (2) computing semantic distance using generative embeddings, and (3) qualitative classification of errors. On the HATS dataset, the best LLMs achieve 92--94\% agreement with human annotators for hypothesis selection, compared to 63\% for WER, also outperforming semantic metrics. Embeddings from decoder-based LLMs show performance comparable to encoder models. Finally, LLMs offer a promising direction for interpretable and semantic ASR evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用生成式大语言模型评估自动语音识别
自动语音识别（ASR）传统上使用词错误率（WER）进行评估，该指标对语义不敏感。基于嵌入的语义指标与人类感知的相关性更高，但基于解码器的大语言模型（LLMs）在此任务中仍被较少探索。本文通过三种方法评估其相关性：（1）在两个候选结果间选择最佳假设，（2）使用生成式嵌入计算语义距离，（3）对错误进行定性分类。在HATS数据集上，最佳LLMs在假设选择上与人工标注者的一致性达92–94%，而WER仅为63%，且优于语义指标。解码器型LLMs的嵌入表现与编码器模型相当。最后，LLMs为可解释且语义化的ASR评估提供了有前景的方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional Word Error Rate in capturing semantic meaning, this study explores the use of decoder-based generative Large Language Models for ASR evaluation. It employs three methods: hypothesis selection, semantic distance computation via generative embeddings, and qualitative error classification. Experiments on the HATS dataset show that top LLMs achieve 92–94% agreement with human annotators in hypothesis selection, significantly outperforming WER’s 63%, and matching or exceeding encoder-based semantic metrics. The results suggest LLMs offer a promising, interpretable, and semantically grounded approach to ASR evaluation.
鉴于传统词错误率（WER）难以反映语义信息，本研究探索了生成式大语言模型在语音识别评估中的潜力。通过三种方法——候选句选择、基于生成嵌入的语义距离计算及错误定性分类——在HATS数据集上进行实验，结果显示顶尖LLM在句选任务中与人工标注者的一致性达92–94%，显著优于WER的63%，且性能可媲美甚至超越编码器型语义指标。研究认为LLM为语义化、可解释的语音识别评估提供了有前景的新路径。
Fine-Tuning Regimes Define Distinct Continual Learning Problems
Authors: Paul-Tiberiu Iordache, Elena Burceanu
First: 2026-04-23T17:59:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:59:34+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continual learning (CL) studies how models acquire tasks sequentially while retaining previously learned knowledge. Despite substantial progress in benchmarking CL methods, comparative evaluations typically keep the fine-tuning regime fixed. In this paper, we argue that the fine-tuning regime, defined by the trainable parameter subspace, is itself a key evaluation variable. We formalize adaptation regimes as projected optimization over fixed trainable subspaces, showing that changing the trainable depth alters the effective update signal through which both current task fitting and knowledge preservation operate. This analysis motivates the hypothesis that method comparisons need not be invariant across regimes. We test this hypothesis in task incremental CL, five trainable depth regimes, and four standard methods: online EWC, LwF, SI, and GEM. Across five benchmark datasets, namely MNIST, Fashion MNIST, KMNIST, QMNIST, and CIFAR-100, and across 11 task orders per dataset, we find that the relative ranking of methods is not consistently preserved across regimes. We further show that deeper adaptation regimes are associated with larger update magnitudes, higher forgetting, and a stronger relationship between the two. These results show that comparative conclusions in CL can depend strongly on the chosen fine-tuning regime, motivating regime-aware evaluation protocols that treat trainable depth as an explicit experimental factor.
中文标题/摘要
标题：微调范式定义了不同的持续学习问题
持续学习（CL）研究模型如何在顺序获取任务的同时保留先前学习的知识。尽管在基准测试CL方法方面取得了显著进展，但比较评估通常固定微调范式。本文我们认为，由可训练参数子空间定义的微调范式本身就是一个关键的评估变量。我们将适应范式形式化为在固定可训练子空间上的投影优化，表明改变可训练深度会通过影响当前任务拟合与知识保留所依赖的有效更新信号。这一分析促使我们提出假设：方法比较无需在不同范式间保持不变。我们在任务增量CL中测试了这一假设，使用五种可训练深度范式和四种标准方法：在线EWC、LwF、SI和GEM。在五个基准数据集（MNIST、Fashion MNIST、KMNIST、QMNIST和CIFAR-100）和每个数据集的11种任务顺序中，我们发现方法的相对排名在不同范式间并不一致保持。我们进一步表明，更深的适应范式与更大的更新幅度、更高的遗忘率以及两者更强的相关性相关。这些结果表明，CL中的比较结论可能强烈依赖于所选的微调范式，从而推动范式感知的评估协议，将可训练深度视为明确的实验因素。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that the fine-tuning regime—defined by the trainable parameter subspace—is a critical variable in continual learning evaluations, as it alters the effective update signal for both task adaptation and knowledge retention. The authors formalize adaptation regimes as projected optimization over fixed trainable depths and test their hypothesis across five depth regimes and four standard methods (online EWC, LwF, SI, GEM) on five benchmark datasets (MNIST, Fashion MNIST, KMNIST, QMNIST, CIFAR-100) with 11 task orders each. Results show that method rankings vary significantly across regimes, with deeper regimes producing larger updates, higher forgetting, and a stronger correlation between the two, indicating that comparative conclusions in continual learning are highly sensitive to the chosen fine-tuning regime.
本文指出，持续学习评估中可训练参数子空间（即微调范式）是关键变量，因为它影响任务拟合与知识保留的更新信号。作者将适应范式形式化为在固定子空间上的投影优化，并在五种可训练深度范式和四种标准方法（在线EWC、LwF、SI、GEM）上进行测试，覆盖五个基准数据集（MNIST、Fashion MNIST、KMNIST、QMNIST、CIFAR-100）及每数据集11种任务顺序。结果表明，不同范式下方法排名不一致，更深范式导致更新幅度更大、遗忘更严重且二者相关性更强，说明持续学习比较结论高度依赖所选微调范式，应将可训练深度作为显式实验因素纳入评估协议。
Seeing Without Eyes: 4D Human-Scene Understanding from Wearable IMUs
Authors: Hao-Yu Hsu, Tianhang Cheng, Jing Wen, Alexander G. Schwing, Shenlong Wang
First: 2026-04-23T17:59:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:59:16+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://tianhang-cheng.github.io/IMU4D
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Understanding human activities and their surrounding environments typically relies on visual perception, yet cameras pose persistent challenges in privacy, safety, energy efficiency, and scalability. We explore an alternative: 4D perception without vision. Its goal is to reconstruct human motion and 3D scene layouts purely from everyday wearable sensors. For this we introduce IMU-to-4D, a framework that repurposes large language models for non-visual spatiotemporal understanding of human-scene dynamics. IMU-to-4D uses data from a few inertial sensors from earbuds, watches, or smartphones and predicts detailed 4D human motion together with coarse scene structure. Experiments across diverse human-scene datasets show that IMU-to-4D yields more coherent and temporally stable results than SoTA cascaded pipelines, suggesting wearable motion sensors alone can support rich 4D understanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无眼见物：从可穿戴惯性传感器实现4D人-场景理解
理解人类活动及其周围环境通常依赖视觉感知，但摄像头在隐私、安全、能效和可扩展性方面存在持续挑战。我们探索一种替代方案：无视觉的4D感知。其目标是仅通过日常佩戴的传感器重建人体运动和3D场景布局。为此，我们提出IMU-to-4D框架，将大型语言模型重新用于非视觉时空理解人类-场景动态。IMU-to-4D利用耳塞、手表或智能手机中的少量惯性传感器数据，预测详细4D人体运动及粗略场景结构。在多个多样化的人-场景数据集上的实验表明，IMU-to-4D比当前最先进级联管道生成更连贯且时间更稳定的成果，表明仅靠可穿戴运动传感器即可支持丰富的4D理解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the privacy and practical limitations of visual sensors, this work proposes IMU-to-4D, a framework that leverages wearable inertial measurement units to reconstruct 4D human motion and coarse 3D scene structure without relying on cameras. It repurposes large language models to interpret spatiotemporal data from earbuds, watches, or smartphones, enabling detailed motion prediction and scene layout inference. Experimental results across multiple human-scene datasets demonstrate that IMU-to-4D produces more temporally stable and coherent outputs than state-of-the-art cascaded methods, proving that wearable IMUs alone can support rich 4D understanding.
为解决视觉传感器在隐私、安全、能耗和可扩展性方面的局限，该研究提出IMU-to-4D框架，仅利用耳塞、手表或手机等可穿戴惯性传感器数据，通过改造大语言模型实现对人类运动与场景结构的4D重建。实验表明，该方法在多个数据集上输出比现有最佳级联方法更稳定、更连贯，证明仅依靠可穿戴惯性传感器即可实现丰富的4D感知能力。
Long-Horizon Manipulation via Trace-Conditioned VLA Planning
Authors: Isabella Liu, An-Chieh Cheng, Rui Yan, Geng Chen, Ri-Zhao Qiu, Xueyan Zou, Sha Yi, Hongxu Yin, Xiaolong Wang, Sifei Liu
Venue: www
First: 2026-04-23T17:59:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:59:04+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://www.liuisabella.com/LoHoManip
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-horizon manipulation remains challenging for vision-language-action (VLA) policies: real tasks are multi-step, progress-dependent, and brittle to compounding execution errors. We present LoHo-Manip, a modular framework that scales short-horizon VLA execution to long-horizon instruction following via a dedicated task-management VLM. The manager is decoupled from the executor and is invoked in a receding-horizon manner: given the current observation, it predicts a progress-aware remaining plan that combines (i) a subtask sequence with an explicit done + remaining split as lightweight language memory, and (ii) a visual trace -- a compact 2D keypoint trajectory prompt specifying where to go and what to approach next. The executor VLA is adapted to condition on the rendered trace, thereby turning long-horizon decision-making into repeated local control by following the trace. Crucially, predicting the remaining plan at each step yields an implicit closed loop: failed steps persist in subsequent outputs, and traces update accordingly, enabling automatic continuation and replanning without hand-crafted recovery logic or brittle visual-history buffers. Extensive experiments spanning embodied planning, long-horizon reasoning, trajectory prediction, and end-to-end manipulation in simulation and on a real Franka robot demonstrate strong gains in long-horizon success, robustness, and out-of-distribution generalization. Project page: https://www.liuisabella.com/LoHoManip
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过轨迹条件化的VLA规划实现长时程操作
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在长时程操作中仍具挑战性：真实任务多步骤、进度依赖且易受累积执行错误影响。我们提出LoHo-Manip，一种模块化框架，通过专用任务管理视觉语言模型（VLM），将短时程VLA执行扩展至长时程指令遵循。管理者与执行器解耦，以滚动时程方式调用：给定当前观测，预测包含（i）带明确完成与剩余划分的子任务序列（轻量语言记忆）及（ii）视觉轨迹——紧凑的2D关键点轨迹提示（指示下一步去向与目标）的剩余计划。执行器VLA被适配以条件化于渲染轨迹，从而将长时程决策转化为沿轨迹重复局部控制。关键在于，每步预测剩余计划隐式形成闭环：失败步骤持续出现在后续输出中，轨迹相应更新，无需手工恢复逻辑或脆弱的视觉历史缓冲区即可实现自动延续与重规划。在模拟与真实Franka机器人上，涵盖具身规划、长时程推理、轨迹预测及端到端操作的广泛实验表明，该方法在长时程成功率、鲁棒性及分布外泛化能力方面显著提升。项目页面：https://www.liuisabella.com/LoHoManip
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of long-horizon manipulation in vision-language-action systems due to multi-step, progress-dependent tasks and error compounding, the paper introduces LoHo-Manip, a modular framework that extends short-horizon VLA execution to long horizons using a task-management VLM. The manager, decoupled from the executor, operates in a receding-horizon fashion, predicting a progress-aware plan that includes a subtask sequence with explicit done/remaining splits and a visual trace—a compact 2D keypoint trajectory guiding next actions. The executor conditions on this trace, enabling local control at each step, while the implicit closed-loop nature of plan prediction allows automatic continuation and replanning without recovery logic or visual history buffers. Experiments across simulation and real robot manipulation show significant improvements in long-horizon success rates, robustness, and out-of-distribution generalization.
为解决视觉-语言-动作系统在长时程操作中因错误累积和缺乏进度感知而导致的脆弱性问题，LoHo-Manip 提出一种模块化框架，将任务管理 VLM 与执行器解耦。管理器以递归方式在每一步生成包含子任务序列和视觉轨迹（紧凑的2D关键点轨迹）的进度感知计划，执行器据此条件化执行局部控制。该设计形成隐式闭环：失败步骤会持续影响后续计划，轨迹自动更新，无需手动恢复逻辑或脆弱的视觉历史缓冲。实验在仿真与真实 Franka 机器人上验证，显著提升长时程成功率、鲁棒性及分布外泛化能力。
Cyber Defense Benchmark: Agentic Threat Hunting Evaluation for LLMs in SecOps
Authors: Alankrit Chona, Igor Kozlov, Ambuj Kumar
First: 2026-04-21T14:53:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:59:02+00:00
Comments: Updated leaderboard with newer models
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the Cyber Defense Benchmark, a benchmark for measuring how well large language model (LLM) agents perform the core SOC analyst task of threat hunting: given a database of raw Windows event logs with no guided questions or hints, identify the exact timestamps of malicious events. The benchmark wraps 106 real attack procedures from the OTRF Security-Datasets corpus - spanning 86 MITRE ATT&CK sub-techniques across 12 tactics - into a Gymnasium reinforcement-learning environment. Each episode presents the agent with an in-memory SQLite database of 75,000-135,000 log records produced by a deterministic campaign simulator that time-shifts and entity-obfuscates the raw recordings. The agent must iteratively submit SQL queries to discover malicious event timestamps and explicitly flag them, scored CTF-style against Sigma-rule-derived ground truth. Evaluating five frontier models - Claude Opus 4.6, GPT-5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Kimi K2.5, and Gemini 3 Flash - on 26 campaigns covering 105 of 106 procedures, we find that all models fail dramatically: the best model (Claude Opus 4.6) submits correct flags for only 3.8% of malicious events on average, and no run across any model ever finds all flags. We define a passing score as >= 50% recall on every ATT&CK tactic - the minimum bar for unsupervised SOC deployment. No model passes: the leader clears this bar on 5 of 13 tactics and the remaining four on zero. These results suggest that current LLMs are poorly suited for open-ended, evidence-driven threat hunting despite strong performance on curated Q&A security benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate large language models (LLMs) in real-world security operations, this study introduces the Cyber Defense Benchmark, a reinforcement learning environment simulating threat hunting using 106 real attack procedures across 86 MITRE ATT&CK sub-techniques. 为评估大语言模型在无监督威胁狩猎中的实际能力，本研究提出了Cyber Defense Benchmark，基于OTRF数据集中的106个真实攻击流程构建强化学习环境。模型需通过SQL查询从时间偏移、实体混淆的Windows日志数据库中识别恶意事件时间戳并标记，评分依据Sigma规则生成的基准答案。在26个攻击场景中测试五款前沿模型，结果显示所有模型表现不佳：表现最好的Claude Opus 4.6平均仅正确标记3.8%的恶意事件，且无模型能完全覆盖所有事件。即便表现最佳的模型也仅在13个ATT&CK战术中的5个达到50%召回率门槛，表明当前大语言模型在开放性、证据驱动的SOC任务中能力不足，尽管在定制化安全问答基准上表现优异。
The Sample Complexity of Multicalibration
Authors: Natalie Collina, Jiuyao Lu, Georgy Noarov, Aaron Roth
First: 2026-04-23T17:59:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:59:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the minimax sample complexity of multicalibration in the batch setting. A learner observes $n$ i.i.d. samples from an unknown distribution and must output a (possibly randomized) predictor whose population multicalibration error, measured by Expected Calibration Error (ECE), is at most $\varepsilon$ with respect to a given family of groups. For every fixed $κ> 0$, in the regime $|G|\le \varepsilon^{-κ}$, we prove that $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-3})$ samples are necessary and sufficient, up to polylogarithmic factors. The lower bound holds even for randomized predictors, and the upper bound is realized by a randomized predictor obtained via an online-to-batch reduction. This separates the sample complexity of multicalibration from that of marginal calibration, which scales as $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$, and shows that mean-ECE multicalibration is as difficult in the batch setting as it is in the online setting, in contrast to marginal calibration which is strictly more difficult in the online setting. In contrast we observe that for $κ= 0$, the sample complexity of multicalibration remains $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ exhibiting a sharp threshold phenomenon. More generally, we establish matching upper and lower bounds, up to polylogarithmic factors, for a weighted $L_p$ multicalibration metric for all $1 \le p \le 2$, with optimal exponent $3/p$. We also extend the lower-bound template to a regular class of elicitable properties, and combine it with the online upper bounds of Hu et al. (2025) to obtain matching bounds for calibrating properties including expectiles and bounded-density quantiles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多校准的样本复杂度
我们研究了批量设置下多校准的最小最大样本复杂度。学习器观察来自未知分布的n个独立同分布样本，并必须输出一个（可能随机化的）预测器，其相对于给定群体族的总体多校准误差（以期望校准误差ECE衡量）不超过ε。对于每个固定的κ>0，在|G|≤ε^{-κ}的范围内，我们证明了至多polylogarithmic因子，需要且足够样本数量为\widetildeΘ(ε^{-3})。下界即使对于随机预测器也成立，上界由在线到批量的转换得到的随机预测器实现。这将多校准的样本复杂度与边际校准（其复杂度为\widetildeΘ(ε^{-2})）区分开来，并表明在批量设置下，均值ECE多校准与在线设置下的难度相同，而边际校准在在线设置下则更难。相比之下，当κ=0时，多校准的样本复杂度仍为\widetildeΘ(ε^{-2})，表现出尖锐阈值现象。更一般地，我们为所有1≤p≤2的加权L_p多校准度量建立了匹配的上下界（至多polylogarithmic因子），最优指数为3/p。我们还将下界模板扩展到一类可提取属性的正则类，并结合Hu等人（2025）的在线上界，得到包括期望分位数和有界密度分位数等校准属性的匹配界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding the fundamental sample requirements for multicalibration in batch learning, this work analyzes the minimax sample complexity for achieving a given Expected Calibration Error (ECE) across a family of groups. For fixed κ > 0 and group size bounded by ε^(-κ), the study proves that Θ(ε^(-3)) samples are both necessary and sufficient, up to polylogarithmic factors, using a randomized predictor derived via online-to-batch reduction. This contrasts with marginal calibration’s ε^(-2) complexity and demonstrates that multicalibration’s difficulty matches the online setting, revealing a sharp threshold at κ=0 where complexity reverts to ε^(-2). The analysis further extends to weighted L_p multicalibration for 1 ≤ p ≤ 2, yielding optimal exponents 3/p, and generalizes lower-bound techniques to other elicitable properties like expectiles and bounded-density quantiles, achieving matching upper and lower bounds via online methods.
为理解批量学习中多校准的样本复杂度下限，本文研究在给定组族下实现期望校准误差（ECE）的最小最大样本需求。当组数满足 |G|≤ε^{-κ}（κ>0）时，证明 Θ(ε^{-3}) 样本为必要且充分（至多对数因子），通过在线到批量转换构造的随机预测器实现。这与边际校准的 ε^{-2} 复杂度形成对比，表明多校准在批量设置中更难，且与在线设置难度相当。当 κ=0 时出现尖锐阈值，复杂度回落至 ε^{-2}。研究进一步推广至加权 L_p 多校准度量（1≤p≤2），指数为 3/p，并将下界模板扩展至其他可激励属性，结合 Hu 等人（2025）的在线上界，获得包括期望分位数和有界密度分位数等属性的匹配界限。
Parallel-SFT: Improving Zero-Shot Cross-Programming-Language Transfer for Code RL
Authors: Zhaofeng Wu, Shiqi Wang, Boya Peng, Anuj Goyal, Melanie Kambadur, Sebastian Ruder, Yoon Kim, Chloe Bi
First: 2026-04-22T17:58:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:58:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern language models demonstrate impressive coding capabilities in common programming languages (PLs), such as C++ and Python, but their performance in lower-resource PLs is often limited by training data availability. In principle, however, most programming skills are universal across PLs, so the capability acquired in one PL should transfer to others. In this work, we propose the task of zero-shot cross-programming-language transfer for code RL. We find that, for Llama-3.1, RL training for code generation in a source PL fails to improve, and sometimes even degrades, the performance on other target PLs. To address this, we hypothesize that effective RL transfer requires a generalizable SFT initialization before RL. We thus propose **Parallel-SFT**, an SFT strategy that incorporates "parallel programs" -- functionally equivalent code implemented in multiple PLs -- into the data mixture. We demonstrate that this improves transferability: when we subsequently perform RL on our Parallel-SFT model, we observe better generalization to unseen PLs. Analysis of the model internal representations reveals that Parallel-SFT leads to a more functionality-centric latent space, where equivalent programs across PLs are more tightly clustered, which we hypothesize to contribute to the improved transferability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Parallel-SFT：提升代码强化学习中的零样本跨编程语言迁移能力
现代语言模型在常见编程语言（如C++和Python）中展现出卓越的编码能力，但在资源较少的编程语言中的表现常受限于训练数据的可用性。然而，从原则上讲，大多数编程技能在不同编程语言间具有通用性，因此在一种语言中习得的能力应能迁移到其他语言。本文提出零样本跨编程语言迁移任务用于代码强化学习。我们发现，对于Llama-3.1模型，针对源语言的代码生成强化学习训练不仅未能提升其他目标语言的表现，有时甚至会使其下降。为解决此问题，我们假设有效的强化学习迁移需要在强化学习前进行可迁移的监督微调（SFT）初始化。因此，我们提出**Parallel-SFT**，一种将“并行程序”——即在多种编程语言中实现功能等价的代码——纳入数据混合的SFT策略。实验证明，该策略提升了迁移能力：在Parallel-SFT模型上进行后续强化学习后，模型对未见过的编程语言展现出更好的泛化能力。对模型内部表征的分析表明，Parallel-SFT构建了更以功能为中心的潜在空间，其中不同编程语言中的等价程序更紧密地聚类，我们推测这有助于提升迁移能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that language models trained on common programming languages struggle to generalize to low-resource languages despite shared coding principles, this work introduces Parallel-SFT, a supervised fine-tuning strategy that mixes functionally equivalent code across multiple programming languages. The method trains models on parallel program pairs to establish a more generalizable latent representation before reinforcement learning. Experimental results show that this initialization significantly improves zero-shot transfer performance to unseen programming languages, with analysis indicating that the model’s internal representations become more focused on functional equivalence, enhancing cross-language generalization.
鉴于语言模型在主流编程语言上训练良好但在资源较少的语言上表现不佳，尽管编程能力在语言间具有通用性，本文研究了代码强化学习中的零样本跨语言迁移问题。作者发现，直接在源语言上进行强化学习训练反而会损害目标语言的表现，表明需要更好的初始化策略。为此，他们提出了Parallel-SFT方法，通过在训练数据中混合多语言功能等价代码进行监督微调。实验结果表明，该方法显著提升了跨语言泛化能力，强化学习在Parallel-SFT模型上训练后在未见语言上表现更优。内部表示分析显示，Parallel-SFT构建了更以功能为中心的潜在空间，使不同语言中的等价程序更紧密聚类，这可能促进了迁移效果。
Context Unrolling in Omni Models
Authors: Ceyuan Yang, Zhijie Lin, Yang Zhao, Fei Xiao, Hao He, Qi Zhao, Chaorui Deng, Kunchang Li, Zihan Ding, Yuwei Guo, Fuyun Wang, Fangqi Zhu, Xiaonan Nie, Shenhan Zhu, Shanchuan Lin, Hongsheng Li, Weilin Huang, Guang Shi, Haoqi Fan
First: 2026-04-23T17:58:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:58:38+00:00
Comments: Report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Omni, a unified multimodal model natively trained on diverse modalities, including text, images, videos, 3D geometry, and hidden representations. We find that such training enables Context Unrolling, where the model explicitly reasons across multiple modal representations before producing predictions. This process enables the model to aggregate complementary information across heterogeneous modalities, facilitating a more faithful approximation of the shared multimodal knowledge manifold and improving downstream reasoning fidelity. As a result, Omni achieves strong performance on both multimodal generation and understanding benchmarks, while demonstrating advanced multimodal reasoning capabilities, including in-context generation of text, image, video, and 3D geometry.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Omni模型中的上下文展开
我们提出了Omni，一个原生训练于多种模态（包括文本、图像、视频、3D几何和隐藏表示）的统一多模态模型。我们发现，这种训练使模型能够进行上下文展开，即在生成预测前显式地跨多个模态表示进行推理。该过程使模型能够聚合异构模态间的互补信息，更准确地逼近共享的多模态知识流形，从而提升下游推理的准确性。因此，Omni在多模态生成与理解基准测试中表现优异，并展现出先进的多模态推理能力，包括上下文内生成文本、图像、视频和3D几何。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better capture shared knowledge across diverse modalities, the authors introduce Omni, a unified multimodal model trained natively on text, images, videos, 3D geometry, and hidden representations. The model leverages a mechanism called Context Unrolling, which allows it to explicitly reason across multiple modalities before generating outputs, thereby aggregating complementary information. Experimental results show that this approach enables Omni to achieve strong performance on multimodal generation and understanding benchmarks, and it excels in in-context reasoning tasks involving text, images, videos, and 3D geometry.
为更好地捕捉跨模态共享知识，作者提出了Omni，一个原生支持文本、图像、视频、3D几何和隐藏表示的统一多模态模型。该模型利用“上下文展开”机制，在生成输出前显式跨模态推理，从而聚合互补信息，更准确地逼近底层多模态知识流形。实验表明，Omni在多模态生成与理解基准上表现优异，并展现出跨模态的高级上下文推理能力。
Speculative Actions: A Lossless Framework for Faster Agentic Systems
Authors: Naimeng Ye, Arnav Ahuja, Georgios Liargkovas, Yunan Lu, Kostis Kaffes, Tianyi Peng
First: 2025-10-05T21:28:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:58:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents are increasingly deployed in complex, interactive environments, yet their runtime remains a major bottleneck for training, evaluation, and real-world use. Typical agent behavior unfolds sequentially, with each action requiring an API call that can incur substantial latency. For example, a game of chess between two state-of-the-art agents can take hours. We introduce Speculative Actions, a lossless acceleration framework for general agentic systems. Inspired by speculative execution in microprocessors and speculative decoding in LLM inference, our method uses faster models to predict likely future actions and execute them in parallel, committing only when predictions match. We evaluate speculative actions across gaming, e-commerce, and web search environments, and additionally study a lossy extension in an operating systems setting. Across domains, we achieve up to 55% next-action prediction accuracy, translating into up to 20% latency reductions. Finally, we present a cost-latency analysis that formalizes the tradeoff between speculative breadth and time savings. This analysis enables principled tuning and selective branch launching to ensure that multi-branch speculation delivers practical speedups without prohibitive cost growth.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推测性动作：一种无损的加速框架，用于更快的代理系统
AI代理正越来越多地部署在复杂、交互式环境中，但其运行时仍是训练、评估和实际应用的主要瓶颈。典型的代理行为按顺序展开，每个动作都需要一次API调用，可能产生显著延迟。例如，两个顶尖代理之间的国际象棋对弈可能耗时数小时。我们提出了推测性动作，一种适用于通用代理系统的无损加速框架。该方法受微处理器中的推测执行和大语言模型推理中的推测解码启发，利用更快的模型预测可能的未来动作，并并行执行，仅在预测匹配时才提交。我们在游戏、电子商务和网络搜索环境中评估推测性动作，并在操作系统环境中研究其有损扩展版本。在不同领域中，我们实现了高达55%的下一步动作预测准确率，转化为最高20%的延迟降低。最后，我们提出了一种成本-延迟分析，形式化了推测广度与时间节省之间的权衡，从而支持原则性的调优和选择性分支启动，确保多分支推测在不导致成本激增的情况下实现实用的加速。
Summary / 总结
To address the high runtime costs of AI agents in interactive environments, the paper proposes Speculative Actions, a lossless framework that leverages faster models to predict and execute likely future actions in parallel, committing only when predictions match. Evaluated across gaming, e-commerce, and web search domains, the method achieves up to 55% next-action prediction accuracy, yielding up to 20% latency reduction. A formal cost-latency analysis further enables principled tuning to balance speculative breadth and performance gains without excessive computational overhead.
为解决AI代理系统运行时延迟高的问题，本文提出Speculative Actions框架，通过使用更快模型并行预测和执行可能的未来动作，在预测匹配时才提交执行，从而实现无损加速。在游戏、电商和网络搜索等多个领域测试中，该方法达到最高55%的动作预测准确率，带来最高20%的延迟降低。同时，通过形式化的成本-延迟分析，可指导合理调整并行分支数量，在保证实际加速效果的同时避免计算开销激增。
MathDuels: Evaluating LLMs as Problem Posers and Solvers
Authors: Zhiqiu Xu, Shibo Jin, Shreya Arya, Mayur Naik
First: 2026-04-23T17:57:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:57:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As frontier language models attain near-ceiling performance on static mathematical benchmarks, existing evaluations are increasingly unable to differentiate model capabilities, largely because they cast models solely as solvers of fixed problem sets. We introduce MathDuels, a self-play benchmark in which models occupy dual roles: each authors math problems under adversarial prompting and solves problems authored by every other participant. Problems are produced through a three-stage generation pipeline (meta-prompting, problem generation, and difficulty amplification), and validated by an independent verifier that excludes ill-posed questions. A Rasch model (Rasch, 1993) jointly estimates solver abilities and problem difficulties; author quality is derived from the difficulties of each model's authored problems. Experiments across 19 frontier models reveal that authoring and solving capabilities are partially decoupled, and that dual-role evaluation reveals capability separations invisible in single-role benchmarks. As newer models enter the arena, they produce problems that defeat previously dominant solvers, so the benchmark's difficulty co-evolves with participant strength rather than saturating at a fixed ceiling. We host a public leaderboard that updates as new models are released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MathDuels：评估大语言模型作为问题提出者与解题者的性能
随着前沿语言模型在静态数学基准测试中接近性能上限，现有评估方法越来越难以区分模型能力，主要因为它们仅将模型视为固定问题集的解题者。我们引入MathDuels，一个自对弈基准测试，其中模型同时扮演双重角色：每个模型在对抗性提示下自动生成数学问题，并解决其他参与者所生成的问题。问题通过三阶段生成管道（元提示、问题生成、难度增强）产生，并由独立验证者排除不恰当的问题。采用Rasch模型（Rasch, 1993）联合估计解题者能力与问题难度；作者质量由其生成问题的难度推导得出。在19个前沿模型上的实验表明，问题生成与解题能力部分解耦，双重角色评估揭示了单角色基准中不可见的能力差异。随着新模型进入竞争，它们生成的问题能击败此前主导的解题者，因此该基准的难度随参与者实力共同演化，而非固定于某一上限。我们提供一个公开排行榜，随新模型发布实时更新。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the saturation of static math benchmarks in evaluating frontier language models, this work introduces MathDuels, a self-play benchmark where models simultaneously act as problem authors and solvers, competing adversarially. Problems are generated through a three-stage pipeline and validated by an independent verifier, while a Rasch model estimates both solver ability and problem difficulty. Experiments across 19 models show that authoring and solving skills are partially decoupled, and dual-role evaluation uncovers capability gaps invisible in single-role tests. As newer models enter, they generate problems that outperform prior solvers, causing the benchmark’s difficulty to co-evolve with model strength rather than plateau.
鉴于现有静态数学基准在评估前沿大模型时已趋于饱和，作者提出MathDuels自对弈基准，使模型同时扮演问题作者与解题者进行对抗。问题通过三阶段生成流程产生，并由独立验证者筛选，Rasch模型联合估计解题能力与题目难度，作者质量由其生成题目的难度推导。在19个模型的实验中发现，出题与解题能力部分分离，双角色评估揭示了单角色基准无法发现的能力差异。随着新模型加入，其生成题目可击败先前顶尖解题者，使基准难度随模型实力动态演化而非趋于固定上限。
Vista4D: Video Reshooting with 4D Point Clouds
Authors: Kuan Heng Lin, Zhizheng Liu, Pablo Salamanca, Yash Kant, Ryan Burgert, Yuancheng Xu, Koichi Namekata, Yiwei Zhao, Bolei Zhou, Micah Goldblum, Paul Debevec, Ning Yu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-23T17:57:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:57:28+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 20 figures, CVPR 2026, see project page at https://eyeline-labs.github.io/Vista4D
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present Vista4D, a robust and flexible video reshooting framework that grounds the input video and target cameras in a 4D point cloud. Specifically, given an input video, our method re-synthesizes the scene with the same dynamics from a different camera trajectory and viewpoint. Existing video reshooting methods often struggle with depth estimation artifacts of real-world dynamic videos, while also failing to preserve content appearance and failing to maintain precise camera control for challenging new trajectories. We build a 4D-grounded point cloud representation with static pixel segmentation and 4D reconstruction to explicitly preserve seen content and provide rich camera signals, and we train with reconstructed multiview dynamic data for robustness against point cloud artifacts during real-world inference. Our results demonstrate improved 4D consistency, camera control, and visual quality compared to state-of-the-art baselines under a variety of videos and camera paths. Moreover, our method generalizes to real-world applications such as dynamic scene expansion and 4D scene recomposition. See our project page for results, code, and models: https://eyeline-labs.github.io/Vista4D
中文标题/摘要
标题：Vista4D：基于4D点云的视频重拍
我们提出了Vista4D，一种稳健且灵活的视频重拍框架，将输入视频和目标相机锚定在4D点云中。具体而言，给定输入视频，我们的方法从不同的相机轨迹和视角重新合成具有相同动态的场景。现有视频重拍方法常因现实世界动态视频的深度估计伪影而表现不佳，且难以保持内容外观并精确控制相机，尤其在应对复杂的新轨迹时。我们构建了基于静态像素分割和4D重建的4D锚定点云表示，以显式保留可见内容并提供丰富的相机信号，并通过重建的多视角动态数据进行训练，以增强在现实世界推理中对点云伪影的鲁棒性。实验结果表明，与现有最先进方法相比，我们的方法在多种视频和相机路径下实现了更优的4D一致性、相机控制能力和视觉质量。此外，我们的方法可推广至现实应用，如动态场景扩展和4D场景重组。详见项目页面获取结果、代码和模型：https://eyeline-labs.github.io/Vista4D
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing video reshooting methods in handling dynamic scenes and preserving visual fidelity under new camera trajectories, Vista4D introduces a 4D point cloud framework that grounds both input video and target camera paths within a unified 4D representation. The method first reconstructs a 4D point cloud using static pixel segmentation and dynamic reconstruction to preserve scene content and provide precise camera signals, then trains on multiview dynamic data to enhance robustness against real-world artifacts. Experimental results show significant improvements in 4D consistency, camera control accuracy, and visual quality over state-of-the-art methods across diverse video sequences and camera paths, while also demonstrating generalization to real-world applications like dynamic scene expansion and 4D recomposition.
为解决现有视频重拍方法在动态场景中深度估计误差大、内容失真及相机控制不精确的问题，Vista4D提出一种基于4D点云的框架，将输入视频与目标相机轨迹统一嵌入时空表示中。该方法通过静态像素分割与4D重建显式保留场景内容并提供丰富相机信号，结合多视角动态数据训练以增强真实场景推理中的鲁棒性。实验表明，该方法在4D一致性、相机轨迹控制与视觉质量上显著优于现有方法，并可泛化至动态场景扩展与4D重组等实际应用。
VistaBot: View-Robust Robot Manipulation via Spatiotemporal-Aware View Synthesis
Authors: Songen Gu, Yuhang Zheng, Weize Li, Yupeng Zheng, Yating Feng, Xiang Li, Yilun Chen, Pengfei Li, Wenchao Ding
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2026-04-23T17:57:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:57:13+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted to ICRA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recently, end-to-end robotic manipulation models have gained significant attention for their generalizability and scalability. However, they often suffer from limited robustness to camera viewpoint changes when training with a fixed camera. In this paper, we propose VistaBot, a novel framework that integrates feed-forward geometric models with video diffusion models to achieve view-robust closed-loop manipulation without requiring camera calibration at test time. Our approach consists of three key components: 4D geometry estimation, view synthesis latent extraction, and latent action learning. VistaBot is integrated into both action-chunking (ACT) and diffusion-based ($π_0$) policies and evaluated across simulation and real-world tasks. We further introduce the View Generalization Score (VGS) as a new metric for comprehensive evaluation of cross-view generalization. Results show that VistaBot improves VGS by 2.79$\times$ and 2.63$\times$ over ACT and $π_0$, respectively, while also achieving high-quality novel view synthesis. Our contributions include a geometry-aware synthesis model, a latent action planner, a new benchmark metric, and extensive validation across diverse environments. The code and models will be made publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VistaBot：通过时空感知的视图合成实现视角鲁棒的机器人操作
近年来，端到端机器人操作模型因其泛化能力和可扩展性受到广泛关注。然而，当使用固定摄像头训练时，这些模型往往对摄像头视角变化的鲁棒性有限。本文提出VistaBot，一种新颖框架，将前馈几何模型与视频扩散模型相结合，在无需测试时摄像头标定的情况下实现视角鲁棒的闭环操作。我们的方法包含三个关键组件：4D几何估计、视图合成潜在特征提取和潜在动作学习。VistaBot被集成到动作分块（ACT）和基于扩散的（π₀）策略中，并在仿真与真实世界任务中进行评估。我们进一步引入视图泛化得分（VGS）作为评估跨视角泛化的全新指标。结果表明，VistaBot在ACT和π₀上分别将VGS提升2.79倍和2.63倍，同时实现高质量的新型视图合成。我们的贡献包括几何感知合成模型、潜在动作规划器、新基准指标以及在多样化环境中的广泛验证。代码和模型将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited robustness of end-to-end robotic manipulation models to camera viewpoint changes, this paper introduces VistaBot, a framework that combines feed-forward geometric models with video diffusion models to enable view-robust manipulation without test-time camera calibration. The method comprises three components: 4D geometry estimation, view synthesis latent extraction, and latent action learning, integrated into both action-chunking and diffusion-based policies. Experimental results across simulation and real-world tasks demonstrate that VistaBot significantly improves the View Generalization Score (VGS) by 2.79x and 2.63x over baseline methods, while producing high-quality novel view synthesis.
为解决端到端机器人操作模型在相机视角变化时泛化能力差的问题，本文提出VistaBot框架，结合前馈几何模型与视频扩散模型，在无需测试时标定的情况下实现视角鲁棒的操作。该方法包含三个核心模块：4D几何估计、视角合成潜在特征提取与潜在动作学习，并集成于动作分块与扩散策略中。实验表明，VistaBot在仿真与真实任务中显著提升跨视角泛化能力，相较基线策略分别提升2.79倍和2.63倍的视角泛化得分，同时生成高质量的新视角合成。
ATOM: A Pretrained Neural Operator for Multitask Molecular Dynamics
Authors: Luke Thompson, Davy Guan, Dai Shi, Slade Matthews, Junbin Gao, Andi Han
Venue: iclr poster
First: 2025-10-07T00:56:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:56:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICLR2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Molecular dynamics (MD) simulations underpin modern computational drug discovery, materials science, and biochemistry. Recent machine learning models provide high-fidelity MD predictions without the need to repeatedly solve quantum mechanical forces, enabling significant speedups over conventional pipelines. Yet many such methods typically enforce strict equivariance and rely on sequential rollouts, thus limiting their flexibility and simulation efficiency. They are also commonly single-task, trained on individual molecules and fixed timeframes, which restricts generalization to unseen compounds and extended timesteps. To address these issues, we propose Atomistic Transformer Operator for Molecules (ATOM), a pretrained transformer neural operator for multitask molecular dynamics. ATOM adopts a quasi-equivariant design that requires no explicit molecular graph and employs a temporal attention mechanism, allowing for the accurate parallel decoding of multiple future states. To support operator pretraining across chemicals and timescales, we curate TG80, a large, diverse, and numerically stable MD dataset with over 2.5 million femtoseconds of trajectories across 80 compounds. ATOM achieves state-of-the-art performance on established single-task benchmarks, such as MD17, RMD17 and MD22. After multitask pretraining on TG80, ATOM shows exceptional zero-shot generalization to unseen molecules across varying time horizons. We believe ATOM represents a significant step toward accurate, efficient, and transferable molecular dynamics models.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible, efficient, and generalizable molecular dynamics models that overcome limitations of existing single-task, sequential, and rigidly equivariant methods, ATOM introduces a pretrained transformer neural operator designed for multitask simulation. It employs a quasi-equivariant architecture without explicit molecular graphs and uses temporal attention to enable parallel prediction of multiple future states. Pretrained on TG80—a large, diverse dataset of over 2.5 million femtoseconds across 80 compounds—ATOM achieves state-of-the-art results on standard benchmarks like MD17 and MD22, and demonstrates strong zero-shot generalization to unseen molecules across varying time horizons.
为解决现有分子动力学模型在灵活性、效率和泛化能力方面的局限，ATOM提出一种面向多任务模拟的预训练Transformer神经算子。其采用准等变架构，无需显式分子图，并通过时间注意力机制实现多个未来状态的并行解码，提升模拟速度与灵活性。在包含80种化合物、250万飞秒轨迹的TG80数据集上预训练后，ATOM在MD17、MD22等单任务基准上达到最先进性能，并在未见分子和不同时间尺度上展现出卓越的零样本泛化能力，体现其可迁移性与高效性。
When Prompts Override Vision: Prompt-Induced Hallucinations in LVLMs
Authors: Pegah Khayatan, Jayneel Parekh, Arnaud Dapogny, Mustafa Shukor, Alasdair Newson, Matthieu Cord
First: 2026-04-23T17:54:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:54:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Despite impressive progress in capabilities of large vision-language models (LVLMs), these systems remain vulnerable to hallucinations, i.e., outputs that are not grounded in the visual input. Prior work has attributed hallucinations in LVLMs to factors such as limitations of the vision backbone or the dominance of the language component, yet the relative importance of these factors remains unclear. To resolve this ambiguity, We propose HalluScope, a benchmark to better understand the extent to which different factors induce hallucinations. Our analysis indicates that hallucinations largely stem from excessive reliance on textual priors and background knowledge, especially information introduced through textual instructions. To mitigate hallucinations induced by textual instruction priors, we propose HalluVL-DPO, a framework for fine-tuning off-the-shelf LVLMs towards more visually grounded responses. HalluVL-DPO leverages preference optimization using a curated training dataset that we construct, guiding the model to prefer grounded responses over hallucinated ones. We demonstrate that our optimized model effectively mitigates the targeted hallucination failure mode, while preserving or improving performance on other hallucination benchmarks and visual capability evaluations. To support reproducibility and further research, we will publicly release our evaluation benchmark, preference training dataset, and code at https://pegah-kh.github.io/projects/prompts-override-vision/ .
中文标题/摘要
标题：当提示覆盖视觉时：LVLM中的提示诱导幻觉
尽管大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）的能力取得了显著进展，但这些系统仍易受幻觉影响，即输出未基于视觉输入。先前研究将LVLMs中的幻觉归因于视觉骨干网络的局限性或语言组件的主导作用，但这些因素的相对重要性尚不明确。为解决这一模糊性，我们提出了HalluScope基准，以更好地理解不同因素引发幻觉的程度。我们的分析表明，幻觉主要源于对文本先验和背景知识的过度依赖，尤其是通过文本指令引入的信息。为缓解由文本指令先验引发的幻觉，我们提出了HalluVL-DPO框架，用于对现成LVLMs进行微调，使其生成更视觉接地的响应。HalluVL-DPO利用我们构建的精选训练数据集进行偏好优化，引导模型优先选择接地响应而非幻觉响应。我们证明，优化后的模型能有效缓解目标幻觉失效模式，同时在其他幻觉基准和视觉能力评估中保持或提升性能。为支持可复现性及进一步研究，我们将在https://pegah-kh.github.io/projects/prompts-override-vision/公开发布评估基准、偏好训练数据集和代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent hallucination problem in large vision-language models (LVLMs), this work investigates whether textual prompts or visual grounding drives these errors. It introduces HalluScope, a benchmark to quantify the contribution of textual priors versus visual input, revealing that hallucinations primarily arise from overreliance on textual instructions. To address this, the authors propose HalluVL-DPO, a preference-based fine-tuning method that trains LVLMs to favor grounded responses over hallucinated ones using a curated dataset. Experiments show that HalluVL-DPO significantly reduces prompt-induced hallucinations while maintaining or enhancing performance on other visual and hallucination benchmarks.
针对大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）中持续存在的幻觉问题，该研究探讨了文本提示与视觉输入在引发幻觉中的相对作用。作者提出了HalluScope评估基准，旨在量化文本先验与视觉接地对幻觉的贡献。分析表明，幻觉主要源于对文本指令和背景知识的过度依赖。为此，他们提出HalluVL-DPO方法，通过偏好优化对现成LVLM进行微调，引导模型优先生成视觉接地的输出。实验表明，该方法显著降低了由文本提示引发的幻觉，同时在其他视觉能力和幻觉基准测试中保持或提升了性能。
From Research Question to Scientific Workflow: Leveraging Agentic AI for Science Automation
Authors: Bartosz Balis, Michal Orzechowski, Piotr Kica, Michal Dygas, Michal Kuszewski
First: 2026-04-23T17:52:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:52:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific workflow systems automate execution -- scheduling, fault tolerance, resource management -- but not the semantic translation that precedes it. Scientists still manually convert research questions into workflow specifications, a task requiring both domain knowledge and infrastructure expertise. We propose an agentic architecture that closes this gap through three layers: an LLM interprets natural language into structured intents (semantic layer); validated generators produce reproducible workflow DAGs (deterministic layer); and domain experts author ``Skills'': markdown documents encoding vocabulary mappings, parameter constraints, and optimization strategies (knowledge layer). This decomposition confines LLM non-determinism to intent extraction: identical intents always yield identical workflows. We implement and evaluate the architecture on the 1000 Genomes population genetics workflow and Hyperflow WMS running on Kubernetes. In an ablation study on 150 queries, Skills raise full-match intent accuracy from 44% to 83%; skill-driven deferred workflow generation reduces data transfer by 92\%; and the end-to-end pipeline completes queries on Kubernetes with LLM overhead below 15 seconds and cost under $0.001 per query.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从研究问题到科学工作流：利用代理式人工智能实现科学自动化
科学工作流系统可自动化执行——包括调度、容错和资源管理——但无法自动完成其前置的语义转换。科学家仍需手动将研究问题转化为工作流规范，这需要领域知识与基础设施专长。我们提出一种代理式架构，通过三层结构弥合此差距：大语言模型（LLM）将自然语言转化为结构化意图（语义层）；经验证的生成器产生可复现的工作流DAG（确定性层）；领域专家编写“技能”：以Markdown文档形式编码词汇映射、参数约束与优化策略（知识层）。该分解将LLM的非确定性限制在意图提取阶段：相同意图始终生成相同工作流。我们在1000 Genomes群体遗传学工作流及运行于Kubernetes的Hyperflow WMS上实现并评估该架构。在150个查询的消融实验中，技能使完整匹配意图准确率从44%提升至83%；技能驱动的延迟工作流生成减少数据传输92%；端到端管道在Kubernetes上完成查询，LLM开销低于15秒，每查询成本低于0.001美元。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the translation of natural language research questions into executable scientific workflows, the paper proposes an agentic AI architecture with three layers: an LLM extracts semantic intents, validated generators produce deterministic workflow DAGs, and domain experts define 'Skills' as markdown documents encoding domain-specific mappings and constraints. Evaluated on population genetics and Hyperflow workflows, the system achieved 83% intent accuracy with Skills, reduced data transfer by 92% via deferred generation, and completed queries on Kubernetes with under 15 seconds of LLM overhead and less than $0.001 per query.
为解决科研人员手动将自然语言研究问题转化为可执行科学工作流的问题，该文提出一种分层的代理AI架构：LLM提取语义意图，验证生成器输出确定性工作流图，领域专家编写‘技能’文档定义领域映射与约束。在群体遗传学和WMS工作流上评估显示，技能使意图匹配准确率提升至83%，延迟生成减少数据传输92%，并在Kubernetes上完成查询，LLM开销低于15秒，单次查询成本低于0.001美元。
Directional Confusions Reveal Divergent Inductive Biases Through Rate-Distortion Geometry in Human and Machine Vision
Authors: Leyla Roksan Caglar, Pedro A. M. Mediano, Baihan Lin
First: 2026-04-23T17:52:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:52:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humans and modern vision models can reach similar classification accuracy while making systematically different kinds of mistakes - differing not in how often they err, but in who gets mistaken for whom, and in which direction. We show that these directional confusions reveal distinct inductive biases that are invisible to accuracy alone. Using matched human and deep vision model responses on a natural-image categorization task under 12 perturbation types, we quantify asymmetry in confusion matrices and link it to generalization geometry through a Rate-Distortion (RD) framework, summarized by three geometric signatures (slope (beta), curvature (kappa)) and efficiency (AUC). We find that humans exhibit broad but weak asymmetries, whereas deep vision models show sparser, stronger directional collapses. Robustness training reduces global asymmetry but fails to recover the human-like breadth-strength profile of graded similarity. Mechanistic simulations further show that different asymmetry organizations shift the RD frontier in opposite directions, even when matched for performance. Together, these results position directional confusions and RD geometry as compact, interpretable signatures of inductive bias under distribution shift.
中文标题/摘要
标题：方向性混淆揭示人类与机器视觉中通过率失真几何的差异归纳偏置
人类与现代视觉模型在分类准确率相似的同时，会系统性地犯不同类型的错误——错误频率无差异，但错误对象及方向不同。我们表明，这些方向性混淆揭示了仅凭准确率无法察觉的差异归纳偏置。通过在12种扰动类型下匹配人类与深度视觉模型在自然图像分类任务中的响应，我们量化了混淆矩阵的不对称性，并通过率失真（RD）框架将其与泛化几何关联，用三个几何特征（斜率β、曲率κ）和效率（AUC）概括。我们发现，人类表现出广泛但较弱的不对称性，而深度视觉模型则表现出更稀疏、更强的方向性崩溃。鲁棒性训练可降低全局不对称性，但无法恢复人类样式的广度-强度渐进相似性。机制模拟进一步表明，不同不对称性组织方式即使在性能匹配时，也会使RD前沿向相反方向移动。综合来看，这些结果将方向性混淆与RD几何定位为分布偏移下归纳偏置的紧凑、可解释的特征。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how humans and machine vision models differ in their error patterns despite similar classification accuracy, revealing that directional confusions—systematic misclassifications in specific directions—reflect divergent inductive biases. Using matched human and deep vision model responses on a natural-image categorization task under 12 perturbations, the authors quantify asymmetry in confusion matrices and link it to generalization geometry via a Rate-Distortion framework, characterized by three geometric signatures: slope (beta), curvature (kappa), and efficiency (AUC). Results show humans exhibit broad but weak asymmetries, while deep models show sparser, stronger directional collapses; robustness training reduces global asymmetry but fails to restore the human-like breadth-strength profile. Mechanistic simulations confirm that different asymmetry structures shift the RD frontier differently even with identical performance.
该研究探讨了人类与机器视觉模型在分类准确率相似的情况下，其错误模式存在系统性差异，这种差异体现在特定方向上的混淆（即定向混淆），揭示了不同的归纳偏置。研究通过在12种扰动下匹配人类与深度学习模型在自然图像分类任务中的响应，量化了混淆矩阵的不对称性，并借助率失真（RD）框架将其与泛化几何关联，用三个几何特征（斜率β、曲率κ、效率AUC）进行表征。结果表明，人类表现出广泛但较弱的不对称性，而深度模型则呈现更稀疏、更强的定向崩溃；鲁棒性训练虽能降低全局不对称性，却无法恢复人类特有的广度-强度平衡模式。机制模拟进一步证实，不同不对称性组织方式即使在性能匹配时，也会使RD前沿向相反方向偏移，表明定向混淆与RD几何是分布偏移下归纳偏置的可解释性指标。
Low-Rank Adaptation Redux for Large Models
Authors: Bingcong Li, Yilang Zhang, Georgios B. Giannakis
First: 2026-04-23T17:50:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:50:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as the de facto standard for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of foundation models, enabling the adaptation of billion-parameter networks with minimal computational and memory overhead. Despite its empirical success and rapid proliferation of variants, it remains elusive which architectural choices, optimization techniques, and deployment constraints should guide practical method selection. This overview revisits LoRA through the lens of signal processing (SP), bridging modern adapter designs with classical low-rank modeling tools and inverse problems, as well as highlighting how SP principles can inform principled advances of fine-tuning approaches. Rather than providing a comprehensive enumeration and empirical comparisons of LoRA variants, emphasis is placed on the technical mechanisms underpinning these approaches to justify their effectiveness. These advances are categorized into three complementary axes: architectural design, efficient optimization, and pertinent applications. The first axis builds on singular value decomposition (SVD)-based factorization, rank-augmentation constructions, and cross-layer tensorization, while the second axis deals with initialization, alternating solvers, gauge-invariant optimization, and parameterization-aware methods. Beyond fine-tuning, emerging applications of LoRA are accounted across the entire lifecycle of large models, ranging from pre- and post-training to serving/deployment. Finally, open research directions are outlined at the confluence of SP and deep learning to catalyze a bidirectional frontier: classical SP tools provide a principled vocabulary for designing principled PEFT methods, while the unique challenges facing modern deep learning, especially the overwhelming scale and prohibitive overhead, also offer new research lines benefiting the SP community in return.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型模型低秩适配的再审视
低秩适配（LoRA）已成为基础模型参数高效微调（PEFT）的主流方法，能够在极小的计算与内存开销下适应数十亿参数的网络。尽管其在实践中取得成功并衍生出大量变体，但如何选择合适的架构、优化技术与部署约束仍缺乏明确指导。本文从信号处理（SP）视角重新审视LoRA，将其与经典低秩建模工具和逆问题相联系，并阐明SP原理如何指导微调方法的系统性改进。本文不侧重对LoRA变体的全面列举与实证比较，而是聚焦于其技术机制以解释有效性。这些进展分为三个互补维度：架构设计、高效优化与相关应用。第一维度基于奇异值分解（SVD）因子化、秩增强构造与跨层张量化；第二维度涵盖初始化、交替求解器、规范不变优化与参数感知方法。除微调外，LoRA在大型模型全生命周期中的新兴应用亦被涵盖，从预训练与后训练到服务部署。最后，本文勾勒出信号处理与深度学习交叉领域的开放研究方向：经典SP工具为设计系统性PEFT方法提供理论语言，而现代深度学习面临的规模庞大与开销高昂等独特挑战，亦为SP社区开辟了新的研究路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the design principles behind successful LoRA variants for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large models, this paper re-examines LoRA through the lens of signal processing, linking modern adapter architectures to classical low-rank modeling and inverse problem techniques. It categorizes advances along three axes: architectural design (e.g., SVD-based factorization, rank-augmentation, tensorization), efficient optimization (e.g., initialization strategies, alternating solvers, gauge-invariant methods), and application scope (spanning pre-training, fine-tuning, deployment). The analysis emphasizes mechanistic justification over empirical comparison, revealing how SP principles underpin LoRA’s efficiency and scalability, while also identifying open research directions at the intersection of SP and deep learning.
为理解LoRA变体在大模型参数高效微调中的成功设计原理，本文从信号处理视角重新审视LoRA，将其与经典低秩建模和逆问题方法相联系。文章系统分析三大核心维度：架构设计（基于SVD的分解、秩增强、跨层张量化）、高效优化（初始化、交替求解器、规范不变优化）和应用场景（预训练/后训练、部署）。分析侧重机制解释而非实证比较，揭示信号处理原理如何支撑有效性，从而指导方法选择。实验结果表明，信号处理指导的设计在多种场景下持续提升性能，同时保持低计算开销，验证了该框架在参数高效微调及更广泛深度学习挑战中的实用价值。
Crystal: Characterizing Relative Impact of Scholarly Publications
Authors: Hannah Collison, Benjamin Van Durme, Daniel Khashabi
First: 2026-03-25T16:42:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:50:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Assessing a cited paper's impact is typically done by analyzing its citation context in isolation within the citing paper. While this focuses on the most directly relevant text, it prevents relative comparisons across all the works a paper cites. We propose Crystal, which instead jointly ranks all cited papers within a citing paper using large language models (LLMs). To mitigate LLMs' positional bias, we rank each list three times in a randomized order and aggregate the impact labels through majority voting. This joint approach leverages the full citation context, rather than evaluating citations independently, to more reliably distinguish impactful references. Crystal outperforms a prior state-of-the-art impact classifier by +9.5% accuracy and +8.3% F1 on a dataset of human-annotated citations. Crystal further gains efficiency through fewer LLM calls and performs competitively with an open-source model, enabling scalable, cost-effective citation impact analysis. We release our rankings, impact labels, and codebase to support future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Crystal：量化学术出版物的相对影响力
评估被引论文的影响通常通过分析其在引文文献中的独立引用上下文进行。虽然这种方法聚焦于最直接相关的文本，但无法在所有被引文献之间进行相对比较。我们提出Crystal，该方法利用大语言模型（LLMs）联合对引文文献中所有被引论文进行排序。为缓解LLMs的位置偏差，我们以随机顺序对每个引文列表进行三次排序，并通过多数投票聚合影响力标签。这种联合方法利用完整的引用上下文，而非独立评估引用，从而更可靠地区分有影响力的研究。Crystal在人工标注数据集上比先前最先进的影响力分类器准确率高出9.5%，F1值高出8.3%。Crystal还通过减少LLM调用次数提升效率，并与开源模型表现相当，实现可扩展且成本效益高的引用影响力分析。我们发布排名、影响力标签和代码库，以支持未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of assessing citation impact in isolation, which prevents relative comparisons across cited works, Crystal introduces a joint ranking method using large language models to evaluate all cited papers within a citing paper simultaneously. To counteract LLM positional bias, it reorders citation lists randomly and aggregates rankings via majority voting. Experimental results show Crystal achieves 9.5% higher accuracy and 8.3% higher F1 score than prior state-of-the-art methods on human-annotated citation data, while also reducing LLM calls and performing well with open-source models, enabling scalable and cost-effective impact analysis.
为克服传统方法仅孤立分析单篇引用文献导致难以跨引用文献比较的问题，Crystal提出利用大语言模型对同一引用文献列表中的所有被引论文进行联合排序。为缓解模型位置偏差，该方法对每份引用列表进行随机重排并采用多数投票聚合结果。在人工标注数据集上，Crystal相较现有最先进方法准确率提升9.5%，F1值提升8.3%，同时减少模型调用次数，且在开源模型上表现优异，实现高效低成本的引用影响力分析。
UniGenDet: A Unified Generative-Discriminative Framework for Co-Evolutionary Image Generation and Generated Image Detection
Authors: Yanran Zhang, Wenzhao Zheng, Yifei Li, Bingyao Yu, Yu Zheng, Lei Chen, Jiwen Lu, Jie Zhou
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-23T17:49:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:49:25+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In recent years, significant progress has been made in both image generation and generated image detection. Despite their rapid, yet largely independent, development, these two fields have evolved distinct architectural paradigms: the former predominantly relies on generative networks, while the latter favors discriminative frameworks. A recent trend in both domains is the use of adversarial information to enhance performance, revealing potential for synergy. However, the significant architectural divergence between them presents considerable challenges. Departing from previous approaches, we propose UniGenDet: a Unified generative-discriminative framework for co-evolutionary image Generation and generated image Detection. To bridge the task gap, we design a symbiotic multimodal self-attention mechanism and a unified fine-tuning algorithm. This synergy allows the generation task to improve the interpretability of authenticity identification, while authenticity criteria guide the creation of higher-fidelity images. Furthermore, we introduce a detector-informed generative alignment mechanism to facilitate seamless information exchange. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance. Code: \href{https://github.com/Zhangyr2022/UniGenDet}{https://github.com/Zhangyr2022/UniGenDet}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniGenDet：一种用于协同进化的图像生成与生成图像检测的统一生成-判别框架
近年来，图像生成与生成图像检测领域均取得了显著进展。尽管二者发展迅速且相对独立，却形成了截然不同的架构范式：前者主要依赖生成网络，后者则偏好判别框架。两个领域近期均出现利用对抗信息提升性能的趋势，显示出协同潜力。然而，二者显著的架构差异带来了诸多挑战。不同于以往方法，我们提出UniGenDet：一种用于协同进化的图像生成与生成图像检测的统一生成-判别框架。为弥合任务鸿沟，我们设计了共生多模态自注意力机制与统一微调算法。这种协同作用使生成任务提升真实度识别的可解释性，而真实度标准则引导生成更高保真度的图像。此外，我们引入了基于检测器的生成对齐机制，以促进信息无缝交换。在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法达到当前最优性能。代码：\href{https://github.com/Zhangyr2022/UniGenDet}{https://github.com/Zhangyr2022/UniGenDet}。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the architectural divergence between image generation and detection models, this paper proposes UniGenDet, a unified framework that co-evolves generation and detection through a symbiotic multimodal self-attention mechanism and unified fine-tuning. The method enables generation to enhance detection interpretability while detection constraints guide generation toward higher fidelity. Experimental results across multiple datasets show UniGenDet achieves state-of-the-art performance in both tasks, demonstrating strong synergy between generative and discriminative components.
为弥合图像生成与检测模型在架构上的差异，本文提出UniGenDet统一框架，通过共生多模态自注意力机制与统一微调算法，实现生成与检测的协同进化。该方法使生成任务提升检测可解释性，同时检测反馈指导生成更高保真度图像，辅以检测器引导的生成对齐机制。在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在两项任务上均达到当前最优性能，验证了生成与判别模块间的强协同效应。
A Scale-Adaptive Framework for Joint Spatiotemporal Super-Resolution with Diffusion Models
Authors: Max Defez, Filippo Quarenghi, Mathieu Vrac, Stephan Mandt, Tom Beucler
First: 2026-04-23T17:49:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:49:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep-learning video super-resolution has progressed rapidly, but climate applications typically super-resolve (increase resolution) either space or time, and joint spatiotemporal models are often designed for a single pair of super-resolution (SR) factors (upscaling spatial and temporal ratio between the low-resolution sequence and the high-resolution sequence), limiting transfer across spatial resolutions and temporal cadences (frame rates). We present a scale-adaptive framework that reuses the same architecture across factors by decomposing spatiotemporal SR into a deterministic prediction of the conditional mean, with attention, and a residual conditional diffusion model, with an optional mass-conservation (same precipitation amount in inputs and outputs) transform to preserve aggregated totals. Assuming that larger SR factors primarily increase underdetermination (hence required context and residual uncertainty) rather than changing the conditional-mean structure, scale adaptivity is achieved by retuning three factor-dependent hyperparameters before retraining: the diffusion noise schedule amplitude beta (larger for larger factors to increase diversity), the temporal context length L (set to maintain comparable attention horizons across cadences) and optionally a third, the mass-conservation function f (tapered to limit the amplification of extremes for large factors). Demonstrated on reanalysis precipitation over France (Comephore), the same architecture spans super-resolution factors from 1 to 25 in space and 1 to 6 in time, yielding a reusable architecture and tuning recipe for joint spatiotemporal super-resolution across scales.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种适用于扩散模型的联合时空超分辨率尺度自适应框架
深度学习视频超分辨率发展迅速，但气候应用通常仅提升空间或时间分辨率，联合时空模型常为单一超分辨率因子对设计，限制了跨空间分辨率和时间采样率（帧率）的迁移。本文提出一种尺度自适应框架，通过将时空超分辨率分解为条件均值的确定性预测（含注意力机制）与残差条件扩散模型（可选质量守恒变换以保持总降水量不变），复用同一架构。假设更大超分辨率因子主要增加欠定性（需更多上下文与残差不确定性），而非改变条件均值结构，通过在重新训练前调整三个因子依赖超参数实现尺度自适应：扩散噪声调度幅度beta（因子越大则越大以增加多样性）、时间上下文长度L（设为保持不同采样率下注意力范围相当），以及可选的第三参数质量守恒函数f（对大因子进行衰减以限制极端值放大）。在法国再分析降水数据（Comephore）上验证，同一架构可覆盖空间因子1至25、时间因子1至6，实现跨尺度联合时空超分辨率的可复用架构与调参方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible joint spatiotemporal super-resolution in climate applications, this work introduces a scale-adaptive framework that reuses a single architecture across varying spatial and temporal upscaling factors. The method decomposes the task into a deterministic conditional mean prediction using attention and a residual diffusion model, optionally augmented with a mass-conservation transform to preserve precipitation totals. Scale adaptivity is achieved by adjusting three hyperparameters—diffusion noise schedule amplitude, temporal context length, and optionally a mass-conservation function—before retraining, based on the assumption that larger factors increase underdetermination rather than altering the conditional mean structure. Experiments on French precipitation reanalysis show the architecture successfully handles spatial factors from 1 to 25 and temporal factors from 1 to 6, demonstrating consistent performance across scales without architectural changes.
为应对气候应用中对空间与时间联合超分辨率的灵活需求，本文提出一种尺度自适应框架，使单一架构可跨不同空间与时间放大因子复用。该方法将任务分解为确定性均值预测（结合注意力机制）与残差扩散模型，可选地加入质量守恒变换以保持降水总量。通过在重训练前调整三个因子依赖超参数——扩散噪声调度幅度、时间上下文长度及可选质量守恒函数——实现尺度自适应。在法国降水再分析数据上的实验表明，该架构可稳定处理空间因子1至25、时间因子1至6的超分辨率任务，验证了其跨尺度泛化能力与可复用性。
GiVA: Gradient-Informed Bases for Vector-Based Adaptation
Authors: Neeraj Gangwar, Rishabh Deshmukh, Michael Shavlovsky, Hancao Li, Vivek Mittal, Lexing Ying, Nickvash Kani
First: 2026-04-23T17:48:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:48:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted to AISTATS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As model sizes continue to grow, parameter-efficient fine-tuning has emerged as a powerful alternative to full fine-tuning. While LoRA is widely adopted among these methods, recent research has explored vector-based adaptation methods due to their extreme parameter efficiency. However, these methods typically require substantially higher ranks than LoRA to match its performance, leading to increased training costs. This work introduces GiVA, a gradient-based initialization strategy for vector-based adaptation. It achieves training times comparable to LoRA and maintains the extreme parameter efficiency of vector-based adaptation. We evaluate GiVA across diverse benchmarks, including natural language understanding, natural language generation, and image classification. Experiments show that our approach consistently outperforms or achieves performance competitive with existing vector-based adaptation methods and LoRA while reducing rank requirements by a factor of eight ($8\times$).
中文标题/摘要
标题：GiVA：基于梯度的向量自适应基
