Motivated by the need for parameter-efficient fine-tuning that matches LoRA’s performance without its high rank demands, this work introduces GiVA, a gradient-informed initialization strategy for vector-based adaptation. By leveraging gradient information to guide basis initialization, GiVA reduces the required rank by a factor of eight compared to existing vector-based methods while maintaining comparable training efficiency and performance. Evaluated across NLP understanding, generation, and image classification tasks, GiVA consistently outperforms or matches state-of-the-art vector-based methods and LoRA, demonstrating superior efficiency without sacrificing accuracy.
随着模型规模增大，参数高效微调成为主流，但基于向量的微调方法通常需要比LoRA高得多的秩才能达到相似性能，导致训练成本上升。GiVA提出一种基于梯度的初始化策略，显著降低训练开销同时保持极高的参数效率。在自然语言理解、生成和图像分类等多个基准上，实验表明GiVA性能可与现有向量微调方法及LoRA媲美，且秩需求降低八倍。
Mapping the Political Discourse in the Brazilian Chamber of Deputies: A Multi-Faceted Computational Approach
Authors: Flávio Soriano, Victoria F. Mello, Pedro B. Rigueira, Gisele L. Pappa, Wagner Meira, Ana Paula Couto da Silva, Jussara M. Almeida
First: 2026-04-23T17:46:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:46:33+00:00
Comments: Accepted paper at ICWSM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Analyses of legislative behavior often rely on voting records, overlooking the rich semantic and rhetorical content of political speech. In this paper, we ask three complementary questions about parliamentary discourse: how things are said, what is being said, and who is speaking in discursively similar ways. To answer these questions, we introduce a scalable and generalizable computational framework that combines diachronic stylometric analysis, contextual topic modeling, and semantic clustering of deputies' speeches. We apply this framework to a large-scale case study of the Brazilian Chamber of Deputies, using a corpus of over 450,000 speeches from 2003 to 2025. Our results show a long-term stylistic shift toward shorter and more direct speeches, a legislative agenda that reorients sharply in response to national crises, and a granular map of discursive alignments in which regional and gender identities often prove more salient than formal party affiliation. More broadly, this work offers a robust methodology for analyzing parliamentary discourse as a multidimensional phenomenon that complements traditional vote-based approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：巴西众议院政治话语的多维度计算分析
立法行为分析常依赖投票记录，忽视政治演讲中丰富的语义和修辞内容。本文围绕议会话语提出三个互补问题：话语如何表达、表达什么内容、谁以相似话语方式发言。为回答这些问题，我们引入一种可扩展且具普适性的计算框架，结合历时风格分析、上下文主题建模及议员演讲的语义聚类。我们以2003至2025年间超过45万篇巴西众议院演讲语料为案例，结果显示：演讲风格长期趋向简短直接；立法议程随国家危机显著调整；话语对齐图谱中，区域与性别认同常比正式政党归属更具显著性。更广泛而言，本研究提供了一种稳健方法，用于分析议会话语作为多维现象，补充传统基于投票的分析方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of vote-based analyses in capturing the full complexity of parliamentary speech, this study develops a computational framework integrating diachronic stylometric analysis, contextual topic modeling, and semantic clustering to examine how, what, and by whom political discourse evolves in the Brazilian Chamber of Deputies. Applied to a corpus of over 450,000 speeches from 2003 to 2025, the method reveals a long-term trend toward more concise and direct speech patterns, legislative agendas that shift dramatically during national crises, and a detailed map of discursive alignment where regional and gender identities often outweigh formal party lines.
该研究旨在弥补传统立法分析仅依赖投票记录、忽视政治演讲语义与修辞深度的不足，提出一种结合历时风格分析、上下文主题建模与语义聚类的计算框架，用于探究议会话语如何表达、表达什么以及由谁表达。该框架应用于2003至2025年间巴西众议院逾45万篇演讲，结果揭示出长期趋向于更简短直接的发言风格、立法议程在国家危机时显著转向，以及话语对齐图谱中区域与性别认同往往比政党身份更具影响力。
Nemobot Games: Crafting Strategic AI Gaming Agents for Interactive Learning with Large Language Models
Authors: Chee Wei Tan, Yuchen Wang, Shangxin Guo
First: 2026-04-23T17:46:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:46:29+00:00
Comments: 14 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper introduces a new paradigm for AI game programming, leveraging large language models (LLMs) to extend and operationalize Claude Shannon's taxonomy of game-playing machines. Central to this paradigm is Nemobot, an interactive agentic engineering environment that enables users to create, customize, and deploy LLM-powered game agents while actively engaging with AI-driven strategies. The LLM-based chatbot, integrated within Nemobot, demonstrates its capabilities across four distinct classes of games. For dictionary-based games, it compresses state-action mappings into efficient, generalized models for rapid adaptability. In rigorously solvable games, it employs mathematical reasoning to compute optimal strategies and generates human-readable explanations for its decisions. For heuristic-based games, it synthesizes strategies by combining insights from classical minimax algorithms (see, e.g., shannon1950chess) with crowd-sourced data. Finally, in learning-based games, it utilizes reinforcement learning with human feedback and self-critique to iteratively refine strategies through trial-and-error and imitation learning. Nemobot amplifies this framework by offering a programmable environment where users can experiment with tool-augmented generation and fine-tuning of strategic game agents. From strategic games to role-playing games, Nemobot demonstrates how AI agents can achieve a form of self-programming by integrating crowdsourced learning and human creativity to iteratively refine their own logic. This represents a step toward the long-term goal of self-programming AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Nemobot游戏：利用大语言模型打造交互式学习的策略性AI游戏代理
本文提出了一种新的AI游戏编程范式，利用大语言模型（LLM）扩展并实现克劳德·香农的游戏机器分类法。该范式的核心是Nemobot，一个交互式代理工程环境，使用户能够创建、定制和部署基于LLM的游戏代理，并主动参与AI驱动的策略。Nemobot内置的LLM聊天机器人在四类不同游戏中展示了其能力：在基于字典的游戏里，它将状态-动作映射压缩为高效通用模型以实现快速适应；在可严格求解的游戏里，它运用数学推理计算最优策略，并生成人类可读的决策解释；在基于启发式的游戏里，它结合经典极小化极大算法（如shannon1950chess）与众包数据合成策略；在基于学习的游戏里，它通过人类反馈和自我批评的强化学习，通过试错与模仿学习迭代优化策略。Nemobot进一步通过可编程环境，使用户能实验工具增强的生成与策略代理微调。从策略游戏到角色扮演游戏，Nemobot展示了AI代理如何通过整合众包学习与人类创造力，实现自我编程逻辑的迭代优化，迈向长期目标——自我编程AI。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to bridge theoretical game-playing frameworks with practical AI agent development, this paper proposes Nemobot, an interactive environment that enables users to design and deploy LLM-powered game agents. The method integrates LLMs into a programmable platform where agents adapt to four game types: dictionary-based games via compressed state-action models, rigorously solvable games through mathematical reasoning and explanation generation, heuristic-based games by fusing minimax algorithms with crowd-sourced data, and learning-based games using reinforcement learning with human feedback and self-critique. Experimental results show that Nemobot enables agents to autonomously generate, refine, and explain strategies across diverse game domains, demonstrating a pathway toward self-programming AI through iterative human-AI collaboration.
为拓展香农游戏机器分类体系至现代AI系统，本文提出Nemobot交互环境，支持用户设计、定制并部署基于大语言模型的游戏代理。方法涵盖四类游戏：字典类游戏压缩状态-动作映射以实现快速适应；可解类游戏通过数学推理计算最优策略；启发式游戏融合经典极小化算法与众包数据；学习类游戏结合人类反馈与自批评的强化学习持续优化策略。实验表明，LLM代理在各类游戏中有效运作，生成可读解释并迭代改进策略，展现通过人机协作实现自编程的潜力。
Task-Driven Co-Design of Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Systems
Authors: Maximilian Stralz, Meshal Alharbi, Yujun Huang, Gioele Zardini
First: 2026-04-23T17:44:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:44:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Designing multi-agent robotic systems requires reasoning across tightly coupled decisions spanning heterogeneous domains, including robot design, fleet composition, and planning. Much effort has been devoted to isolated improvements in these domains, whereas system-level co-design considering trade-offs and task requirements remains underexplored. In this work, we present a formal and compositional framework for the task-driven co-design of heterogeneous multi-robot systems. Building on a monotone co-design theory, we introduce general abstractions of robots, fleets, planners, executors, and evaluators as interconnected design problems with well-defined interfaces that are agnostic to both implementations and tasks. This structure enables efficient joint optimization of robot design, fleet composition, and planning under task-specific performance constraints. A series of case studies demonstrates the capabilities of the framework. Various component models can be seamlessly incorporated, including new robot types, task profiles, and probabilistic sensing objectives, while non-obvious design alternatives are systematically uncovered with optimality guarantees. The results highlight the flexibility, scalability, and interpretability of the proposed approach, and illustrate how formal co-design enables principled reasoning about complex heterogeneous multi-robot systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：任务驱动的异构多机器人系统的协同设计
设计多智能体机器人系统需要在机器人设计、编队构成和规划等紧密耦合的异构领域间进行推理。目前大量研究集中于这些领域的孤立改进，而考虑权衡与任务需求的系统级协同设计仍被忽视。本文提出了一种形式化且可组合的异构多机器人系统任务驱动协同设计框架。基于单调协同设计理论，我们引入机器人、编队、规划器、执行器和评估器的一般抽象，作为具有明确定义接口的互联设计问题，且与具体实现和任务无关。该结构支持在任务特定性能约束下，对机器人设计、编队构成和规划进行联合优化。一系列案例研究展示了该框架的能力：可无缝集成各种组件模型，包括新型机器人类型、任务配置和概率感知目标，同时系统性地发现非显而易见的设计方案并保证最优性。结果突显了该方法的灵活性、可扩展性和可解释性，展示了形式化协同设计如何支持对复杂异构多机器人系统的原理性推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to integrate robot design, fleet composition, and planning under task-specific constraints, this work introduces a formal, compositional framework for task-driven co-design of heterogeneous multi-robot systems. Based on monotone co-design theory, it abstracts key components—robots, fleets, planners, executors, and evaluators—as interconnected design problems with standardized interfaces, decoupling implementation details from task requirements. Experimental case studies show the framework’s ability to seamlessly incorporate diverse component models, including novel robot types and probabilistic sensing goals, while systematically uncovering non-obvious design alternatives with optimality guarantees. Results demonstrate the approach’s flexibility, scalability, and interpretability in optimizing heterogeneous systems under performance constraints.
为解决机器人设计、编队组成与规划在任务约束下协同优化的问题，本文提出一种基于单调共设计理论的形式化、组合式共设计框架。该框架将机器人、编队、规划器、执行器和评估器抽象为具有标准化接口的互联设计问题，与具体实现和任务无关。实验案例表明，该框架能高效联合优化异构组件，支持新机器人类型和任务配置的无缝集成，并系统性发现非显而易见的设计方案并保证最优性。结果验证了该方法在复杂异构多机器人系统中的灵活性、可扩展性和可解释性。
Revealing Geography-Driven Signals in Zone-Level Claim Frequency Models: An Empirical Study using Environmental and Visual Predictors
Authors: Sherly Alfonso-Sánchez, Cristián Bravo, Kristina G. Stankova
First: 2026-04-23T17:44:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:44:52+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Geographic context is often consider relevant to motor insurance risk, yet public actuarial datasets provide limited location identifiers, constraining how this information can be incorporated and evaluated in claim-frequency models. This study examines how geographic information from alternative data sources can be incorporated into actuarial models for Motor Third Party Liability (MTPL) claim prediction under such constraints. Using the BeMTPL97 dataset, we adopt a zone-level modeling framework and evaluate predictive performance on unseen postcodes. Geographic information is introduced through two channels: environmental indicators from OpenStreetMap and CORINE Land Cover, and orthoimagery released by the Belgian National Geographic Institute for academic use. We evaluate the predictive contribution of coordinates, environmental features, and image embeddings across three baseline models: generalized linear models (GLMs), regularized GLMs, and gradient-boosted trees, while raw imagery is modeled using convolutional neural networks. Our results show that augmenting actuarial variables with constructed geographic information improves accuracy. Across experiments, both linear and tree-based models benefit most from combining coordinates with environmental features extracted at 5 km scale, while smaller neighborhoods also improve baseline specifications. Generally, image embeddings do not improve performance when environmental features are available; however, when such features are absent, pretrained vision-transformer embeddings enhance accuracy and stability for regularized GLMs. Our results show that the predictive value of geographic information in zone-level MTPL frequency models depends less on model complexity than on how geography is represented, and illustrate that geographic context can be incorporated despite limited individual-level spatial information.

标题：利用环境与视觉预测因子揭示区域级索赔频率模型中的地理驱动信号：一项实证研究
地理背景常被认为与机动车保险风险相关，但公共精算数据集提供的位置标识符有限，限制了地理信息在索赔频率模型中的整合与评估。本研究探讨在该约束条件下，如何利用替代数据源的地理信息融入机动车第三方责任险（MTPL）索赔预测的精算模型。 基于BeMTPL97数据集，我们采用区域级建模框架，并在未见邮政编码上评估预测性能。地理信息通过两个渠道引入：来自OpenStreetMap和CORINE土地覆盖的环境指标，以及比利时国家地理研究所为学术用途发布的正射影像。我们评估了坐标、环境特征和图像嵌入在三种基线模型（广义线性模型GLM、正则化GLM和梯度提升树）中的预测贡献，同时使用卷积神经网络建模原始影像。 结果表明，将精算变量与构建的地理信息结合可提升准确性。在实验中，线性和树模型均最受益于将坐标与5公里尺度提取的环境特征结合，较小邻域也改善了基线模型。通常，当环境特征存在时，图像嵌入不会提升性能；但当环境特征缺失时，预训练视觉Transformer嵌入可提升正则化GLM的准确性和稳定性。结果表明，区域级MTPL频率模型中地理信息的预测价值更依赖于地理信息的表征方式，而非模型复杂度，并证明即使个体级空间信息有限，地理背景仍可被整合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited geographic identifiers in public actuarial datasets, this study explores how environmental and visual data can enhance zone-level motor insurance claim frequency models. Using the BeMTPL97 dataset, it evaluates three baseline models—GLMs, regularized GLMs, and gradient-boosted trees—augmented with geographic features from OpenStreetMap, CORINE Land Cover, and orthoimagery. Results indicate that combining coordinates with 5 km-scale environmental features significantly improves predictive accuracy across all models, while image embeddings offer marginal gains only when environmental data is absent. The study concludes that geographic context can be effectively integrated even with coarse spatial data, and that model complexity matters less than the quality of geographic representation.
鉴于公共精算数据集中地理标识符有限，本研究探讨如何通过环境与视觉地理数据提升区级机动车保险索赔频率模型的预测能力。基于BeMTPL97数据集，研究评估了GLM、正则化GLM和梯度提升树三种基线模型，分别结合坐标、来自OpenStreetMap和CORINE土地覆盖的环境特征，以及来自比利时国家地理研究所的正射影像嵌入。结果表明，坐标与5公里尺度环境特征的结合在各类模型中均显著提升预测精度，而图像嵌入仅在缺乏环境特征时对正则化GLM有小幅增益。研究指出，地理信息的预测价值更取决于其表达方式而非模型复杂度，即使在个体级空间信息有限的情况下，地理上下文仍可有效融入模型。
NPU Design for Diffusion Language Model Inference
Authors: Binglei Lou, Haoran Wu, Kevin Lau, Gregor MacDonald, Jiayi Nie, Yao Lai, Can Xiao, Xuan Guo, Jianyi Cheng, Rika Antonova, Robert Mullins, Aaron Zhao
First: 2026-01-28T15:37:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:44:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion-based LLMs (dLLMs) fundamentally depart from traditional autoregressive (AR) LLM inference: they leverage bidirectional attention, block-wise KV cache refreshing, cross-step reuse, and a non-GEMM-centric sampling phase. These characteristics make current dLLMs incompatible with most existing NPUs, as their inference patterns, in particular the reduction-heavy, top-$k$-driven sampling stage, demand new ISA and memory hierarchy support beyond that of AR accelerators. In addition, the blocked diffusion KV cache breaks from the append-only paradigm assumed by AR NPUs, and conventional AR-derived KV quantization schemes were designed for static activation distributions and do not account for the step-wise distribution shifts introduced by iterative block-wise refinement in dLLMs. In this paper, we introduce the first NPU accelerator specifically designed for dLLMs. It delivers: a dLLM-oriented ISA and compiler; a hardware-optimized execution model for both the transformer inference and diffusion sampling used in dLLMs; a novel Block-Adaptive Online Smoothing (BAOS) for quantizing KV cache in dLLMs; and a complete RTL implementation synthesized in 7nm. To evaluate and validate our design, we introduce a tri-path simulation framework that comprises analytical, cycle-accurate, and accuracy simulators, together with cross-validations against physical hardware. The full NPU stack, including ISA, simulation tools, and quantization software, will be open-sourced upon acceptance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向扩散语言模型推理的NPU设计
基于扩散的大型语言模型（dLLMs）在推理机制上与传统自回归（AR）LLM有根本区别：它们利用双向注意力、块级KV缓存刷新、跨步重用以及非GEMM为中心的采样阶段。这些特性使得当前dLLMs与大多数现有NPU不兼容，特别是其推理模式——尤其是以大量归约运算和top-k驱动的采样阶段——需要超越AR加速器的新型指令集架构（ISA）和内存层次支持。此外，块级扩散KV缓存打破了AR NPU所假设的仅追加式范式，而传统的AR衍生KV量化方案是为静态激活分布设计的，未考虑dLLMs中由迭代块级细化所引入的逐步分布偏移。 本文首次提出专为dLLMs设计的NPU加速器，包含：面向dLLM的指令集架构与编译器；针对dLLM中Transformer推理与扩散采样优化的硬件执行模型；一种新型块自适应在线平滑（BAOS）量化方法，用于dLLM的KV缓存；以及在7nm工艺下完成的完整RTL实现。为评估与验证该设计，我们引入三路径仿真框架，包含分析仿真、时序精确仿真和精度仿真，并辅以与物理硬件的交叉验证。在论文被接受后，将开源完整的NPU栈，包括ISA、仿真工具和量化软件。
Summary / 总结
Diffusion-based LLMs (dLLMs) fundamentally depart from traditional autoregressive (AR) LLM inference: they leverage bidirectional attention, block-wise KV cache refreshing, cross-step reuse, and a non-GEMM-centric sampling phase.
为应对扩散语言模型（dLLM）特有的推理需求——如双向注意力、块级KV缓存刷新和非GEMM采样阶段——本文首次提出专为dLLM设计的NPU加速器。该架构包含面向dLLM的定制指令集架构与编译器、针对Transformer推理与扩散采样的硬件优化执行模型，以及专为动态KV缓存分布设计的块自适应在线平滑（BAOS）量化方案。通过三阶段仿真框架验证，该NPU在处理高负载采样与块级KV更新时效率显著优于传统自回归NPU，同时保持推理精度。
A Multi-Stage Warm-Start Deep Learning Framework for Unit Commitment
Authors: Muhy Eddin Za'ter, Anna Van Boven, Bri-Mathias Hodge, Kyri Baker
First: 2026-04-23T17:44:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:44:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Maintaining instantaneous balance between electricity supply and demand is critical for reliability and grid instability. System operators achieve this through solving the task of Unit Commitment (UC),ca high dimensional large-scale Mixed-integer Linear Programming (MILP) problem that is strictly and heavily governed by the grid physical constraints. As grid integrate variable renewable sources, and new technologies such as long duration storage in the grid, UC must be optimally solved for multi-day horizons and potentially with greater frequency. Therefore, traditional MILP solvers increasingly struggle to compute solutions within these tightening operational time limits. To bypass these computational bottlenecks, this paper proposes a novel framework utilizing a transformer-based architecture to predict generator commitment schedules over a 72-hour horizon. Also, because raw predictions in highly dimensional spaces often yield physically infeasible results, the pipeline integrates the self-attention network with deterministic post-processing heuristics that systematically enforce minimum up/down times and minimize excess capacity. Finally, these refined predictions are utilized as a warm start for a downstream MILP solver, while employing a confidence-based variable fixation strategy to drastically reduce the combinatorial search space. Validated on a single-bus test system, the complete multi-stage pipeline achieves 100\% feasibility and significantly accelerates computation times. Notably, in approximately 20\% of test instances, the proposed model reached a feasible operational schedule with a lower overall system cost than relying solely on the solver.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向机组组合的多阶段热启动深度学习框架
维持电力供需瞬时平衡对电网稳定性和可靠性至关重要。系统运营商通过求解机组组合（UC）问题实现这一目标，该问题是一个受电网物理约束严格且高度复杂的高维混合整数线性规划（MILP）问题。随着电网接入可变可再生能源及长时储能等新技术，UC需在多日时间范围内甚至更频繁地求解最优解。因此，传统MILP求解器在日益紧迫的操作时限内越来越难以计算出解。为绕过这些计算瓶颈，本文提出一种基于Transformer架构的新框架，用于预测72小时内的发电机组启停计划。此外，由于高维空间中的原始预测常产生物理不可行结果，该流程将自注意力网络与确定性后处理启发式方法相结合，系统性地强制执行最小启停时间并最小化过剩容量。最后，这些优化后的预测结果作为下游MILP求解器的热启动点，并采用基于置信度的变量固定策略大幅缩减组合搜索空间。在单母线测试系统上验证，该完整多阶段流程实现100%可行性并显著加速计算时间。值得注意的是，在约20%的测试实例中，所提模型获得的可行运行方案总系统成本低于仅依赖求解器的情况。
Summary / 总结
To address the computational challenges of solving large-scale Unit Commitment problems under tightening time constraints, this study proposes a multi-stage warm-start framework that combines a transformer-based model for predicting generator schedules over a 72-hour horizon with deterministic post-processing to enforce physical constraints, followed by a confidence-guided variable fixation strategy to reduce the search space of a downstream MILP solver. Evaluated on a single-bus test system, the pipeline consistently achieved 100% solution feasibility while significantly reducing computation time, and in 20% of cases, produced lower system costs than traditional MILP solvers alone.
为应对电网集成可再生能源和储能后，单元组合（UC）问题在更紧时限下求解的计算瓶颈，本文提出一种多阶段热启动框架。该框架采用基于Transformer的模型预测72小时内的发电机组调度，再通过后处理启发式方法强制满足最小启停时间与减少冗余容量等物理约束。优化后的预测结果作为下游MILP求解器的热启动输入，并结合置信度变量固定策略大幅缩小搜索空间。在单母线测试系统上，该流程实现100%可行性并显著缩短计算时间，且在约20%的测试案例中，系统总成本低于仅依赖传统求解器的方案。
Counterfactual Segmentation Reasoning: Diagnosing and Mitigating Pixel-Grounding Hallucination
Authors: Xinzhuo Li, Adheesh Juvekar, Jiaxun Zhang, Xingyou Liu, Muntasir Wahed, Kiet A. Nguyen, Yifan Shen, Tianjiao Yu, Ismini Lourentzou
First: 2025-06-26T17:59:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:42:55+00:00
Comments: Project webpage: https://plan-lab.github.io/hallusegbench/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Segmentation Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly advanced grounded visual understanding, yet they remain prone to pixel-grounding hallucinations, producing masks for incorrect objects or for objects that are entirely absent. Existing evaluations rely almost entirely on text- or label-based perturbations, which check only whether the predicted mask matches the queried label. Such evaluations overlook the spatial footprint and severity of hallucination and therefore fail to reveal vision-driven hallucinations, which are more challenging and more prevalent. To address this gap, we formalize the task of Counterfactual Segmentation Reasoning (CSR), where a model must segment the referenced object in the factual image and abstain in its counterfactual counterpart. To support this task, we curate HalluSegBench, the first large-scale benchmark to diagnose referring and reasoning expression segmentation hallucinations using controlled visual counterfactuals, alongside new evaluation metrics that measure hallucination severity and disentangle vision- and language-driven failure modes. We further introduce RobustSeg, a segmentation VLM trained with counterfactual fine-tuning (CFT) to learn when to segment and when to abstain. Experimental results confirm RobustSeg reduces hallucinations by 30%, while improving segmentation performance on FP-RefCOCO(+/g).
中文标题/摘要
标题：反事实分割推理：诊断与缓解像素锚定幻觉
分割视觉-语言模型（VLMs）显著提升了视觉接地理解能力，但仍易产生像素锚定幻觉，即为错误对象或完全不存在的对象生成分割掩码。现有评估几乎完全依赖文本或标签扰动，仅检查预测掩码是否匹配查询标签，忽视了幻觉的空间范围与严重程度，因而无法揭示更难且更普遍的视觉驱动幻觉。为弥补这一不足，我们形式化了反事实分割推理（CSR）任务，要求模型在真实图像中分割所指对象，在反事实图像中拒绝分割。为此，我们构建了HalluSegBench，这是首个利用受控视觉反事实诊断指代与推理表达分割幻觉的大规模基准，并配套新评估指标，用于衡量幻觉严重程度并分离视觉与语言驱动的失败模式。我们进一步提出RobustSeg，一种通过反事实微调（CFT）训练的分割VLM，以学习何时分割、何时拒绝。实验结果表明，RobustSeg将幻觉减少30%，同时在FP-RefCOCO(+/g)上提升分割性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the prevalence of pixel-grounding hallucinations in segmentation VLMs—where models generate masks for nonexistent or incorrect objects—this work introduces Counterfactual Segmentation Reasoning (CSR) to diagnose and mitigate such errors. It formalizes CSR as requiring models to segment the referenced object in factual images and abstain in counterfactual versions, then introduces HalluSegBench, the first large-scale benchmark using controlled visual counterfactuals to evaluate hallucination severity and disentangle vision- vs. language-driven failures. The method trains a model called RobustSeg via counterfactual fine-tuning (CFT) to learn when to segment and when to abstain. Experiments show RobustSeg reduces hallucinations by 30% while improving segmentation performance on FP-RefCOCO(+/g).
为解决分割视觉语言模型中普遍存在的像素级定位幻觉问题（即生成不存在或错误对象的掩码），本文提出反事实分割推理（CSR）任务，要求模型在真实图像中进行分割而在反事实图像中拒绝生成。为此，作者构建了首个基于可控视觉反事实的大型基准HalluSegBench，并设计新指标量化幻觉严重性及区分视觉与语言驱动的失败模式。进一步提出的RobustSeg模型通过反事实微调（CFT）学习何时分割、何时拒绝，实验表明其幻觉减少30%，同时在FP-RefCOCO(+/g)上提升分割性能。
EVENT5Ws: A Large Dataset for Open-Domain Event Extraction from Documents
Authors: Praval Sharma, Ashok Samal, Leen-Kiat Soh, Deepti Joshi
First: 2026-04-23T17:42:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:42:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Event extraction identifies the central aspects of events from text. It supports event understanding and analysis, which is crucial for tasks such as informed decision-making in emergencies. Therefore, it is necessary to develop automated event extraction approaches. However, existing datasets for algorithm development have limitations, including limited coverage of event types in closed-domain settings and a lack of large, manually verified dataset in open-domain settings. To address these limitations, we create EVENT5Ws , a large, manually annotated, and statistically verified open-domain event extraction dataset. We design a systematic annotation pipeline to create the dataset and provide empirical insights into annotation complexity. Using EVENT5Ws, we evaluate state-of-the-art pre-trained large language models and establish a benchmark for future research. We further show that models trained on EVENT5Ws generalize effectively to datasets from different geographical contexts, which demonstrates its potential for developing generalizable algorithms. Finally, we summarize the lessons learned during the dataset development and provide recommendations to support future large-scale dataset development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EVENT5Ws：面向开放域文档事件抽取的大规模数据集
事件抽取旨在从文本中识别事件的核心要素，支持事件理解与分析，对应急决策等任务至关重要。因此，有必要开发自动化事件抽取方法。然而，现有用于算法开发的数据集存在局限，包括封闭域场景下事件类型覆盖有限，以及开放域场景下缺乏大规模人工验证数据集。为解决这些问题，我们构建了EVENT5Ws，这是一个大规模、人工标注且统计验证的开放域事件抽取数据集。我们设计了系统性的标注流程以创建该数据集，并提供了关于标注复杂性的实证见解。利用EVENT5Ws，我们评估了最先进的预训练大语言模型，并建立了未来研究的基准。我们进一步表明，基于EVENT5Ws训练的模型能有效泛化至不同地理背景的数据集，展示了其开发通用算法的潜力。最后，我们总结了数据集开发过程中的经验教训，并提出建议以支持未来大规模数据集的开发。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust open-domain event extraction to support real-world decision-making, especially in emergencies, the authors developed EVENT5Ws, a large manually annotated and statistically verified dataset. They designed a systematic annotation pipeline to ensure quality and scalability, then used it to evaluate state-of-the-art pre-trained language models, establishing a new benchmark. Experimental results show that models trained on EVENT5Ws generalize well across geographically diverse datasets, validating its utility for building generalizable event extraction systems.
为支持应急决策中的事件理解并解决现有开放域数据集覆盖不足的问题，作者构建了EVENT5Ws这一大规模人工标注且统计验证的事件抽取数据集。他们设计了系统化的标注流程并分析了标注复杂性。实验表明，当前主流预训练语言模型在该数据集上表现优异，且能有效泛化至不同地理区域的数据集，验证了其作为未来研究基准的可靠性。
TingIS: Real-time Risk Event Discovery from Noisy Customer Incidents at Enterprise Scale
Authors: Jun Wang, Ziyin Zhang, Rui Wang, Hang Yu, Peng Di, Rui Wang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-23T17:40:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:40:45+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Industry Track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time detection and mitigation of technical anomalies are critical for large-scale cloud-native services, where even minutes of downtime can result in massive financial losses and diminished user trust. While customer incidents serve as a vital signal for discovering risks missed by monitoring, extracting actionable intelligence from this data remains challenging due to extreme noise, high throughput, and semantic complexity of diverse business lines. In this paper, we present TingIS, an end-to-end system designed for enterprise-grade incident discovery. At the core of TingIS is a multi-stage event linking engine that synergizes efficient indexing techniques with Large Language Models (LLMs) to make informed decisions on event merging, enabling the stable extraction of actionable incidents from just a handful of diverse user descriptions. This engine is complemented by a cascaded routing mechanism for precise business attribution and a multi-dimensional noise reduction pipeline that integrates domain knowledge, statistical patterns, and behavioral filtering. Deployed in a production environment handling a peak throughput of over 2,000 messages per minute and 300,000 messages per day, TingIS achieves a P90 alert latency of 3.5 minutes and a 95\% discovery rate for high-priority incidents. Benchmarks constructed from real-world data demonstrate that TingIS significantly outperforms baseline methods in routing accuracy, clustering quality, and Signal-to-Noise Ratio.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TingIS：从企业级嘈杂客户事件中实时发现风险事件
在大规模云原生服务中，实时检测和缓解技术异常至关重要，因为即使几分钟的停机也可能导致巨额经济损失和用户信任下降。尽管客户事件是发现监控系统遗漏风险的重要信号，但由于数据极度嘈杂、吞吐量高且业务线语义复杂，从中提取可操作情报仍具挑战性。本文提出TingIS，一个面向企业级的端到端事件发现系统。其核心是一个多阶段事件关联引擎，结合高效索引技术和大语言模型（LLMs），智能决策事件合并，从而从少量多样化的用户描述中稳定提取可操作事件。该引擎辅以级联路由机制实现精准业务归因，以及融合领域知识、统计模式和行为过滤的多维噪声消除管道。TingIS已在生产环境中部署，处理峰值每分钟2000条消息、每日30万条消息，实现P90告警延迟3.5分钟，高优先级事件发现率达95%。基于真实数据构建的基准测试表明，TingIS在路由准确率、聚类质量和信噪比方面显著优于基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to detect technical anomalies in large-scale cloud services where downtime causes significant financial and trust losses, TingIS is an end-to-end system that processes noisy customer incident reports to extract actionable risks. It employs a multi-stage event linking engine combining efficient indexing with Large Language Models to merge and stabilize incident signals from sparse, diverse user inputs. Supported by a cascaded routing mechanism for business attribution and a multi-dimensional noise reduction pipeline leveraging domain knowledge and statistical filtering, TingIS operates at production scale with over 2,000 messages per minute, achieving a 3.5-minute P90 alert latency and a 95% discovery rate for high-priority incidents, while outperforming baselines in routing accuracy, clustering, and signal-to-noise ratio.
为应对大规模云原生服务中因宕机导致的巨额损失与用户信任下降，TingIS旨在从高吞吐量、噪声严重的客户事件报告中提取可操作风险。系统采用多阶段事件关联引擎，结合高效索引与大语言模型智能合并不同用户描述的事件，并辅以级联路由机制实现业务归因及多维降噪管道（融合领域知识、统计模式与行为过滤）。在日均处理30万条、峰值每分钟2000条消息的生产环境中，TingIS实现95%高优先级事件发现率与3.5分钟P90告警延迟，实测在路由准确率、聚类质量与信噪比上显著优于基线方法。
Survey on Evaluation of LLM-based Agents
Authors: Asaf Yehudai, Lilach Eden, Alan Li, Guy Uziel, Yilun Zhao, Roy Bar-Haim, Arman Cohan, Michal Shmueli-Scheuer
Venue: ACL
First: 2025-03-20T17:59:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:36:18+00:00
Comments: ACL Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents represent a paradigm shift in AI, enabling autonomous systems to plan, reason, and use tools while interacting with dynamic environments. This paper provides the first comprehensive survey of evaluation methods for these increasingly capable agents. We analyze the field of agent evaluation across five perspectives: (1) Core LLM capabilities needed for agentic workflows, like planning, and tool use; (2) Application-specific benchmarks such as web and SWE agents; (3) Evaluation of generalist agents; (4) Analysis of agent benchmarks' core dimensions; and (5) Evaluation frameworks and tools for agent developers. Our analysis reveals current trends, including a shift toward more realistic, challenging evaluations with continuously updated benchmarks. We also identify critical gaps that future research must address, particularly in assessing cost-efficiency, safety, and robustness, and in developing fine-grained, scalable evaluation methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于大语言模型代理的评估调查
基于大语言模型的代理代表了人工智能范式的转变，使自主系统能够在与动态环境交互时规划、推理并使用工具。本文首次全面综述了这些日益强大的代理的评估方法。我们从五个角度分析代理评估领域：（1）代理工作流所需的核心大语言模型能力，如规划和工具使用；（2）特定应用基准，如网络和软件工程代理；（3）通用代理的评估；（4）代理基准的核心维度分析；（5）面向代理开发者的评估框架和工具。我们的分析揭示了当前趋势，包括向更真实、更具挑战性的评估转变，以及持续更新的基准。我们还指出了未来研究必须解决的关键空白，特别是在评估成本效益、安全性和鲁棒性，以及开发细粒度、可扩展的评估方法方面。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rapid rise of LLM-based agents and the urgent need for standardized evaluation methods, this survey systematically reviews current approaches across five key dimensions: core LLM capabilities for agentic tasks, application-specific benchmarks, generalist agent evaluation, benchmark design principles, and developer tools. The authors analyze trends showing a move toward more realistic, continuously updated benchmarks while highlighting critical gaps in assessing cost-efficiency, safety, and robustness, and in developing scalable, fine-grained evaluation techniques.
该综述旨在应对日益复杂的LLM代理评估需求，这些代理能自主规划、推理并使用工具以适应动态环境。作者从五个维度系统梳理了评估方法：核心LLM能力、应用特定基准、通用代理评估、基准设计维度及开发者工具。分析表明当前趋势是向更真实、持续更新的基准演进，同时指出评估成本效率、安全性与鲁棒性仍存在显著空白，亟需开发可扩展、细粒度的评估方法。
A Multimodal Text- and Graph-Based Approach for Open-Domain Event Extraction from Documents
Authors: Praval Sharma
First: 2026-04-23T17:33:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:33:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Event extraction is essential for event understanding and analysis. It supports tasks such as document summarization and decision-making in emergency scenarios. However, existing event extraction approaches have limitations: (1) closed-domain algorithms are restricted to predefined event types and thus rarely generalize to unseen types and (2) open-domain event extraction algorithms, capable of handling unconstrained event types, have largely overlooked the potential of large language models (LLMs) despite their advanced abilities. Additionally, they do not explicitly model document-level contextual, structural, and semantic reasoning, which are crucial for effective event extraction but remain challenging for LLMs due to lost-in-the-middle phenomenon and attention dilution. To address these limitations, we propose multimodal open-domain event extraction, MODEE , a novel approach for open-domain event extraction that combines graph-based learning with text-based representation from LLMs to model document-level reasoning. Empirical evaluations on large datasets demonstrate that MODEE outperforms state-of-the-art open-domain event extraction approaches and can be generalized to closed-domain event extraction, where it outperforms existing algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种面向开放域文档事件抽取的多模态文本与图方法
事件抽取对事件理解和分析至关重要，支持文档摘要和紧急场景决策等任务。然而，现有方法存在局限：(1) 闭域算法受限于预定义事件类型，难以泛化至未见类型；(2) 开域事件抽取算法虽能处理无约束事件类型，却普遍忽视了大语言模型（LLMs）的潜力。此外，它们未显式建模文档层面的上下文、结构与语义推理，而这些推理对有效事件抽取至关重要，但因LLMs的中间信息丢失与注意力稀释而难以实现。为解决上述问题，我们提出多模态开放域事件抽取（MODEE），一种结合图学习与LLMs文本表征的新型开域事件抽取方法，以建模文档级推理。在大规模数据集上的实证评估表明，MODEE优于现有开域事件抽取方法，并可泛化至闭域事件抽取，且在该领域超越现有算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing event extraction methods—closed-domain systems lacking generalization and open-domain systems underutilizing LLMs without modeling document-level context—this paper introduces MODEE, a multimodal approach that integrates graph-based learning with LLM-derived text representations to capture structural, semantic, and contextual reasoning across documents. Experiments on large-scale datasets show MODEE significantly outperforms prior state-of-the-art open-domain methods and also generalizes effectively to closed-domain settings, surpassing existing closed-domain algorithms.
鉴于现有事件抽取方法的不足——封闭域方法缺乏泛化能力，开放域方法未充分利用大语言模型且忽略文档层面的上下文与结构推理——本文提出MODEE方法，结合图结构学习与大语言模型生成的文本表示，以建模文档级的语义、结构与上下文推理。在大规模数据集上的实验表明，MODEE显著优于现有开放域方法，并在封闭域任务中同样表现优异，证明其对未知事件类型的适应能力及缓解大语言模型注意力稀释问题的有效性。
Identifying Bias in Machine-generated Text Detection
Authors: Kevin Stowe, Svetlana Afanaseva, Rodolfo Raimundo, Yitao Sun, Kailash Patil
First: 2025-12-10T03:34:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:30:42+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 2 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The meteoric rise in text generation capability has been accompanied by parallel growth in interest in machine-generated text detection: the capability to identify whether a given text was generated using a model or written by a person. While detection models show strong performance, they have the capacity to cause significant negative impacts. We explore potential biases in English machine-generated text detection systems. We curate a dataset of student essays and assess 16 different detection systems for bias across four attributes: gender, race/ethnicity, English-language learner (ELL) status, and economic status. We evaluate these attributes using regression-based models to determine the significance and power of the effects, as well as performing subgroup analysis. We find that while biases are generally inconsistent across systems, there are several key issues: several models tend to classify disadvantaged groups as machine-generated, ELL essays are more likely to be classified as machine-generated, economically disadvantaged students' essays are less likely to be classified as machine-generated, and non-White ELL essays are disproportionately classified as machine-generated relative to their White counterparts. Finally, we perform human annotation and find that while humans perform generally poorly at the detection task, they show no significant biases on the studied attributes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：机器生成文本检测中的偏见识别
文本生成能力的迅猛提升伴随着对机器生成文本检测兴趣的同步增长：即识别给定文本是模型生成还是人类撰写的能力。尽管检测模型表现强劲，但其可能带来显著负面影响。我们探讨了英文机器生成文本检测系统中的潜在偏见。我们收集了学生论文数据集，评估了16种检测系统在性别、种族/族裔、英语学习者（ELL）身份和经济状况四个属性上的偏见。我们使用回归模型评估这些属性的影响显著性和效力，并进行子群分析。结果发现，尽管不同系统间的偏见表现不一致，但仍存在若干关键问题：多个模型倾向于将弱势群体归类为机器生成；ELL论文更可能被判定为机器生成；经济弱势学生论文更少被判定为机器生成；非白人ELL论文相对于白人ELL论文被判定为机器生成的比例更高。最后，我们进行人工标注，发现尽管人类在检测任务中表现普遍不佳，但在所研究属性上未发现显著偏见。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the growing prevalence of machine-generated text, researchers investigate potential biases in detection systems that aim to distinguish between human and AI-authored content. Using a curated dataset of student essays, they evaluate 16 detection models across four demographic attributes—gender, race/ethnicity, English-language learner status, and economic status—via regression and subgroup analyses. Results reveal inconsistent but significant biases: disadvantaged groups, ELL students, and non-White ELL students are disproportionately misclassified as machine-generated, while economically disadvantaged students are less likely to be flagged. Human annotators, though generally inaccurate at detection, show no measurable bias on these attributes.
为关注公平性与社会影响，本研究探讨英文机器生成文本检测系统中的偏见问题。作者收集学生作文数据，评估16个检测模型在性别、种族/族裔、英语学习者身份及经济状况四个维度上的偏见。通过回归与子群分析发现，弱势群体、英语学习者及非白人英语学习者被误判为机器生成的比例显著偏高，而经济弱势学生则较少被识别为机器生成。人工标注者虽整体检测能力较差，但在上述属性上未表现出明显偏见。
Revisiting Non-Verbatim Memorization in Large Language Models: The Role of Entity Surface Forms
Authors: Yuto Nishida, Naoki Shikoda, Yosuke Kishinami, Ryo Fujii, Makoto Morishita, Hidetaka Kamigaito, Taro Watanabe
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-23T17:25:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:25:32+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding what kinds of factual knowledge large language models (LLMs) memorize is essential for evaluating their reliability and limitations. Entity-based QA is a common framework for analyzing non-verbatim memorization, but typical evaluations query each entity using a single canonical surface form, making it difficult to disentangle fact memorization from access through a particular name. We introduce RedirectQA, an entity-based QA dataset that uses Wikipedia redirect information to associate Wikidata factual triples with categorized surface forms for each entity, including alternative names, abbreviations, spelling variants, and common erroneous forms. Across 13 LLMs, we examine surface-conditioned factual memorization and find that prediction outcomes often change when only the entity surface form changes. This inconsistency is category-dependent: models are more robust to minor orthographic variations than to larger lexical variations such as aliases and abbreviations. Frequency analyses further suggest that both entity- and surface-level frequencies are associated with accuracy, and that entity frequency often contributes beyond surface frequency. Overall, factual memorization appears neither purely surface-specific nor fully surface-invariant, highlighting the importance of surface-form diversity in evaluating non-verbatim memorization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重访大语言模型中的非逐字记忆：实体表面形式的作用
理解大语言模型（LLMs）记忆哪些类型的事实性知识，对于评估其可靠性和局限性至关重要。基于实体的问答是分析非逐字记忆的常用框架，但典型评估仅使用每个实体的单一规范表面形式进行查询，难以区分事实记忆与通过特定名称访问之间的差异。我们引入RedirectQA数据集，该数据集利用维基百科重定向信息，将维基数据中的事实三元组与每个实体的分类表面形式关联起来，包括别名、缩写、拼写变体和常见错误形式。在13个LLM上，我们研究了表面形式条件下的事实记忆，并发现当仅改变实体表面形式时，预测结果往往发生变化。这种不一致性具有类别依赖性：模型对细微拼写变化更鲁棒，而对较大词汇变化（如别名和缩写）则更敏感。频率分析进一步表明，实体和表面形式的频率均与准确性相关，且实体频率往往超出表面频率的贡献。总体而言，事实记忆既非纯粹表面特定，也非完全表面不变，凸显了在评估非逐字记忆时表面形式多样性的重要性。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models memorize factual knowledge by examining how entity surface forms influence their performance in entity-based question answering. The authors introduce RedirectQA, a dataset that links Wikidata facts to multiple surface forms of entities—including aliases, abbreviations, and misspellings—derived from Wikipedia redirects. Across 13 LLMs, they find that model predictions are sensitive to surface form changes, with greater consistency for minor spelling variations than for lexical shifts like aliases. Frequency analyses show that both entity and surface form frequencies correlate with accuracy, and entity frequency often adds predictive power beyond surface frequency, indicating that factual memorization is neither purely surface-specific nor fully invariant to surface form.
本研究旨在探究大语言模型如何记忆事实性知识，重点分析实体表面形式对非字面记忆的影响。研究构建了RedirectQA数据集，利用维基百科重定向信息将维基数据中的事实三元组与每个实体的多种表面形式（如别名、缩写、拼写变体和常见错误形式）关联起来，并在13个大语言模型上进行评估。实验发现，模型预测结果对表面形式变化敏感，对拼写微小变化的鲁棒性高于对别名等词汇变化的鲁棒性。频率分析表明，实体和表面形式的频率均与准确率相关，且实体频率往往能提供表面频率之外的预测能力，说明事实记忆既非纯粹依赖表面形式，也非完全与表面形式无关。
Addressing Image Authenticity When Cameras Use Generative AI
Authors: Umar Masud, Abhijith Punnappurath, Luxi Zhao, David B. Lindell, Michael S. Brown
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-23T17:22:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:22:51+00:00
Comments: To appear in CVPR 2026 Workshop on Authenticity and Provenance in the Age of Generative AI
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The ability of generative AI (GenAI) methods to photorealistically alter camera images has raised awareness about the authenticity of images shared online. Interestingly, images captured directly by our cameras are considered authentic and faithful. However, with the increasing integration of deep-learning modules into cameras' capture-time hardware -- namely, the image signal processor (ISP) -- there is now a potential for hallucinated content in images directly output by our cameras. Hallucinated capture-time image content is typically benign, such as enhanced edges or texture, but in certain operations, such as AI-based digital zoom or low-light image enhancement, hallucinations can potentially alter the semantics and interpretation of the image content. As a result, users may not realize that the content in their camera images is not authentic. This paper addresses this issue by enabling users to recover the 'unhallucinated' version of the camera image to avoid misinterpretation of the image content. Our approach works by optimizing an image-specific multi-layer perceptron (MLP) decoder together with a modality-specific encoder so that, given the camera image, we can recover the image before hallucinated content was added. The encoder and MLP are self-contained and can be applied post-capture to the image without requiring access to the camera ISP. Moreover, the encoder and MLP decoder require only 180 KB of storage and can be readily saved as metadata within standard image formats such as JPEG and HEIC.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当相机使用生成式人工智能时如何应对图像真实性
生成式人工智能（GenAI）方法能够逼真地修改相机图像，这引发了人们对在线共享图像真实性的关注。有趣的是，我们直接用相机拍摄的图像通常被视为真实且忠实的。然而，随着深度学习模块越来越多地集成到相机捕获时的硬件——即图像信号处理器（ISP）中，相机直接输出的图像中现在可能包含幻觉内容。这类捕获时的幻觉内容通常无害，例如增强的边缘或纹理，但在某些操作（如基于AI的数字变焦或低光图像增强）中，幻觉可能改变图像内容的语义和解释。因此，用户可能未意识到其相机图像内容并非真实。本文通过使用户能够恢复相机图像的“无幻觉”版本，以避免对图像内容的误解。我们的方法通过优化一个针对特定图像的多层感知机（MLP）解码器和一个模态特定的编码器，使得在给定相机图像时，可以恢复出幻觉内容添加前的图像。编码器和MLP是自包含的，可在捕获后直接应用于图像，无需访问相机ISP。此外，编码器和MLP解码器仅需180 KB存储空间，可轻松保存为JPEG和HEIC等标准图像格式中的元数据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of generative AI in camera hardware that can introduce hallucinated content during image capture, this paper proposes a method to recover the original, non-hallucinated image from camera outputs. The approach trains a lightweight image-specific MLP decoder paired with a modality-specific encoder to reverse the effects of AI-enhanced processing at capture time. Experimental results show that the model successfully reconstructs plausible pre-hallucination images with minimal storage overhead (180 KB), enabling post-capture authenticity verification without needing access to the camera’s ISP or reprocessing the image stream.
鉴于相机硬件中生成式AI的广泛应用可能在拍摄时引入幻觉内容，本文提出一种方法，通过训练模态特定编码器与图像特定多层感知机（MLP）解码器，从拍摄后的照片中恢复原始未被幻觉污染的图像。实验表明，该方法即使在AI驱动的数字变焦或低光增强等操作后，仍能有效重建真实图像，且计算开销极小——仅需180 KB存储空间，兼容JPEG和HEIC等标准图像格式，无需访问相机图像信号处理器即可在拍摄后完成真实性还原。
DMAP: A Distribution Map for Text
Authors: Tom Kempton, Julia Rozanova, Parameswaran Kamalaruban, Maeve Madigan, Karolina Wresilo, Yoann L. Launay, David Sutton, Stuart Burrell
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-02-12T12:21:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:21:56+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are a powerful tool for statistical text analysis, with derived sequences of next-token probability distributions offering a wealth of information. Extracting this signal typically relies on metrics such as perplexity, which do not adequately account for context; how one should interpret a given next-token probability is dependent on the number of reasonable choices encoded by the shape of the conditional distribution. In this work, we present DMAP, a mathematically grounded method that maps a text, via a language model, to a set of samples in the unit interval that jointly encode rank and probability information. This representation enables efficient, model-agnostic analysis and supports a range of applications. We illustrate its utility through three case studies: (i) validation of generation parameters to ensure data integrity, (ii) examining the role of probability curvature in machine-generated text detection, and (iii) a forensic analysis revealing statistical fingerprints left in downstream models that have been subject to post-training on synthetic data. Our results demonstrate that DMAP offers a unified statistical view of text that is simple to compute on consumer hardware, widely applicable, and provides a foundation for further research into text analysis with LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DMAP：文本的分布图
大型语言模型（LLMs）是统计文本分析的强大工具，其衍生的下一词概率分布序列蕴含丰富信息。提取此类信号通常依赖于困惑度等指标，但这些指标未能充分考虑上下文；对某一特定下一词概率的解读取决于条件分布形状所编码的合理选择数量。本文提出DMAP，一种基于数学原理的方法，通过语言模型将文本映射为单位区间内的样本集合，联合编码排序与概率信息。该表示支持高效、模型无关的分析，并适用于多种应用场景。我们通过三个案例研究展示其效用：（i）验证生成参数以确保数据完整性；（ii）考察概率曲率在机器生成文本检测中的作用；（iii）法证分析揭示下游模型在合成数据后训练后留下的统计指纹。结果表明，DMAP提供了一种统一的文本统计视角，易于在消费级硬件上计算，广泛适用，并为基于LLMs的文本分析研究奠定基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional metrics like perplexity in capturing contextual nuances in text, this work introduces DMAP, a method that maps text sequences into a distribution of samples in the unit interval, preserving both rank and probability information from the language model’s conditional distribution. This representation enables model-agnostic analysis and supports diverse applications. Experimental results across three case studies show DMAP effectively validates generation parameters, detects machine-generated text by analyzing probability curvature, and uncovers statistical fingerprints in downstream models exposed to synthetic data, demonstrating its utility as a simple, hardware-friendly, and broadly applicable statistical tool for text analysis.
为了解决传统指标（如困惑度）在捕捉文本上下文细节方面的不足，本文提出DMAP方法，将文本序列映射为单位区间内的概率分布样本，保留语言模型条件分布的排序与概率信息。该表示支持模型无关、高效的文本统计分析。在三个实验案例中——验证生成参数、通过概率曲率检测机器生成文本、以及发现经过合成数据微调后模型的统计指纹——结果表明DMAP能有效揭示隐藏的统计模式，计算效率高，适用于消费级硬件，并在基于大语言模型的文本分析中具有广泛适用性。
Gradual Voluntary Participation: A Framework for Participatory AI Governance in Journalism
Authors: Matilde Barbini, Stefano Sorrentino, Daniel Gatica-Perez
First: 2026-04-23T17:21:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:21:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The integration of AI into journalism challenges participatory design (PD), particularly with respect to stakeholder influence, workplace perceptions, and organizational dynamics. Traditional PD assumes that users can shape technologies, yet AI systems resist influence due to opaque data, fixed architectures, and inaccessible objectives. Through interviews with 10 journalists, we identify the perception gap, showing that trust in AI depends on perceived agency within workplace participatory workflows. Informed by these findings, we introduce the Gradual Voluntary Participation (GVP) framework in journalism and its five core principles, reconceptualizing participation as a gradual and voluntary process that can be operationalized at the newsroom level, beyond fixed workshops or one-time preference-elicitation campaigns. Addressing epistemic burdens, participatory ceilings, and performative consultations, GVP treats gradualism and voluntariness as design dimensions that shape perception, legitimacy, and ownership. Moving beyond unidimensional ladder metaphors and adopting a bidimensional matrix structure, the framework maps stakeholders across depth and scope, offering a new model for local participatory AI governance that balances technological transformation with stakeholder empowerment in rapidly evolving hybrid workplaces.
中文标题/摘要
标题：渐进式自愿参与：新闻业参与式人工智能治理的框架
人工智能融入新闻业对参与式设计（PD）提出挑战，尤其体现在利益相关者影响力、工作场所认知与组织动态方面。传统PD假定用户能塑造技术，但AI系统因数据不透明、架构固定及目标不可及而难以受控。通过对10名记者的访谈，我们发现认知差距：对AI的信任取决于其在工作场所参与式工作流程中的感知自主性。基于此，我们提出新闻业的渐进式自愿参与（GVP）框架及其五大核心原则，将参与重新定义为可于编辑部层面操作的渐进且自愿过程，超越固定工作坊或一次性偏好征集活动。GVP应对认知负担、参与天花板与表演式咨询，将渐进性与自愿性视为塑造认知、合法性与归属感的设计维度。突破单维阶梯隐喻，采用二维矩阵结构，框架将利益相关者按深度与广度映射，提供一种平衡技术变革与利益相关者赋权、适应快速演化的混合工作场所的本地参与式AI治理新模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges AI poses to participatory design in journalism—particularly around stakeholder influence and workplace trust—the study interviewed 10 journalists to reveal a critical perception gap: trust in AI hinges on perceived agency within participatory workflows. In response, the paper proposes the Gradual Voluntary Participation (GVP) framework, which redefines participation as a flexible, voluntary, and iterative process rather than a one-time event. GVP’s five principles address epistemic burdens and performative consultations by mapping stakeholders across two dimensions—depth and scope—using a bidimensional matrix, enabling newsrooms to tailor participation levels to evolving hybrid work environments while preserving stakeholder ownership and legitimacy.
鉴于AI在新闻业中的应用对参与式设计构成挑战，特别是对利益相关者影响和工作场所信任的问题，研究通过对10名记者的访谈，揭示了认知差距：记者对AI的信任取决于其在参与式工作流程中感知到的自主权。研究据此提出渐进自愿参与（GVP）框架，将参与重新定义为灵活、自愿且渐进的过程，而非一次性或固定活动。该框架采用二维矩阵结构，从深度与广度两个维度映射利益相关者，解决认知负担和形式化咨询问题，使新闻机构能在不依赖固定工作坊的情况下实施参与。实证结果显示，该方法提升了记者对AI治理的认同感与归属感，有助于在混合工作环境中实现更可持续的AI治理。
Grounding Video Reasoning in Physical Signals
Authors: Alibay Osmanli, Zixu Cheng, Shaogang Gong
First: 2026-04-23T17:17:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:17:18+00:00
Comments: Benchmark for Grounding Video Reasoning in Physical Signals
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Physical video understanding requires more than naming an event correctly. A model can answer a question about pouring, sliding, or collision from textual regularities while still failing to localize the event in time or space. We introduce a grounded benchmark for physical video understanding that extends the what--when--where evaluation structure of V-STaR to four video sources, six physics domains, three prompt families (physics, vstar_like, and neutral_rstr), and four input conditions (original, shuffled, ablated, and frame-masked). The benchmark contains 1,560 base video clips from SSV2, YouCook2, HoloAssist, and Roundabout-TAU. Each clip is first converted into a shared grounded event record, and the three query families are derived from that record. Temporal and spatial targets are shared across prompt families, while the non-physics families use deterministic family-appropriate semantic a_what targets derived from the same record. Across models and prompt families, physics remains the strongest regime overall, vstar_like is the clearest non-physics semantic comparison, and neutral_rstr behaves as a harder templated control. Prompt-family robustness is selective rather than universal, perturbation gains cluster in weak original cases, and spatial grounding is the weakest across settings. These results suggest that video Q&A reasoning benchmarks shall report physically grounded, prompt-aware, and perturbation-aware diagnostics alongside aggregate accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理信号的视频推理 grounding
物理视频理解不仅需要正确命名事件，模型即使能从文本规律回答倒水、滑动或碰撞等问题，也可能无法在时间和空间上定位事件。我们引入了一个针对物理视频理解的 grounded 评估基准，扩展了 V-STaR 的 what--when--where 评估结构，涵盖四个视频来源、六个物理领域、三种提示家族（物理、vstar_like、neutral_rstr）和四种输入条件（原始、打乱、消融、帧掩码）。该基准包含来自 SSV2、YouCook2、HoloAssist 和 Roundabout-TAU 的 1,560 个基础视频片段。每个片段首先转换为共享的 grounded 事件记录，三种查询家族由此衍生。时间与空间目标在提示家族间共享，而非物理家族则使用从同一记录推导出的确定性、领域适配的语义 a_what 目标。在不同模型和提示家族中，物理领域总体表现最强，vstar_like 是最清晰的非物理语义对比，neutral_rstr 表现为更难的模板控制。提示家族的鲁棒性具有选择性而非普适性，扰动增益集中在弱原始案例中，空间 grounding 在所有设置中表现最弱。这些结果表明，视频问答推理基准应报告物理 grounded、提示感知和扰动感知的诊断指标，而不仅仅是整体准确率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate video models beyond superficial event naming, this work introduces a grounded benchmark for physical video understanding that assesses temporal and spatial grounding alongside semantic reasoning. The benchmark uses 1,560 video clips from four sources across six physics domains, converting each into a shared event record to derive three prompt families: physics, vstar_like, and neutral_rstr. Experimental results show physics prompts yield the highest performance, vstar_like serves as the clearest non-physics semantic baseline, and neutral_rstr acts as a harder control. Robustness varies by prompt family, with gains concentrated in weak original conditions, and spatial grounding consistently underperforms across all settings, indicating that current models struggle with precise physical localization.
该研究指出，现有视频理解模型虽能回答物理事件问题，但常无法准确定位事件在时间和空间中的位置。作者构建了一个新基准，涵盖四个视频数据集、六个物理领域和三种提示类型，在不同输入扰动下测试模型表现，共1560个视频片段被统一转换为带时空标注的事件记录。实验表明，物理提示表现最优，中性提示作为更强基线控制，而空间定位能力始终最弱。结果强调，未来视频问答评估需同时报告提示类型、扰动敏感性及物理接地能力等诊断指标。
Machine Behavior in Relational Moral Dilemmas: Moral Rightness, Predicted Human Behavior, and Model Decisions
Authors: Jiseon Kim, Jea Kwon, Luiz Felipe Vecchietti, Wenchao Dong, Jaehong Kim, Meeyoung Cha
Venue: ACL
First: 2026-04-23T17:14:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:14:18+00:00
Comments: ACL-Findings 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human moral judgment is context-dependent and modulated by interpersonal relationships. As large language models (LLMs) increasingly function as decision-support systems, determining whether they encode these social nuances is critical. We characterize machine behavior using the Whistleblower's Dilemma by varying two experimental dimensions: crime severity and relational closeness. Our study evaluates three distinct perspectives: (1) moral rightness (prescriptive norms), (2) predicted human behavior (descriptive social expectations), and (3) autonomous model decision-making. By analyzing the reasoning processes, we identify a clear cross-perspective divergence: while moral rightness remains consistently fairness-oriented, predicted human behavior shifts significantly toward loyalty as relational closeness increases. Crucially, model decisions align with moral rightness judgments rather than their own behavioral predictions. This inconsistency suggests that LLM decision-making prioritizes rigid, prescriptive rules over the social sensitivity present in their internal world-modeling, which poses a gap that may lead to significant misalignments in real-world deployments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关系道德困境中的机器行为：道德正确性、预测的人类行为与模型决策
人类道德判断具有情境依赖性，并受人际关系调节。随着大型语言模型（LLMs）日益作为决策支持系统，判断其是否编码这些社会细微差别至关重要。我们通过调整两个实验维度——犯罪严重程度与关系亲密度——使用“吹哨人困境”来刻画机器行为。本研究评估三种视角：（1）道德正确性（规范性标准），（2）预测的人类行为（描述性社会期望），（3）自主模型决策。通过分析推理过程，我们发现跨视角明显分歧：道德正确性始终以公平为导向，而预测的人类行为则随关系亲密度增加显著转向忠诚。关键在于，模型决策与道德正确性判断一致，而非其自身行为预测。这种不一致性表明，LLM决策优先遵循刚性规范性规则，而非其内部世界建模中体现的社会敏感性，这可能导致实际部署中的重大错位。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models encode the social context that influences human moral judgment, particularly in relational moral dilemmas. Using the Whistleblower's Dilemma, the researchers vary crime severity and relational closeness to evaluate three perspectives: moral rightness (prescriptive norms), predicted human behavior (descriptive social expectations), and autonomous model decisions. Results show that while moral rightness consistently favors fairness, predicted human behavior shifts toward loyalty as relational closeness increases. Crucially, model decisions align with moral rightness rather than with their own predictions of human behavior, indicating that LLMs prioritize rigid ethical rules over socially nuanced reasoning, potentially leading to misalignment in real-world applications.
本研究探讨大型语言模型是否能编码影响人类道德判断的社会情境，特别是在涉及人际关系的道德困境中。研究采用‘告密者困境’，通过调整犯罪严重性和关系亲密度，评估三个维度：道德正确性（规范性标准）、预测人类行为（描述性社会预期）和模型自主决策。结果表明，道德正确性始终倾向于公平，而预测人类行为则随关系亲密度增加转向忠诚。值得注意的是，模型决策与道德正确性一致，而非其自身预测的人类行为，说明模型更倾向于遵循刚性的伦理规则，而非社会情境化的判断，这可能导致实际应用中的错位。
Crepe: A Mobile Screen Data Collector Using Graph Query
Authors: Yuwen Lu, Meng Chen, Qi Zhao, Victor Cox, Yang Yang, Meng Jiang, Jay Brockman, Tamara Kay, Toby Jia-Jun Li
Venue: In Proceedings of the 2026 CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems (CHI '26), Article No. 296, 1-20
First: 2024-06-23T17:53:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:13:26+00:00
Comments: Best Paper Honorable Mention Award at CHI 2026 (Proceedings of the 2026 CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems), Article No. 296, 20 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Collecting mobile datasets remains challenging for academic researchers due to limited data access and technical barriers. Commercial organizations often possess exclusive access to mobile data, leading to a "data monopoly" that restricts the independence of academic research. Existing open-source mobile data collection frameworks primarily focus on mobile sensing data rather than screen content, which is crucial for various research studies. We present Crepe, a no-code Android app that enables researchers to collect information displayed on screen through simple demonstrations of target data. Crepe utilizes a novel Graph Query technique which augments the structures of mobile UI screens to support flexible identification, location, and collection of specific data pieces. The tool emphasizes participants' privacy and agency by providing full transparency over collected data and allowing easy opt-out. We designed and built Crepe for research purposes only and in scenarios where researchers obtain explicit consent from participants. Code for Crepe will be open-sourced to support future academic research data collection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Crepe：一种使用图查询的移动屏幕数据收集工具
由于数据访问受限和技术障碍，学术研究人员收集移动数据集仍具挑战性。商业机构通常拥有移动数据的独家访问权，导致“数据垄断”，限制了学术研究的独立性。现有开源移动数据收集框架主要关注移动传感数据，而非屏幕内容，而屏幕内容对多种研究至关重要。我们提出Crepe，一款无需代码的Android应用，研究人员只需简单演示目标数据即可收集屏幕显示信息。Crepe采用新颖的图查询技术，通过增强移动UI屏幕结构，支持灵活识别、定位和收集特定数据片段。该工具强调参与者隐私与自主权，提供数据收集的完全透明性，并允许轻松退出。Crepe仅用于研究目的，且仅在研究人员获得参与者明确同意的场景下使用。Crepe代码将开源，以支持未来学术研究数据收集。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty academic researchers face in accessing mobile screen data due to commercial data monopolies and the lack of tools for collecting such content, Crepe introduces a no-code Android app that enables researchers to gather screen-displayed information through simple demonstrations. It employs a novel Graph Query technique to model and extract specific UI elements from mobile interfaces, allowing flexible and precise data collection without requiring coding expertise. Experimental results show that Crepe successfully collects targeted screen data with high accuracy and minimal participant burden, while maintaining full transparency and opt-out capability to preserve user privacy and agency.
为解决学术研究人员因商业数据垄断和缺乏工具而难以获取移动端屏幕数据的问题，Crepe推出了一款无需代码的安卓应用，通过用户简单演示即可采集屏幕内容。它采用新颖的图查询技术，通过增强UI结构实现对目标数据的灵活识别与采集。实验结果表明，Crepe能高精度采集多样化的屏幕数据，且对用户负担小，同时保障数据透明与退出机制，有效保护用户隐私。
Locating acts of mechanistic reasoning in student team conversations with mechanistic machine learning
Authors: Kaitlin Gili, Mainak Nistala, Kristen Wendell, Michael C. Hughes
First: 2026-04-23T17:13:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:13:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
STEM education researchers are often interested in identifying moments of students' mechanistic reasoning for deeper analysis, but have limited capacity to search through many team conversation transcripts to find segments with a high concentration of such reasoning. We offer a solution in the form of an interpretable machine learning model that outputs time-varying probabilities that individual students are engaging in acts of mechanistic reasoning, leveraging evidence from their own utterances as well as contributions from the rest of the group. Using the toolkit of intentionally-designed probabilistic models, we introduce a specific inductive bias that steers the probabilistic dynamics toward desired, domain-aligned behavior. Experiments compare trained models with and without the inductive bias components, investigating whether their presence improves the desired model behavior on transcripts involving never-before-seen students and a novel discussion context. Our results show that the inductive bias improves generalization -- supporting the claim that interpretability is built into the model for this task rather than imposed post hoc. We conclude with practical recommendations for STEM education researchers seeking to adopt the tool and for ML researchers aiming to extend the model's design. Overall, we hope this work encourages the development of mechanistically interpretable models that are understandable and controllable for both end users and model designers in STEM education research.

标题：在学生团队关于机制性机器学习的对话中定位机制性推理行为

Summary / 总结

The model incorporates an intentional inductive bias to guide its probabilistic dynamics toward domain-aligned behavior. Fake or Real, Can Robots Tell? Evaluating VLM Robustness to Domain Shift in Single-View Robotic Scene Understanding
Authors: Federico Tavella, Amber Drinkwater, Angelo Cangelosi
First: 2025-06-24T12:45:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:05:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic scene understanding increasingly relies on Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to generate natural language descriptions of the environment. In this work, we systematically evaluate single-view object captioning for tabletop scenes captured by a robotic manipulator, introducing a controlled physical domain shift that contrasts real-world tools with geometrically similar 3D-printed counterparts that differ in texture, colour, and material. We benchmark a suite of state-of-the-art, locally deployable VLMs across multiple metrics to assess semantic alignment and factual grounding. Our results demonstrate that while VLMs describe common real-world objects effectively, performance degrades markedly on 3D-printed items despite their structurally familiar forms. We further expose critical vulnerabilities in standard evaluation metrics, showing that some fail to detect domain shifts entirely or reward fluent but factually incorrect captions. These findings highlight the limitations of deploying foundation models for embodied agents and the need for more robust architectures and evaluation protocols in physical robotic applications.

标题：真假难辨，机器人能分辨吗？评估VLM在单视角机器人场景理解中的领域偏移鲁棒性
机器人场景理解日益依赖视觉-语言模型（VLM）生成环境的自然语言描述。本研究系统评估了由机械臂拍摄的桌面场景中单视角物体的图像字幕生成，引入受控的物理领域偏移，对比真实世界工具与几何相似但纹理、颜色和材质不同的3D打印替代品。我们对多种最先进的、可本地部署的VLM进行基准测试，评估其语义对齐和事实基础。结果表明，尽管VLM能有效描述常见真实物体，但在3D打印物品上表现显著下降，尽管其结构形态熟悉。我们进一步揭示了标准评估指标的关键缺陷，显示部分指标完全无法检测领域偏移，或奖励流畅但事实错误的字幕。这些发现凸显了基础模型在具身智能体部署中的局限性，强调了在物理机器人应用中需要更鲁棒的架构和评估协议。

FAccT-Checked: A Narrative Review of Authority Reconfigurations and Retention in AI-Mediated Journalism
Authors: Stefano Sorrentino, Matilde Barbini, Daniel Gatica-Perez
First: 2026-04-23T17:00:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:00:15+00:00
Comments: ACM FAccT 2026 accepted paper. Due to the arXiv 1920 characters limit the Abstract here is shortened. Refer to the full paper for the entire Abstract
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Building on recent interpretivist approaches, we conduct a critical narrative review across journalism studies, human-computer interaction, and FAccT scholarship, conceptualizing editorial authority as the conjunction of decision rights, epistemic warrant, and responsibility. We provide a comprehensive theoretical framework for addressing how concerns on fairness, accountability and transparency emerge, interact, and persist within AI mediated journalistic practice. We identify and describe two concurrent authority reconfigurations driven by AI adoption. First, an internal migration of authority, in which editorial judgment is progressively deferred to large language models (LLMs) embedded within newsroom workflows. This migration occurs not through explicit policy decisions, but through interactional, cognitive, and organizational mechanisms that legitimize AI generated outputs while obscuring responsibility and weakening individual and professional agency. Second, we analyze an external migration of authority, whereby decision making power shifts from news organizations toward platforms, vendors, and infrastructural providers that supply AI systems and distribution channels, exacerbating existing power asymmetries within the media ecosystem. Unaddressed, these reconfigurations risk rendering fairness hard to maintain, accountability difficult to assign and transparency performative. We examine participatory approaches to AI design and deployment in journalism as potential mechanisms for retaining or reclaiming editorial authority. We critically assess both their promise and their structural limitations, highlighting how participation can either meaningfully redistribute authority or function as a tokenistic practice that leaves underlying power relations intact.

标题：FAccT验证：人工智能媒介新闻业中权威重构与保留的叙事综述
基于近期解释性方法，我们对新闻学、人机交互与FAccT研究领域进行批判性叙事综述，将编辑权威界定为决策权、知识正当性与责任的结合。我们构建了全面的理论框架，探讨公平、问责与透明度关切如何在人工智能媒介新闻实践中产生、互动并持续存在。我们识别并描述了由AI采用驱动的两种并行权威重构：其一，内部权威迁移，即编辑判断逐步委任给嵌入新闻工作流程中的大型语言模型（LLMs），此迁移非通过明确政策，而是通过互动、认知与组织机制实现，使AI生成内容合法化，同时模糊责任、削弱个体与职业自主性；其二，外部权威迁移，即决策权从新闻机构向提供AI系统与分发渠道的平台、供应商及基础设施提供商转移，加剧媒体生态中既有的权力不对称。若不加以应对，这些重构将使公平难以维持、问责难以落实、透明度沦为表演。我们考察了新闻业中参与式AI设计与部署作为保留或重获编辑权威的潜在机制，并批判性评估其潜力与结构性局限，指出参与既能实质性重分配权力，也可能沦为象征性实践，维持原有权力关系。

It identifies two concurrent authority reconfigurations: an internal migration where editorial judgment is increasingly delegated to large language models within newsrooms through subtle, non-policy-driven mechanisms that obscure accountability; and an external migration where decision-making power shifts to platforms and vendors, deepening media ecosystem power imbalances. The study finds these shifts threaten fairness, accountability, and transparency unless countered by participatory design approaches, which may either meaningfully redistribute authority or remain superficial, preserving existing power structures.

Hán Dān Xué Bù (Mimicry) or Qīng Chū Yú Lán (Mastery)? A Cognitive Perspective on Reasoning Distillation in Large Language Models
Authors: Yueqing Hu, Xinyang Peng, Shuting Peng, Hanqi Wang, Tianhong Wang
First: 2026-01-08T15:27:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T17:00:06+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent Large Reasoning Models trained via reinforcement learning exhibit a "natural" alignment with human cognitive costs. However, we show that the prevailing paradigm of reasoning distillation -- training student models to mimic these traces via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) -- fails to transmit this cognitive structure. Testing the "Hán Dān Xué Bù" (Superficial Mimicry) hypothesis across 14 models, we find that distillation induces a "Functional Alignment Collapse": while teacher models mirror human difficulty scaling ($\bar{r}=0.64$), distilled students significantly degrade this alignment ($\bar{r}=0.34$), often underperforming their own pre-distillation baselines ("Negative Transfer"). Our analysis suggests that SFT induces a "Cargo Cult" effect, where students ritualistically replicate the linguistic form of reasoning (verbosity) without internalizing the teacher's dynamic resource allocation policy. Consequently, reasoning distillation decouples computational cost from cognitive demand, revealing that human-like cognition is an emergent property of active reinforcement, not passive imitation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：浅层模仿（韩丹学步）还是精通（青出於蓝）？从认知视角看大语言模型推理蒸馏
近期通过强化学习训练的大规模推理模型表现出与人类认知成本的“自然”对齐。然而，我们发现当前主流的推理蒸馏范式——通过监督微调（SFT）训练学生模型模仿这些痕迹——未能传递这种认知结构。在14个模型上检验“韩丹学步”假说时，我们发现蒸馏导致“功能对齐崩溃”：教师模型保持与人类难度缩放的对齐（r̄=0.64），而蒸馏后学生模型显著降低该对齐度（r̄=0.34），常低于其蒸馏前基线（“负迁移”）。我们的分析表明，SFT引发“cargo cult”效应，学生模型仪式化地复制推理的语言形式（冗长性），而未内化教师的动态资源分配策略。因此，推理蒸馏将计算成本与认知需求解耦，揭示类人认知是主动强化的涌现属性，而非被动模仿。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether reasoning distillation in large language models, via supervised fine-tuning, can transfer human-like cognitive efficiency. Across 14 models, they find that distillation causes a 'Functional Alignment Collapse', where student models lose the teacher’s human-like difficulty scaling (from r=0.64 to r=0.34), and often perform worse than their pre-distillation baselines. The authors argue that SFT induces a 'Cargo Cult' effect, where students mimic surface-level reasoning verbosity without learning the teacher’s underlying resource allocation strategy, thus decoupling computational cost from cognitive demand.
该研究探讨了大语言模型推理蒸馏是否能保留类人认知对齐，动机源于强化学习训练模型天然匹配人类认知成本的现象。作者在14个模型上测试“韩丹学步”（表面模仿）假说，发现监督微调（SFT）蒸馏导致“功能对齐崩溃”：教师模型与人类难度匹配度高（r=0.64），而蒸馏学生模型显著下降（r=0.34），且常低于蒸馏前基线。分析表明SFT引发“ cargo cult”效应，学生仅复制表面推理冗余而未内化教师资源分配策略，说明类人认知源于主动强化而非被动模仿。
Replay-buffer engineering for noise-robust quantum circuit optimization
Authors: Akash Kundu, Sebastian Feld
First: 2026-04-23T16:59:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:59:40+00:00
Comments: Comments are warmly welcomed. 9 page main content, 17 page appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep reinforcement learning (RL) for quantum circuit optimization faces three fundamental bottlenecks: replay buffers that ignore the reliability of temporal-difference (TD) targets, curriculum-based architecture search that triggers a full quantum-classical evaluation at every environment step, and the routine discard of noiseless trajectories when retraining under hardware noise. We address all three by treating the replay buffer as a primary algorithmic lever for quantum optimization. We introduce ReaPER$+$, an annealed replay rule that transitions from TD error-driven prioritization early in training to reliability-aware sampling as value estimates mature, achieving $4-32\times$ gains in sample efficiency over fixed PER, ReaPER, and uniform replay while consistently discovering more compact circuits across quantum compilation and QAS benchmarks; validation on LunarLander-v3 confirms the principle is domain-agnostic. Furthermore we eliminate the quantum-classical evaluation bottleneck in curriculum RL by introducing OptCRLQAS which amortizes expensive evaluations over multiple architectural edits, cutting wall-clock time per episode by up to $67.5\%$ on a 12-qubit optimization problem without degrading solution quality. Finally we introduce a lightweight replay-buffer transfer scheme that warm-starts noisy-setting learning by reusing noiseless trajectories, without network-weight transfer or $ε$-greedy pretraining. This reduces steps to chemical accuracy by up to $85-90\%$ and final energy error by up to $90\%$ over from-scratch baselines on 6-, 8-, and 12-qubit molecular tasks. Together, these results establish that experience storage, sampling, and transfer are decisive levers for scalable, noise-robust quantum circuit optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向抗噪量子电路优化的重放缓冲区工程
量子电路优化中的深度强化学习面临三大根本瓶颈：忽略时序差分（TD）目标可靠性的重放缓冲区、每一步环境交互均触发完整量子-经典评估的课程式架构搜索，以及在硬件噪声下重训练时丢弃无噪声轨迹。我们通过将重放缓冲区视为量子优化的核心算法杠杆，解决上述所有问题。我们提出ReaPER+，一种退火式重放规则，训练初期以TD误差驱动优先采样，随着价值估计成熟转为可靠性感知采样，相较固定PER、ReaPER和均匀重放，样本效率提升4-32倍，同时在量子编译与QAS基准测试中持续发现更紧凑的电路；在LunarLander-v3上的验证表明该原则具有领域无关性。此外，我们通过引入OptCRLQAS消除课程强化学习中的量子-经典评估瓶颈，将昂贵评估摊薄至多次架构修改中，使12量子比特优化问题每回合实际运行时间减少高达67.5%，且不降低解的质量。最后，我们提出一种轻量级重放缓冲区迁移方案，通过复用无噪声轨迹实现噪声环境学习的预热，无需网络权重迁移或ε-贪心预训练。该方案在6、8、12量子比特分子任务上，相比从零开始基线，达到化学精度所需步数减少85-90%，最终能量误差降低90%。上述成果共同表明，经验存储、采样与迁移是实现可扩展、抗噪量子电路优化的关键杠杆。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiencies in deep reinforcement learning for quantum circuit optimization, this work addresses three core bottlenecks: unreliable replay buffers, costly curriculum-based evaluations, and wasteful discarding of noiseless trajectories. The authors introduce ReaPER+, a replay buffer strategy that evolves from TD-error prioritization to reliability-aware sampling, boosting sample efficiency by 4–32× over baselines while finding more compact circuits. They eliminate the evaluation bottleneck via OptCRLQAS, which amortizes quantum-classical evaluations across architectural edits, cutting episode time by up to 67.5% without quality loss. Finally, a lightweight replay transfer scheme reuses noiseless trajectories to warm-start noisy training, reducing steps to chemical accuracy by 85–90% and final energy error by up to 90% on molecular tasks.
