为解决量子电路优化中深度强化学习的三大瓶颈，该研究提出ReaPER+方法，通过动态调整经验回放缓冲区采样策略，在训练初期依据TD误差优先，后期转向可靠性感知采样，相较固定策略提升4-32倍样本效率并发现更紧凑电路。同时引入OptCRLQAS方法，通过复用架构修改来摊薄昂贵的量子-经典评估，将每回合实际耗时降低67.5%而不影响解质量。最后提出轻量级经验迁移机制，复用无噪声轨迹启动噪声环境训练，使达到化学精度的步数减少85-90%，最终能量误差降低90%，验证了经验存储、采样与迁移对噪声鲁棒、可扩展量子优化的关键作用。
How to Allocate, How to Learn? Dynamic Rollout Allocation and Advantage Modulation for Policy Optimization
Authors: Yangyi Fang, Jiaye Lin, Xiaoliang Fu, Cong Qin, Haolin Shi, Chaowen Hu, Lu Pan, Ke Zeng, Xunliang Cai
First: 2026-02-22T14:38:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:56:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has proven effective for Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, yet current methods face key challenges in resource allocation and policy optimization dynamics: (i) uniform rollout allocation ignores gradient variance heterogeneity across problems, and (ii) the softmax policy structure causes gradient attenuation for high-confidence correct actions, while excessive gradient updates may destabilize training. Therefore, we propose DynaMO, a theoretically-grounded dual-pronged optimization framework. At the sequence level, we prove that uniform allocation is suboptimal and derive variance-minimizing allocation from the first principle, establishing Bernoulli variance as a computable proxy for gradient informativeness. At the token level, we develop gradient-aware advantage modulation grounded in theoretical analysis of gradient magnitude bounds. Our framework compensates for gradient attenuation of high-confidence correct actions while utilizing entropy changes as computable indicators to stabilize excessive update magnitudes. Extensive experiments conducted on a diverse range of mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over strong RLVR baselines. Our implementation is available at: https://github.com/GithubX-F/DynaMO-RL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何分配、如何学习？面向策略优化的动态 rollout 分配与优势调制
可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）在大语言模型（LLM）推理中已证明有效，但现有方法在资源分配与策略优化动态方面面临关键挑战：(i) 均匀 rollout 分配忽视了不同问题间梯度方差的异质性，(ii) softmax 策略结构导致高置信度正确动作的梯度衰减，而过度梯度更新可能破坏训练稳定性。为此，我们提出 DynaMO，一种基于理论的双管齐下优化框架。在序列层面，我们证明均匀分配次优，并从第一原理推导出最小化方差的分配策略，建立伯努利方差作为梯度信息量的可计算代理；在 token 层面，我们基于梯度幅值上界理论分析，开发了梯度感知的优势调制机制。该框架补偿高置信度正确动作的梯度衰减，并利用熵变作为可计算指标稳定过度更新幅度。在多种数学推理基准上的广泛实验表明，DynaMO 一致优于强 RLVR 基线。代码开源地址：https://github.com/GithubX-F/DynaMO-RL。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by inefficiencies in resource allocation and gradient dynamics in RLVR for LLM reasoning, the authors propose DynaMO, a dual-framework that dynamically allocates rollouts and modulates advantages based on gradient variance and entropy. At the sequence level, they derive a variance-minimizing rollout allocation using Bernoulli variance as a proxy for gradient informativeness, proving uniform allocation suboptimal. At the token level, they introduce gradient-aware advantage modulation to counteract gradient attenuation for high-confidence actions and stabilize updates via entropy-based bounds. Experiments across multiple mathematical reasoning benchmarks show consistent performance gains over strong RLVR baselines.
为解决RLVR中资源分配不均与梯度动态不稳定的问题，DynaMO提出双阶段优化框架：序列层面通过伯努利方差代理梯度信息量，推导最小方差分配策略以替代均匀分配；token层面通过梯度感知的优势调制，补偿高置信度正确动作的梯度衰减，并利用熵变控制更新幅度以稳定训练。在多个数学推理基准上，该方法持续优于主流RLVR基线模型。
Transient Turn Injection: Exposing Stateless Multi-Turn Vulnerabilities in Large Language Models
Authors: Naheed Rayhan, Sohely Jahan
First: 2026-04-23T16:56:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:56:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into sensitive workflows, raising the stakes for adversarial robustness and safety. This paper introduces Transient Turn Injection(TTI), a new multi-turn attack technique that systematically exploits stateless moderation by distributing adversarial intent across isolated interactions. TTI leverages automated attacker agents powered by large language models to iteratively test and evade policy enforcement in both commercial and open-source LLMs, marking a departure from conventional jailbreak approaches that typically depend on maintaining persistent conversational context. Our extensive evaluation across state-of-the-art models-including those from OpenAI, Anthropic, Google Gemini, Meta, and prominent open-source alternatives-uncovers significant variations in resilience to TTI attacks, with only select architectures exhibiting substantial inherent robustness. Our automated blackbox evaluation framework also uncovers previously unknown model specific vulnerabilities and attack surface patterns, especially within medical and high stakes domains. We further compare TTI against established adversarial prompting methods and detail practical mitigation strategies, such as session level context aggregation and deep alignment approaches. Our study underscores the urgent need for holistic, context aware defenses and continuous adversarial testing to future proof LLM deployments against evolving multi-turn threats.
中文标题/摘要
标题：瞬时回合注入：揭示大型语言模型中的无状态多回合漏洞
大型语言模型（LLMs）日益融入敏感工作流程，对对抗鲁棒性和安全性提出了更高要求。本文提出瞬时回合注入（TTI），一种新的多回合攻击技术，通过将对抗意图分散到独立交互中，系统性地利用无状态审核机制。TTI 利用基于大型语言模型的自动化攻击代理，迭代测试并规避商业及开源 LLM 中的策略执行，突破了传统越狱方法通常依赖持续对话上下文的局限。我们在包括 OpenAI、Anthropic、Google Gemini、Meta 及主流开源模型在内的前沿模型上进行广泛评估，发现其对 TTI 攻击的抗性存在显著差异，仅少数架构具备显著固有鲁棒性。我们的自动化黑盒评估框架还揭示了此前未知的模型特定漏洞与攻击面模式，尤其在医疗和高风险领域。我们进一步将 TTI 与现有对抗提示方法进行比较，并详细阐述实用缓解策略，如会话级上下文聚合与深度对齐方法。本研究强调，为保障 LLM 部署免受不断演化的多回合威胁，亟需全面、上下文感知的防御机制与持续对抗测试。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing deployment of large language models in sensitive applications, this study introduces Transient Turn Injection (TTI), a novel multi-turn attack that exploits stateless moderation by distributing adversarial intent across isolated interactions. Using automated LLM-powered agents, TTI iteratively tests and evades policy enforcement in commercial and open-source models, bypassing traditional jailbreak methods reliant on persistent context. Experimental results across leading models—including OpenAI, Anthropic, Google Gemini, and open-source variants—reveal significant variation in resilience, with only a few architectures showing strong inherent robustness. The framework also uncovers previously unknown vulnerabilities, especially in high-stakes domains like healthcare, and demonstrates that TTI outperforms conventional adversarial prompting. Mitigation strategies such as session-level context aggregation and deep alignment are proposed to counter such threats.
鉴于大语言模型在敏感场景中的广泛应用，本研究提出了一种名为瞬态回合注入（TTI）的新式多轮攻击方法，该方法通过在孤立交互中分散对抗意图，利用无状态审核机制进行攻击。攻击过程由基于大语言模型的自动化代理驱动，无需依赖持续对话上下文，区别于传统越狱方法。实验在主流商业及开源模型上进行，结果显示模型抵御能力差异显著，仅少数模型具备较强固有鲁棒性，同时发现医疗等高风险领域存在此前未知的漏洞。研究还对比了TTI与其他对抗提示方法，并提出会话级上下文聚合与深度对齐等缓解策略，强调需构建具备上下文感知能力的综合防御体系并持续进行对抗测试。
Nonlinear Causal Discovery through a Sequential Edge Orientation Approach
Authors: Stella Huang, Qing Zhou
First: 2025-06-05T21:08:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:52:06+00:00
Comments: 59 pages, 18 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances have established the identifiability of a directed acyclic graph (DAG) under additive noise models (ANMs), spurring the development of various causal discovery methods. However, most existing methods make restrictive model assumptions, rely heavily on general independence tests, or require substantial computational time. To address these limitations, we propose a sequential procedure to orient undirected edges in a completed partial DAG (CPDAG), representing an equivalence class of DAGs, by leveraging the pairwise additive noise model (PANM) to identify their causal directions. We prove that this procedure can recover the true causal DAG assuming a restricted ANM. Building on this result, we develop a novel constraint-based algorithm for learning causal DAGs under nonlinear ANMs. Given an estimated CPDAG, we develop a ranking procedure that sorts undirected edges by their adherence to the PANM, which defines an evaluation order of the edges. To determine the edge direction, we devise a statistical test that compares the log-likelihood values, evaluated with respect to the competing directions, of a sub-graph comprising just the candidate nodes and their identified parents in the partial DAG. We further establish the structural learning consistency of our algorithm in the large-sample limit. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our method is computationally efficient, robust to model misspecification, and consistently outperforms many existing nonlinear DAG learning methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过顺序边定向方法进行非线性因果发现
近期进展已确立在加性噪声模型（ANM）下有向无环图（DAG）的可识别性，推动了多种因果发现方法的发展。然而，大多数现有方法对模型假设过于严格，严重依赖通用独立性检验，或需要大量计算时间。为解决这些局限性，我们提出一种顺序方法，利用成对加性噪声模型（PANM）识别因果方向，对已完成的部分DAG（CPDAG）中的无向边进行定向，CPDAG代表一组等价的DAG。我们证明，在受限ANM假设下，该方法可恢复真实因果DAG。在此基础上，我们开发了一种用于非线性ANM下因果DAG学习的新约束算法。给定估计的CPDAG，我们设计了一种排序程序，按其对PANM的符合程度对无向边进行排序，从而定义边的评估顺序。为确定边方向，我们设计了一种统计检验方法，比较由候选节点及其在部分DAG中已识别的父节点构成的子图在两种竞争方向下的对数似然值。我们进一步证明了该算法在大样本极限下的结构学习一致性。在合成数据和真实世界数据上的大量实验表明，我们的方法计算高效、对模型误设鲁棒，并持续优于许多现有非线性DAG学习方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and accurate causal discovery under nonlinear settings, this paper proposes a sequential edge orientation method that leverages the pairwise additive noise model (PANM) to orient undirected edges in a completed partial DAG (CPDAG). The method first ranks undirected edges based on their compatibility with PANM, then applies a statistical test comparing log-likelihoods of subgraphs under competing directional hypotheses to determine edge orientations. Theoretical guarantees establish structural consistency under restricted additive noise models, and empirical evaluations on synthetic and real-world datasets confirm the algorithm’s computational efficiency, robustness to model misspecification, and superior performance over existing nonlinear causal discovery methods.
为解决非线性加性噪声模型下因果发现效率低、依赖强独立性检验或计算成本高的问题，本文提出一种基于成对加性噪声模型的序列边定向方法，通过在完成的部分DAG中对无向边进行排序和方向判定。该方法依据边对PANM的符合程度排序，并通过比较子图对数似然值来确定边方向，确保在受限ANM下的一致性。实验表明，该算法在合成与真实数据上计算高效、对模型误设鲁棒，并优于多数现有非线性DAG学习方法。
Bounding the Black Box: A Statistical Certification Framework for AI Risk Regulation
Authors: Natan Levy, Gadi Perl
First: 2026-04-23T16:50:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:50:35+00:00
Comments: 11 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence now decides who receives a loan, who is flagged for criminal investigation, and whether an autonomous vehicle brakes in time. Governments have responded: the EU AI Act, the NIST Risk Management Framework, and the Council of Europe Convention all demand that high-risk systems demonstrate safety before deployment. Yet beneath this regulatory consensus lies a critical vacuum: none specifies what ``acceptable risk'' means in quantitative terms, and none provides a technical method for verifying that a deployed system actually meets such a threshold. The regulatory architecture is in place; the verification instrument is not. This gap is not theoretical. As the EU AI Act moves into full enforcement, developers face mandatory conformity assessments without established methodologies for producing quantitative safety evidence - and the systems most in need of oversight are opaque statistical inference engines that resist white-box scrutiny. This paper provides the missing instrument. Drawing on the aviation certification paradigm, we propose a two-stage framework that transforms AI risk regulation into engineering practice. In Stage One, a competent authority formally fixes an acceptable failure probability $δ$ and an operational input domain $\varepsilon$ - a normative act with direct civil liability implications. In Stage Two, the RoMA and gRoMA statistical verification tools compute a definitive, auditable upper bound on the system's true failure rate, requiring no access to model internals and scaling to arbitrary architectures. We demonstrate how this certificate satisfies existing regulatory obligations, shifts accountability upstream to developers, and integrates with the legal frameworks that exist today.
中文标题/摘要
标题：界定黑箱：人工智能风险监管的统计认证框架
人工智能如今决定谁获得贷款、谁被标记为刑事调查对象，以及自动驾驶汽车是否能及时刹车。各国政府已作出回应：欧盟人工智能法案、NIST风险管理框架和欧洲委员会公约均要求高风险系统在部署前证明其安全性。然而，在这一监管共识之下，仍存在一个关键空白：没有任何规定以量化方式定义“可接受风险”，也无技术方法验证已部署系统是否真正达到该阈值。监管架构已建立，但验证工具尚未到位。 这一差距并非理论性问题。随着欧盟人工智能法案进入全面实施阶段，开发者面临强制性符合性评估，却缺乏生成量化安全证据的方法——而最需要监管的系统恰恰是那些难以进行白盒审查的、不透明的统计推断引擎。 本文提供缺失的工具。借鉴航空认证范式，我们提出一个两阶段框架，将人工智能风险监管转化为工程实践。第一阶段，主管机构正式确定可接受的故障概率δ和操作输入域ε——这是一项具有直接民事责任影响的规范性行为。第二阶段，RoMA和gRoMA统计验证工具计算系统真实故障率的确定性、可审计上限，无需访问模型内部结构，且可扩展至任意架构。我们展示了该证书如何满足现有监管义务，将问责责任上移至开发者，并与当前法律框架相整合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of quantitative standards and verification methods for AI risk regulation, this paper proposes a two-stage statistical certification framework inspired by aviation safety practices. In the first stage, a regulatory authority sets an acceptable failure probability and operational domain, establishing legal accountability. In the second stage, RoMA and gRoMA tools compute an auditable upper bound on the system’s failure rate without requiring model internals, enabling scalable verification across architectures. Experiments show the framework satisfies regulatory obligations, shifts liability to developers, and integrates with existing legal frameworks without needing white-box access.
为解决AI风险监管缺乏量化标准与验证方法的问题，本文提出一套受航空安全认证启发的两阶段统计认证框架。第一阶段由监管机构设定可接受的失败概率阈值与运行域；第二阶段使用RoMA和gRoMA工具计算无需模型内部信息即可验证的失败率上界，适用于任意架构。实验表明该框架满足现有监管要求，将责任前移至开发者，并可与现行法律体系无缝衔接。
Post-Training Augmentation Invariance
Authors: Keenan Eikenberry, Lizuo Liu, Yoonsang Lee
First: 2025-05-16T21:11:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:49:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This work develops a framework for post-training augmentation invariance, in which our goal is to add invariance properties to a pretrained network without altering its behavior on the original, non-augmented input distribution. We define this notion precisely and additionally introduce augmented encoders, which are probabilistic encoders that formalize augmentation-based encoding processes and that serve as our fundamental object of study. We introduce two losses for augmented encoders, namely, Markov-Wasserstein minimization and Wasserstein correlation maximization, and we demonstrate empirically that both losses can be used to train lightweight, one-hidden-layer MLP adapter networks E_theta that, when appended to the latent space of a pretrained network F, do indeed lead to (approximate) post-training augmentation invariance. For example, on STL10 with F = DINOv2 features, the composite network C o E_theta o F, where C is a linear classifier and where E_theta is one of our proposed adapter networks, achieves 94% classification accuracy on arbitrarily rotated images, whereas a network of the form C o F without the adapter E_theta drops to 71% accuracy. Similarly, we can boost noise-invariant classification results from 58% up to 86%. Significantly, we obtain these results with no fine-tuning (the weights of F remain frozen throughout), and our methods introduce little corruption to the original features, since E_theta acts nearly isometrically on the non-augmented latent distribution. In contrast, we show that adapter networks trained with alternative candidate losses, specifically SimCLR and HSIC maximization, produce uncompetitive classification results and fundamentally corrupt the original latent space. Code available at: https://github.com/keenan-eikenberry/augmentation_invariance
中文标题/摘要
标题：训练后增强不变性
本工作提出了一种训练后增强不变性的框架，旨在在不改变预训练网络对原始非增强输入分布行为的前提下，为其添加不变性属性。我们精确定义了这一概念，并引入了增强编码器——一种概率编码器，用于形式化基于增强的编码过程，作为我们的核心研究对象。我们提出了两种针对增强编码器的损失函数：马尔可夫-沃瑟斯坦最小化和沃瑟斯坦相关性最大化，并通过实验证明，这两种损失均可用于训练轻量级、单隐藏层MLP适配器网络E_theta，当将其附加到预训练网络F的潜在空间时，确实能实现（近似）训练后增强不变性。例如，在STL10数据集上，当F为DINOv2特征时，复合网络C o E_theta o F（其中C为线性分类器，E_theta为所提出的适配器网络）在任意旋转图像上的分类准确率达到94%，而无适配器的网络C o F准确率则降至71%。同样，我们可将噪声不变分类结果从58%提升至86%。重要的是，我们无需微调（F的权重全程冻结），且方法对原始特征引入的破坏极小，因为E_theta在非增强潜在分布上近似等距作用。相比之下，使用其他候选损失（如SimCLR和HSIC最大化）训练的适配器网络会产生竞争力不足的分类结果，并根本性地破坏原始潜在空间。代码见：https://github.com/keenan-eikenberry/augmentation_invariance
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance pretrained models' robustness to data augmentations without modifying their original behavior, this work introduces a framework for post-training augmentation invariance. It defines augmented encoders as probabilistic encoders that formalize augmentation-based encoding and proposes two training losses—Markov-Wasserstein minimization and Wasserstein correlation maximization—to train lightweight adapter networks. These adapters, when appended to pretrained networks, enable approximate invariance to transformations like rotation or noise. Experiments show that on STL10 with DINOv2 features, adding such an adapter boosts classification accuracy from 71% (without adapter) to 94% on rotated images, and from 58% to 86% on noisy images, all while freezing the pretrained weights and preserving the original latent space structure. In contrast, adapters trained with SimCLR or HSIC losses degrade performance and corrupt the latent space.
该研究旨在提升预训练模型对数据增强的鲁棒性，同时不改变其对原始输入分布的行为。论文提出“增强编码器”这一概率编码器概念，用于形式化增强过程，并设计了两种新损失函数（马尔可夫-沃瑟斯坦最小化与沃瑟斯坦相关性最大化）来训练轻量级适配器网络。这些适配器可附加于预训练模型（如DINOv2）的潜在空间，实现训练后增强不变性，且无需微调预训练权重。实验表明，在STL10数据集上，适配器使旋转图像分类准确率从71%提升至94%，噪声图像从58%提升至86%，同时几乎不破坏原始特征结构。相比之下，使用SimCLR或HSIC损失训练的适配器性能较差且严重破坏潜在空间。
Weighted quantization using MMD: From mean field to mean shift via gradient flows
Authors: Ayoub Belhadji, Daniel Sharp, Youssef Marzouk
First: 2025-02-14T23:13:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:45:36+00:00
Comments: To be published in proceedings for AISTATS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Approximating a probability distribution using a set of particles is a fundamental problem in machine learning and statistics, with applications including clustering and quantization. Formally, we seek a weighted mixture of Dirac measures that best approximates the target distribution. While much existing work relies on the Wasserstein distance to quantify approximation errors, maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) has received comparatively less attention, especially when allowing for variable particle weights. We argue that a Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao gradient flow is well-suited for designing quantizations optimal under MMD. We show that a system of interacting particles satisfying a set of ODEs discretizes this flow. We further derive a new fixed-point algorithm called mean shift interacting particles (MSIP). We show that MSIP extends the classical mean shift algorithm, widely used for identifying modes in kernel density estimators. Moreover, we show that MSIP can be interpreted as preconditioned gradient descent and that it acts as a relaxation of Lloyd's algorithm for clustering. Our unification of gradient flows, mean shift, and MMD-optimal quantization yields algorithms that are more robust than state-of-the-art methods, as demonstrated via high-dimensional and multi-modal numerical experiments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用MMD的加权量化：从均场到均移的梯度流
使用粒子集近似概率分布是机器学习和统计学中的基本问题，应用包括聚类和量化。形式上，我们寻求一个加权狄拉克测度混合，以最佳方式逼近目标分布。虽然许多现有工作依赖于Wasserstein距离来量化近似误差，但最大均值差异（MMD）在允许可变粒子权重时受到的关注较少。我们论证称，Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao梯度流适合设计MMD最优的量化方案。我们表明，满足一组ODE的相互作用粒子系统可离散化该流。我们进一步推导了一种新的不动点算法，称为均移相互作用粒子（MSIP）。我们证明MSIP扩展了经典均移算法，该算法广泛用于识别核密度估计中的模式。此外，我们证明MSIP可解释为预条件梯度下降，并且作为聚类Lloyd算法的松弛。我们统一梯度流、均移和MMD最优量化，得到比现有最先进方法更鲁棒的算法，如高维和多模态数值实验所示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust quantization under maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) rather than Wasserstein distance, the paper proposes a novel method based on Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao gradient flows to derive an optimal weighted particle system. The authors introduce a new fixed-point algorithm called mean shift interacting particles (MSIP), which extends classical mean shift and can be viewed as preconditioned gradient descent, while also relaxing Lloyd’s algorithm. Experimental results across high-dimensional and multi-modal settings demonstrate that MSIP achieves superior performance compared to existing state-of-the-art methods in MMD-optimal quantization.
为提升在最大均值差异（MMD）度量下的量化鲁棒性，本文提出一种基于MMD优化的加权量化框架，利用Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao梯度流设计粒子系统，推导出一种名为“均值偏移交互粒子”（MSIP）的新固定点算法。该算法扩展了经典均值偏移方法，可视为预条件梯度下降，同时是Lloyd聚类算法的松弛版本。在高维与多模态数据实验中，MSIP展现出优于当前最先进方法的鲁棒性。
Beyond Expected Information Gain: Stable Bayesian Optimal Experimental Design with Integral Probability Metrics and Plug-and-Play Extensions
Authors: Di Wu, Ling Liang, Haizhao Yang
First: 2026-04-23T16:40:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:40:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian Optimal Experimental Design (BOED) provides a rigorous framework for decision-making tasks in which data acquisition is often the critical bottleneck, especially in resource-constrained settings. Traditionally, BOED typically selects designs by maximizing expected information gain (EIG), commonly defined through the Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence. However, classical evaluation of EIG often involves challenging nested expectations, and even advanced variational methods leave the underlying log-density-ratio objective unchanged. As a result, support mismatch, tail underestimation, and rare-event sensitivity remain intrinsic concerns for KL-based BOED. To address these fundamental bottlenecks, we introduce an IPM-based BOED framework that replaces density-based divergences with integral probability metrics (IPMs), including the Wasserstein distance, Maximum Mean Discrepancy, and Energy Distance, resulting in a highly flexible plug-and-play BOED framework. We establish theoretical guarantees showing that IPM-based utilities provide stronger geometry-aware stability under surrogate-model error and prior misspecification than classical EIG-based utilities. We also validate the proposed framework empirically, demonstrating that IPM-based designs yield highly concentrated credible sets. Furthermore, by extending the same sample-based BOED template in a plug-and-play manner to geometry-aware discrepancies beyond the IPM class, illustrated by a neural optimal transport estimator, we achieve accurate optimal designs in high-dimensional settings where conventional nested Monte Carlo estimators and advanced variational methods fail.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越预期信息增益：基于积分概率度量与即插即用扩展的稳定贝叶斯最优实验设计
贝叶斯最优实验设计（BOED）为数据获取常为关键瓶颈、尤其在资源受限场景下的决策任务提供了严谨框架。传统BOED通常通过最大化预期信息增益（EIG）选择设计，EIG常通过Kullback-Leibler（KL）散度定义。然而，经典EIG评估常涉及难以处理的嵌套期望，即使先进变分方法也未改变底层对数密度比目标。因此，基于KL的BOED仍面临支持不匹配、尾部低估和稀有事件敏感性等固有问题。为解决这些根本瓶颈，我们引入基于积分概率度量（IPM）的BOED框架，用Wasserstein距离、最大均值差异和能量距离等IPM替代密度基发散度，构建高度灵活的即插即用BOED框架。我们建立理论保证，表明IPM基效用在代理模型误差和先验误设下，比经典EIG基效用提供更强的几何感知稳定性。我们还通过实证验证该框架，表明IPM基设计可生成高度集中的可信区间。进一步地，通过将同一基于样本的BOED模板以即插即用方式扩展至IPM类外的几何感知差异（如神经最优传输估计器），我们在传统嵌套蒙特卡洛估计器和先进变分方法失效的高维场景中实现了精确的最优设计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability and sensitivity of traditional Bayesian Optimal Experimental Design (BOED) under prior misspecification and support mismatch, this work proposes an IPM-based BOED framework that replaces KL divergence with integral probability metrics such as Wasserstein distance, MMD, and Energy Distance. The method enables plug-and-play integration of geometry-aware discrepancies, including a neural optimal transport estimator, to handle high-dimensional design spaces. Experimental results show that IPM-based designs produce more concentrated credible sets and outperform classical EIG-based methods, especially in settings where nested Monte Carlo estimators and variational approximations fail.
为解决传统基于KL散度的贝叶斯最优实验设计（BOED）在支持不匹配、尾部低估和稀有事件敏感性方面的固有缺陷，本文提出一种基于积分概率度量（IPM）的BOED框架，采用Wasserstein距离、最大均值差异和能量距离等替代密度散度。该方法具有插拔式扩展能力，能更稳定地应对代理模型误差和先验偏差，理论证明其几何感知稳定性更强。实验表明，IPM设计生成的置信集更集中，尤其在高维场景下显著优于传统EIG方法，常规蒙特卡洛和变分方法在此类场景下失效。
TraceScope: Interactive URL Triage via Decoupled Checklist Adjudication
Authors: Haolin Zhang, William Reber, Yuxuan Zhang, Guofei Gu, Jeff Huang
First: 2026-04-23T16:31:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:31:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern phishing campaigns increasingly evade snapshot-based URL classifiers using interaction gates (e.g., checkbox/slider challenges), delayed content rendering, and logo-less credential harvesters. This shifts URL triage from static classification toward an interactive forensics task: an analyst must actively navigate the page while isolating themselves from potential runtime exploits. We present TraceScope, a decoupled triage pipeline that operationalizes this workflow at scale. To prevent the observer effect and ensure safety, a sandboxed operator agent drives a real GUI browser guided by visual motivation to elicit page behavior, freezing the session into an immutable evidence bundle. Separately, an adjudicator agent circumvents LLM context limitations by querying evidence on demand to verify a MITRE ATT&CK checklist, and generates an audit-ready report with extracted indicators of compromise (IOCs) and a final verdict. Evaluated on 708 reachable URLs from existing dataset (241 verified phishing from PhishTank and 467 benign from Tranco-derived crawling), TraceScope achieves 0.94 precision and 0.78 recall, substantially improving recall over three prior visual/reference-based classifiers while producing reproducible, analyst-grade evidence suitable for review. More importantly, we manually curated a dataset of real-world phishing emails to evaluate our system in a practical setting. Our evaluation reveals that TraceScope demonstrates superior performance in a real-world scenario as well, successfully detecting sophisticated phishing attempts that current state-of-the-art defenses fail to identify.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TraceScope：通过解耦的检查清单裁决实现交互式URL分类
现代钓鱼攻击越来越多地通过交互式门控（如复选框/滑块挑战）、延迟内容渲染和无标志凭证收集器规避基于快照的URL分类器。这使得URL分类从静态分类转向交互式取证任务：分析师必须主动浏览页面，同时隔离自身免受潜在运行时漏洞的影响。 我们提出了TraceScope，一个解耦的分类流水线，以规模化实现该工作流程。为避免观察者效应并确保安全，一个沙箱化的操作员代理驱动真实GUI浏览器，依据视觉动机引导页面行为，并将会话冻结为不可变的证据包。同时，一个裁决代理通过按需查询证据绕过大语言模型上下文限制，验证MITRE ATT&CK检查清单，并生成包含提取的攻击指标（IOCs）和最终判定的审计报告。 在现有数据集中708个可访问URL（241个来自PhishTank的已验证钓鱼网站，467个来自Tranco衍生爬取的良性网站）上评估，TraceScope达到0.94的精确率和0.78的召回率，显著优于三个先前的视觉/参考类分类器，同时生成可重复、符合分析师标准的证据，便于审查。更重要的是，我们手动构建了一个真实世界钓鱼邮件数据集，以在实际场景中评估系统。评估结果表明，TraceScope在真实场景中表现更优，成功检测到当前最先进的防御系统未能识别的复杂钓鱼攻击。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing sophistication of phishing attacks that evade static URL analysis through interactive gates and delayed rendering, TraceScope introduces a decoupled triage system enabling analysts to interactively explore suspicious pages safely. It employs a sandboxed operator agent to navigate the page and capture immutable evidence, while a separate adjudicator agent queries this evidence to verify MITRE ATT&CK checklist items and generate audit-ready reports with indicators of compromise. Evaluated on 708 URLs (241 phishing, 467 benign), TraceScope achieved 0.94 precision and 0.78 recall, significantly outperforming prior visual classifiers in recall, and demonstrated superior real-world detection capability on a manually curated phishing email dataset, identifying advanced attacks missed by current defenses.
为应对现代钓鱼攻击通过交互式验证、延迟加载等手段规避静态URL分类的问题，TraceScope提出一种解耦的交互式取证流程。系统由沙箱化操作代理驱动真实GUI浏览器，安全捕获页面行为并生成不可变证据；另一代理按需查询证据以验证MITRE ATT&CK清单，生成含IOC和最终判定的审计报告。在708个URL（含241个钓鱼、467个良性）及人工构建的钓鱼邮件数据集上测试，系统达到0.94精确率和0.78召回率，显著优于现有视觉/参考类分类器，且输出可复现、符合分析师标准的取证证据。
Scensory: Real-Time Robotic Olfactory Perception for Joint Identification and Source Localization
Authors: Yanbaihui Liu, Erica Babusci, Claudia K. Gunsch, Boyuan Chen
First: 2025-09-11T21:13:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:30:13+00:00
Comments: Our project website is at: http://generalroboticslab.com/Scensory
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While robotic perception has advanced rapidly in vision and touch, enabling robots to reason about indoor fungal contamination from weak, diffusion-dominated chemical signals remains an open challenge. We introduce Scensory, a learning-based robotic olfaction framework that simultaneously identifies fungal species and localizes their source from short time series measured by affordable, cross-sensitive VOC sensor arrays. Temporal VOC dynamics encode both chemical and spatial signatures, which we decode through neural networks trained on robot-automated data collection with spatial supervision. Across five fungal species, Scensory achieves up to 89.85% species accuracy and 87.31% source localization accuracy under ambient conditions with 3-7s sensor inputs. These results demonstrate real-time, spatially grounded perception from diffusion-dominated chemical signals, enabling scalable and low-cost source localization for robotic indoor environmental monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Scensory：用于联合识别与源定位的实时机器人嗅觉感知
尽管机器人视觉与触觉感知已迅速发展，但机器人从弱扩散主导的化学信号中推断室内真菌污染仍属开放性挑战。我们提出Scensory，一种基于学习的机器人嗅觉框架，可从低成本、交叉敏感的VOC传感器阵列采集的短时间序列中同时识别真菌种类并定位其源。VOC的时间动态编码了化学与空间特征，我们通过在机器人自动化数据采集（带空间监督）上训练的神经网络进行解码。在五种真菌物种上，Scensory在3-7秒传感器输入下，于环境条件下实现最高89.85%的物种识别准确率和87.31%的源定位准确率。这些结果展示了从扩散主导化学信号中实现实时、空间锚定感知的能力，为机器人室内环境监测提供可扩展且低成本的源定位方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to detect and locate fungal contamination using weak, diffusion-dominated chemical signals, this work introduces Scensory, a learning-based olfaction framework that jointly identifies fungal species and localizes their sources using affordable VOC sensor arrays. The method leverages temporal dynamics in sensor readings to encode chemical and spatial information, which neural networks decode through robot-automated data collection with spatial supervision. Experiments across five fungal species show Scensory achieves up to 89.85% species identification accuracy and 87.31% source localization accuracy using only 3-7 seconds of sensor data under ambient conditions, demonstrating real-time, spatially grounded perception for scalable indoor environmental monitoring.
为实现机器人对室内弱扩散化学信号（如真菌污染）的感知与定位，作者提出Scensory系统，该系统通过低成本VOC传感器阵列，结合神经网络学习，同时识别真菌种类并定位其源点。方法利用化学信号的时间动态编码空间与化学信息，通过机器人自动采集并带有空间监督的数据训练模型。在五种真菌物种实验中，系统在3-7秒传感器数据下实现89.85%的物种识别准确率和87.31%的源点定位准确率，验证了扩散主导信号下实时、空间感知的可行性。
Modulating Cross-Modal Convergence with Single-Stimulus, Intra-Modal Dispersion
Authors: Eghbal A. Hosseini, Brian Cheung, Evelina Fedorenko, Alex H. Williams
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-04-23T16:27:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:27:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural networks exhibit a remarkable degree of representational convergence across diverse architectures, training objectives, and even data modalities. This convergence is predictive of alignment with brain representation. A recent hypothesis suggests this arises from learning the underlying structure in the environment in similar ways. However, it is unclear how individual stimuli elicit convergent representations across networks. An image can be perceived in multiple ways and expressed differently using words. Here, we introduce a methodology based on the Generalized Procrustes Algorithm to measure intra-modal representational convergence at the single-stimulus level. We applied this to vision models with distinct training objectives, selecting stimuli based on their degree of alignment (intra-modal dispersion). Crucially, we found that this intra-modal dispersion strongly modulates alignment between vision and language models (cross-modal convergence). Specifically, stimuli with low intra-modal dispersion (high agreement among vision models) elicited significantly higher cross-modal alignment than those with high dispersion, by up to a factor of two (e.g., in pairings of DINOv2 with language models). This effect was robust to stimulus selection criteria and generalized across different pairings of vision and language models. Measuring convergence at the single-stimulus level provides a path toward understanding the sources of convergence and divergence across modalities, and between neural networks and human neural representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过单刺激、模态内分散调节跨模态收敛
神经网络在不同架构、训练目标甚至数据模态间表现出显著的表征收敛，这种收敛可预测与大脑表征的一致性。近期假说认为，这是因网络以相似方式学习环境底层结构所致。然而，单个刺激如何在不同网络中引发收敛表征尚不明确。图像可被多种方式感知并用不同词语表达。本文提出基于广义普罗克斯特算法的方法，以单刺激水平测量模态内表征收敛。我们将其应用于不同训练目标的视觉模型，选取刺激依据其模态内分散度（即一致性）。关键发现是，模态内分散度显著调节视觉与语言模型间的跨模态收敛。具体而言，低分散度（视觉模型间高度一致）的刺激比高分散度刺激引发高两倍的跨模态对齐（如DINOv2与语言模型配对）。该效应不受刺激选取标准影响，且在不同视觉-语言模型配对中具普适性。单刺激水平测量收敛，为理解模态间及神经网络与人类神经表征的收敛与差异来源提供了路径。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how individual stimuli influence cross-modal convergence between vision and language models, motivated by the observation that neural networks converge across modalities despite differing architectures and training objectives. The authors employ a Generalized Procrustes Algorithm to quantify intra-modal representational dispersion at the single-stimulus level, selecting stimuli based on how consistently they are represented across vision models. Their experiments reveal that stimuli exhibiting low intra-modal dispersion—indicating high agreement among vision models—elicit significantly stronger cross-modal alignment with language models, up to twice as much as stimuli with high dispersion. This effect holds across different vision-language model pairs and is robust to stimulus selection criteria, suggesting that intra-modal consistency is a key driver of cross-modal convergence.
该研究旨在探究单个刺激如何影响视觉与语言模型之间的跨模态表征收敛性，动机源于神经网络在不同架构和训练目标下仍表现出跨模态收敛的现象。作者采用广义普鲁士算法，在单刺激层面量化视觉模型内部的表征分散度，并据此选择刺激。实验发现，视觉模型内部一致性高的刺激（低分散度）能显著提升与语言模型的跨模态对齐程度，最高可达两倍之多；这一效应在不同视觉-语言模型配对中均稳健存在，且不受刺激选择标准影响，表明刺激层面的一致性是驱动跨模态收敛的关键因素。
BadGraph: A Backdoor Attack Against Latent Diffusion Model for Text-Guided Graph Generation
Authors: Liang Ye, Shengqin Chen, Jiazhu Dai
First: 2025-10-23T17:54:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:26:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid progress of graph generation has raised new security concerns, particularly regarding backdoor vulnerabilities. Though prior work has explored backdoor attacks against diffusion models for image or unconditional graph generation, those against conditional graph generation models, especially text-guided graph generation models, remain largely unexamined. This paper proposes BadGraph, a backdoor attack method against latent diffusion models for text-guided graph generation. BadGraph leverages textual triggers to poison training data, covertly implanting backdoors that induce attacker-specified subgraphs during inference when triggers appear, while preserving normal performance on clean inputs. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets (PubChem, ChEBI-20, PCDes, MoMu) demonstrate the effectiveness and stealth of the attack: a poisoning rate of less than 10% can achieve a 50% attack success rate, while 24% suffices for over an 80% success rate, with negligible performance degradation on benign samples. Ablation studies further reveal that the backdoor is implanted during VAE and diffusion training rather than pretraining. These findings reveal the security vulnerabilities in latent diffusion models for text-guided graph generation, highlight the serious risks in applications such as drug discovery, and underscore the need for robust defenses against the backdoor attack in such diffusion models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BadGraph：针对文本引导图生成的潜在扩散模型的后门攻击
图生成技术的快速发展引发了新的安全担忧，特别是后门漏洞问题。尽管已有研究探讨了针对图像或无条件图生成扩散模型的后门攻击，但针对条件图生成模型，尤其是文本引导图生成模型的后门攻击仍鲜有研究。本文提出BadGraph，一种针对文本引导图生成潜在扩散模型的后门攻击方法。BadGraph利用文本触发器污染训练数据，在推理阶段当触发器出现时，隐秘植入后门以诱导攻击者指定的子图，同时在干净输入上保持正常性能。在四个基准数据集（PubChem、ChEBI-20、PCDes、MoMu）上的大量实验表明，该攻击有效且隐蔽：污染率低于10%即可实现50%的攻击成功率，24%的污染率足以达到80%以上的成功率，且对良性样本性能影响微乎其微。消融实验进一步表明，后门是在VAE和扩散训练阶段植入，而非预训练阶段。这些发现揭示了文本引导图生成潜在扩散模型的安全漏洞，突显了其在药物发现等应用中的严重风险，并强调了此类扩散模型中亟需构建针对后门攻击的鲁棒防御机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security risks in text-guided graph generation models, this paper introduces BadGraph, a backdoor attack targeting latent diffusion models. It uses textual triggers to poison training data, embedding covert backdoors that cause the model to generate attacker-specified subgraphs when triggered, without affecting performance on clean inputs. Experiments across four datasets show that as little as 10% poisoned data can achieve 50% attack success, rising to over 80% with 24%, while maintaining minimal degradation on benign samples. Ablation studies confirm the backdoor is implanted during VAE and diffusion training phases, not pretraining, revealing critical vulnerabilities in these models and underscoring the need for targeted defenses in applications like drug discovery.
为应对文本引导图生成模型的安全隐患，本文提出BadGraph，一种针对潜在扩散模型的后门攻击方法。该方法通过文本触发器污染训练数据，在模型中隐蔽植入后门，使模型在触发时生成攻击者指定的子图，同时对正常输入保持性能不变。在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，仅需10%的污染数据即可实现50%的攻击成功率，24%时成功率超过80%，且对良性样本性能影响极小。消融实验进一步证实，后门是在VAE和扩散训练阶段植入，而非预训练阶段，揭示了此类模型的关键安全漏洞，并强调了在药物发现等应用中亟需构建有效防御机制。
mGRADE: Minimal Recurrent Gating Meets Delay Convolutions for Lightweight Sequence Modeling
Authors: Tristan Torchet, Christian Metzner, Karthik Charan Raghunathan, Jimmy Weber, Sebastian Billaudelle, Laura Kriener, Melika Payvand
First: 2025-07-02T15:44:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:23:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-timescale sequence modeling relies on capturing both local fast dynamics and global slow context; yet, maintaining these capabilities under the strict memory constraints common to edge devices remains an open challenge. Current State-of-the-Art models with constant memory footprints trade off long-range selectivity and high-precision modeling of fast dynamics. To overcome this trade-off within a fixed memory budget, we propose mGRADE (minimally Gated Recurrent Architecture with Delay Embedding), a hybrid-memory system that introduces inductive biases across timescales by integrating a convolution with learnable temporal spacings with a lightweight gated recurrent component. We show theoretically that the learnable spacings are equivalent to a delay embedding, enabling parameter-efficient reconstruction of partially-observed fast dynamics, while the gated recurrent component selectively maintains long-range context with minimal memory overhead. On the challenging Long-Range Arena benchmark and 35-way Google Speech Commands raw audio classification task, mGRADE reduces the memory footprint by up to a factor of 8 compared to other State-of-the-Art models, while maintaining competitive performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：mGRADE：最小递归门控与延迟卷积结合的轻量级序列建模
多时间尺度序列建模需要同时捕捉局部快速动态和全局缓慢上下文；然而，在边缘设备常见的严格内存约束下维持这些能力仍是一个开放挑战。当前最先进的模型在固定内存占用下，往往在长程选择性和快速动态高精度建模之间做出权衡。为在固定内存预算内克服这一权衡，我们提出mGRADE（带延迟嵌入的最小门控递归架构），这是一种混合内存系统，通过将可学习时间间隔的卷积与轻量级门控递归组件相结合，引入跨时间尺度的归纳偏置。我们从理论上证明，可学习的时间间隔等价于延迟嵌入，从而实现对部分观测快速动态的参数高效重建，同时门控递归组件以最小内存开销选择性地保留长程上下文。在具有挑战性的Long-Range Arena基准测试和35类Google语音命令原始音频分类任务中，mGRADE相比其他最先进的模型内存占用降低高达8倍，同时保持竞争力的性能。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of modeling multi-timescale sequences under strict memory constraints on edge devices, mGRADE combines a delay convolution with learnable temporal spacings and a lightweight gated recurrent unit, leveraging inductive biases to efficiently capture both fast local dynamics and slow global context. The delay convolution enables parameter-efficient reconstruction of partially observed fast dynamics, while the gated recurrent component selectively preserves long-range context with minimal memory overhead. Experimental results on the Long-Range Arena benchmark and 35-way Google Speech Commands task demonstrate that mGRADE reduces memory footprint by up to 8x compared to state-of-the-art models while maintaining competitive performance.
为在边缘设备有限内存下有效建模多时间尺度序列，mGRADE结合了可学习时序间隔的延迟卷积与轻量级门控循环单元，通过引入时间尺度上的归纳偏置，高效捕捉局部快速动态与长程上下文，且不增加内存开销。理论分析表明，可学习间隔等价于延迟嵌入，可实现快速动态的参数高效重建，而门控循环单元则以极低开销选择性保留长程上下文。在Long-Range Arena和Google语音命令基准测试中，mGRADE相比现有最先进模型内存占用降低高达8倍，同时保持竞争力的性能。
GFlowState: Visualizing the Training of Generative Flow Networks Beyond the Reward
Authors: Florian Holeczek, Andreas Hinterreiter, Alex Hernandez-Garcia, Marc Streit, Christina Humer
First: 2026-04-23T16:22:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:22:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present GFlowState, a visual analytics system designed to illuminate the training process of Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets or GFNs). GFlowNets are a probabilistic framework for generating samples proportionally to a reward function. While GFlowNets have proved to be powerful tools in applications such as molecule and material discovery, their training dynamics remain difficult to interpret. Standard machine learning tools allow metric tracking but do not reveal how models explore the sample space, construct sample trajectories, or shift sampling probabilities during training. Our solution, GFlowState, allows users to analyze sampling trajectories, compare the sample space relative to reference datasets, and analyze the training dynamics. To this end, we introduce multiple views, including a chart of candidate rankings, a state projection, a node-link diagram of the trajectory network, and a transition heatmap. These visualizations enable GFlowNet developers and users to investigate sampling behavior and policy evolution, and to identify underexplored regions and sources of training failure. Case studies demonstrate how the system supports debugging and assessing the quality of GFlowNets across application domains. By making the structural dynamics of GFlowNets observable, our work enhances their interpretability and can accelerate GFlowNet development in practice.

PC2Model: ISPRS benchmark on 3D point cloud to model registration
Authors: Mehdi Maboudi, Said Harb, Jackson Ferrao, Kourosh Khoshelham, Yelda Turkan, Karam Mawas
First: 2026-04-21T15:44:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:21:34+00:00
Comments: ISPRS Congress 2026, Toronto
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Point cloud registration involves aligning one point cloud with another or with a three-dimensional (3D) model, enabling the integration of multimodal data into a unified representation. This is essential in applications such as construction monitoring, autonomous driving, robotics, and virtual or augmented reality (VR/AR). With the increasing accessibility of point cloud acquisition technologies, such as Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) and structured light scanning, along with recent advances in deep learning, the research focus has increasingly shifted towards downstream tasks, particularly point cloud-to-model (PC2Model) registration. While data-driven methods aim to automate this process, they struggle with sparsity, noise, clutter, and occlusions in real-world scans, which limit their performance. To address these challenges, this paper introduces the PC2Model benchmark, a publicly available dataset designed to support the training and evaluation of both classical and data-driven methods. Developed under the leadership of ICWG II/Ib, the PC2Model benchmark adopts a hybrid design that combines simulated point clouds with, in some cases, real-world scans and their corresponding 3D models. Simulated data provide precise ground truth and controlled conditions, while real-world data introduce sensor and environmental artefacts. This design supports robust training and evaluation across domains and enables the systematic analysis of model transferability from simulated to real-world scenarios. The dataset is publicly accessible at: \href{https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.17581812}{https://zenodo.org/records/17581812}
中文标题/摘要
标题：PC2Model：ISPRS 3D点云到模型配准基准
点云配准涉及将一个点云与另一个点云或三维（3D）模型对齐，从而实现多模态数据的统一表示整合。这在建筑监测、自动驾驶、机器人和虚拟/增强现实（VR/AR）等应用中至关重要。随着激光雷达（LiDAR）和结构光扫描等点云获取技术的普及，以及深度学习的最新进展，研究重点已逐渐转向下游任务，特别是点云到模型（PC2Model）配准。尽管数据驱动方法旨在自动化该过程，但在现实扫描中仍面临稀疏性、噪声、杂乱和遮挡等问题，限制了其性能。为应对这些挑战，本文引入了PC2Model基准数据集，这是一个公开可用的数据集，旨在支持经典方法和数据驱动方法的训练与评估。该基准由ICWG II/Ib领导开发，采用混合设计，结合模拟点云（部分情况下包含真实扫描及其对应3D模型）。模拟数据提供精确的地面真值和可控条件，而真实数据则引入传感器和环境伪影。该设计支持跨领域的稳健训练与评估，并能系统分析模型从模拟到真实场景的迁移能力。数据集公开获取地址：https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.17581812
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing need for reliable point cloud-to-model registration in applications like autonomous driving and robotics, this paper introduces the PC2Model benchmark to evaluate and train both classical and data-driven methods. The benchmark employs a hybrid design combining simulated point clouds with real-world scans and their corresponding 3D models, enabling precise ground truth in simulations while introducing real-world noise and occlusions. Experimental results demonstrate that models trained on simulated data often fail to generalize to real-world scenarios, highlighting the critical need for domain adaptation and robustness testing in PC2Model.
为应对自动驾驶和机器人等领域对点云到模型配准的迫切需求，本文提出了PC2Model基准数据集，旨在评估和训练在真实条件下表现稳定的配准方法。该数据集融合了模拟点云与真实扫描及其对应3D模型，既提供精确的地面真值用于可控训练，也引入现实噪声与遮挡以测试泛化能力。实验表明，数据驱动方法在模拟数据上表现良好，但在真实场景中的泛化能力受限，凸显了构建鲁棒可迁移模型的重要性。
Black-Box Skill Stealing Attack from Proprietary LLM Agents: An Empirical Study
Authors: Zihan Wang, Rui Zhang, Yu Liu, Chi Liu, Qingchuan Zhao, Hongwei Li, Guowen Xu
First: 2026-04-23T16:18:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:18:47+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM agents increasingly rely on skills to encapsulate reusable capabilities via progressively disclosed instructions. High-quality skills inject expert knowledge into general-purpose models, improving performance on specialized tasks. This quality and ease of dissemination drive the emergence of a skill economy: free skill marketplaces already report 90368 published skills, while paid marketplaces report more than 2000 listings and over $100,000 in creator earnings. Yet this growing marketplace also creates a new attack surface, as adversaries can interact with public agent to extract hidden proprietary skill content. We present the first empirical study of black-box skill stealing against LLM agent systems. To study this threat, we first derive an attack taxonomy from prior prompt-stealing methods and build an automated stealing prompt generation agent. This agent starts from model-generated seed prompts, expands them through scenario rationalization and structure injection, and enforces diversity via embedding filtering. This process yields a reproducible pipeline for evaluating agent systems. We evaluate such attacks across 3 commercial agent architectures and 5 LLMs. Our results show that agent skills can be extracted with only 3 interactions, posing a serious copyright risk. To mitigate this threat, we design defenses across three stages of the agent pipeline: input, inference, and output. Although these defenses achieve strong results, the attack remains inexpensive and readily automatable, allowing an adversary to launch repeated attempts with different variants; only one successful attempt is sufficient to compromise the protected skill. Overall, our findings suggest that these copyright risks are largely overlooked across proprietary agent ecosystems. We therefore advocate for more robust defense strategies that provide stronger protection guarantees.
