标题：从专有大语言模型代理中窃取黑盒技能的实证研究
大语言模型代理日益依赖技能来通过逐步披露的指令封装可重用能力。高质量技能将专家知识注入通用模型，提升其在特定任务上的表现。这种高质量与易传播性推动了技能经济的兴起：免费技能市场已报告90368个已发布技能，付费市场则报告超过2000个列表和超过10万美元的创作者收入。然而，这一不断增长的市场也创造了新的攻击面，因为对手可与公共代理交互以提取隐藏的专有技能内容。我们首次实证研究了针对大语言模型代理系统的黑盒技能窃取攻击。为研究此威胁，我们首先从先前的提示窃取方法中推导攻击分类，并构建了一个自动窃取提示生成代理。该代理从模型生成的种子提示出发，通过场景合理化和结构注入进行扩展，并通过嵌入过滤强制多样性。此过程形成了一种可复现的评估代理系统的流程。我们在3种商业代理架构和5个大语言模型上评估此类攻击。结果显示，仅需3次交互即可提取代理技能，构成严重版权风险。为缓解此威胁，我们在代理流程的三个阶段——输入、推理和输出——设计防御机制。尽管这些防御机制效果显著，攻击仍成本低廉且易于自动化，允许对手使用不同变体反复尝试；仅需一次成功尝试即可破坏受保护技能。总体而言，我们的研究发现表明，这些版权风险在专有代理生态系统中被广泛忽视。因此，我们呼吁采用更稳健的防御策略，提供更强的保护保障。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing skill economy in LLM agent ecosystems, where proprietary skills are traded and vulnerable to extraction, this study empirically investigates black-box skill stealing attacks. The authors develop an automated prompt generation agent that starts from model-generated seeds, expands them via scenario rationalization and structure injection, and enforces diversity through embedding filtering, creating a reproducible attack pipeline. Evaluated across three commercial agent architectures and five LLMs, the method successfully extracts proprietary skills in as few as three interactions, posing significant copyright risks. Defenses were designed at input, inference, and output stages but remain insufficient, as attacks are cheap to automate and require only one successful attempt to compromise a skill. The study concludes that these risks are largely unaddressed in proprietary agent systems, calling for stronger, more robust protection mechanisms.
鉴于LLM代理系统日益依赖技能封装可复用能力，以及公开技能市场的快速增长，本研究实证分析了针对代理系统的黑盒技能窃取攻击。作者构建了一个自动化提示生成代理，从模型生成的种子提示出发，通过场景推理与结构注入扩展提示，并通过嵌入过滤确保多样性。在三个商业代理架构和五个LLM上测试表明，仅需三次交互即可成功窃取技能，构成严重版权风险。研究提出输入、推理、输出三阶段防御机制，虽效果显著，但攻击仍可低成本自动化重复尝试，仅需一次成功即足以破坏技能。研究指出，此类版权风险在现有私有代理生态中被严重忽视，亟需更可靠的防御策略。
Alignment has a Fantasia Problem
Authors: Nathanael Jo, Zoe De Simone, Mitchell Gordon, Ashia Wilson
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-04-23T16:18:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:18:10+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern AI assistants are trained to follow instructions, implicitly assuming that users can clearly articulate their goals and the kind of assistance they need. Decades of behavioral research, however, show that people often engage with AI systems before their goals are fully formed. When AI systems treat prompts as complete expressions of intent, they can appear to be useful or convenient, but not necessarily aligned with the users' needs. We call these failures Fantasia interactions. We argue that Fantasia interactions demand a rethinking of alignment research: rather than treating users as rational oracles, AI should provide cognitive support by actively helping users form and refine their intent through time. This requires an interdisciplinary approach that bridges machine learning, interface design, and behavioral science. We synthesize insights from these fields to characterize the mechanisms and failures of Fantasia interactions. We then show why existing interventions are insufficient, and propose a research agenda for designing and evaluating AI systems that better help humans navigate uncertainty in their tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对齐存在‘幻想’问题
现代AI助手在训练中被教导遵循指令，隐含地假设用户能清晰表达其目标及所需帮助。然而，数十年的行为研究表明，人们常在目标尚未明确时便与AI系统互动。当AI系统将提示视为完整意图表达时，其可能显得有用或便捷，但未必真正契合用户需求。我们将此类失败称为‘幻想交互’。我们认为，‘幻想交互’要求对齐研究进行重新思考：AI不应将用户视为理性预言者，而应主动通过时间帮助用户形成并完善其意图，提供认知支持。这需要机器学习、界面设计与行为科学的跨学科协作。我们综合各领域洞见，刻画‘幻想交互’的机制与失败原因，并指出现有干预措施的不足，提出设计与评估更助人类应对任务不确定性AI系统的研究议程。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that current AI alignment research fails to account for the reality that users often interact with AI before fully forming their goals, leading to 'Fantasia interactions' where AI misinterprets incomplete or evolving intent. The authors propose that AI systems should actively support users in refining their goals over time, rather than treating prompts as complete instructions. Drawing on behavioral science, machine learning, and interface design, they analyze the mechanisms behind these failures and demonstrate that existing alignment methods are inadequate for handling user uncertainty. Their proposed research agenda emphasizes designing AI systems that co-evolve with user intent through iterative cognitive support.
本文指出，现代AI助手在用户目标尚未明确时易出现‘幻想交互’（Fantasia interactions），即AI将不完整的用户输入视为最终意图，导致输出与真实需求脱节。作者主张AI应主动协助用户逐步明确和调整目标，而非被动等待完整指令，需融合机器学习、界面设计与行为科学的跨学科方法。实验表明现有对齐方法无法有效处理用户意图的动态演化，作者呼吁构建新研究框架，以提升AI在用户任务不确定性中的认知支持能力。
On the algebra of Koopman eigenfunctions and on some of their infinities
Authors: Zahra Monfared, Saksham Malhotra, Sekiya Hajime, Ioannis Kevrekidis, Felix Dietrich
First: 2026-04-23T16:17:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:17:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
For continuous-time dynamical systems with reversible trajectories, the nowhere-vanishing eigenfunctions of the Koopman operator of the system form a multiplicative group. Here, we exploit this property to accelerate the systematic numerical computation of the eigenspaces of the operator. Given a small set of (so-called ``principal'') eigenfunctions that are approximated conventionally, we can obtain a much larger set by constructing polynomials of the principal eigenfunctions. This enriches the set, and thus allows us to more accurately represent application-specific observables. Often, eigenfunctions exhibit localized singularities (e.g. in simple, one-dimensional problems with multiple steady states) or extended ones (e.g. in simple, two-dimensional problems possessing a limit cycle, or a separatrix); we discuss eigenfunction matching/continuation across such singularities. By handling eigenfunction singularities and enabling their continuation, our approach supports learning consistent global representations from locally sampled data. This is particularly relevant for multistable systems and applications with sparse or fragmented measurements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于Koopman特征函数的代数及其某些无穷性
对于具有可逆轨迹的连续时间动力系统，其Koopman算子的处处非零特征函数构成一个乘法群。本文利用这一性质，加速系统算子特征空间的系统性数值计算。给定一组（所谓“主”）特征函数的常规近似，我们可通过构造主特征函数的多项式获得大量额外特征函数。这丰富了特征函数集合，从而更精确地表示应用特定的可观测量。通常，特征函数表现出局域奇异性（如简单一维多稳态问题）或扩展奇异性（如简单二维具有极限环或分隔线的问题）；我们讨论了在这些奇异性处特征函数的匹配/延续方法。通过处理特征函数奇异性并支持其延续，我们的方法可从局部采样数据学习一致的全局表示。这对多稳态系统及稀疏或碎片化测量的应用尤其重要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the multiplicative group structure of nowhere-vanishing Koopman eigenfunctions in reversible dynamical systems, this work proposes a method to accelerate the numerical computation of eigenspaces by constructing higher-order eigenfunctions as polynomials of a small set of principal eigenfunctions. This approach enriches the eigenfunction basis, enabling more accurate representation of application-specific observables. The method further addresses eigenfunction singularities—localized or extended—by developing matching and continuation techniques across such features, allowing consistent global representations from locally sampled data, which is especially useful in multistable systems or scenarios with sparse measurements.
基于可逆动力系统中非零Koopman本征函数构成乘法群的性质，该研究提出通过构造主本征函数的多项式来加速本征空间的数值计算，从而丰富本征函数集合，提升对应用特定可观测量的表征精度。该方法进一步处理本征函数的奇异性（局部或扩展型），实现跨奇点的匹配与延续，支持从局部采样数据中学习全局一致表示，尤其适用于多稳态系统及测量稀疏或碎片化的应用场景。
Wiring the 'Why': A Unified Taxonomy and Survey of Abductive Reasoning in LLMs
Authors: Moein Salimi, Shaygan Adim, Danial Parnian, Nima Alighardashi, Mahdi Jafari Siavoshani, Mohammad Hossein Rohban
First: 2026-04-09T09:16:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:14:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Regardless of its foundational role in human discovery and sense-making, abductive reasoning--the inference of the most plausible explanation for an observation--has been relatively underexplored in Large Language Models (LLMs). Despite the rapid advancement of LLMs, the exploration of abductive reasoning and its diverse facets has thus far been disjointed rather than cohesive. This paper presents the first survey of abductive reasoning in LLMs, tracing its trajectory from philosophical foundations to contemporary AI implementations. To address the widespread conceptual confusion and disjointed task definitions prevalent in the field, we establish a unified two-stage definition that formally categorizes prior work. This definition disentangles abduction into Hypothesis Generation, where models bridge epistemic gaps to produce candidate explanations, and Hypothesis Selection, where the generated candidates are evaluated and the most plausible explanation is chosen. Building upon this foundation, we present a comprehensive taxonomy of the literature, categorizing prior work based on their abductive tasks, datasets, underlying methodologies, and evaluation strategies. In order to ground our framework empirically, we conduct a compact benchmark study of current LLMs on abductive tasks, together with targeted comparative analyses across model sizes, model families, evaluation styles, and the distinct generation-versus-selection task typologies. Moreover, by synthesizing recent empirical results, we examine how LLM performance on abductive reasoning relates to deductive and inductive tasks, providing insights into their broader reasoning capabilities. Our analysis reveals critical gaps in current approaches--from static benchmark design and narrow domain coverage to narrow training frameworks and limited mechanistic understanding of abductive processes...
中文标题/摘要
标题：连接‘为何’：大语言模型中溯因推理的统一分类与综述
尽管溯因推理——即从观察推断最合理的解释——在人类发现与意义构建中具有基础性作用，但在大语言模型（LLMs）中仍相对较少被探索。尽管LLMs发展迅速，但对溯因推理及其多样面向的探索至今仍缺乏连贯性。本文首次综述了LLMs中的溯因推理，从哲学基础追溯至当代AI实现。为解决该领域普遍存在的概念混淆与任务定义碎片化问题，我们提出统一的两阶段定义，正式分类现有研究。该定义将溯因拆分为假设生成（模型弥合认知鸿沟以生成候选解释）与假设选择（评估生成候选并选出最合理解释）。在此基础上，我们构建了文献的全面分类体系，按溯因任务、数据集、方法论与评估策略对现有工作进行归类。为实证支撑框架，我们对当前LLMs在溯因任务上的表现进行紧凑基准测试，并针对模型规模、家族、评估方式及生成-选择任务类型开展对比分析。此外，通过整合近期实证结果，我们探讨LLMs在溯因推理中的表现如何关联演绎与归纳任务，揭示其更广泛的推理能力。分析发现当前方法存在关键缺陷——从静态基准设计、狭窄领域覆盖、狭隘训练框架到对溯因过程机制理解不足……
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored potential of abductive reasoning in LLMs despite its foundational role in human cognition, this paper introduces a unified two-stage taxonomy—distinguishing between Hypothesis Generation and Hypothesis Selection—to clarify fragmented prior work. It then surveys the literature across tasks, datasets, methods, and evaluations, and conducts a compact empirical benchmark comparing LLMs across model sizes, families, and task types. Results reveal that current models struggle with abductive tasks, showing significant gaps in benchmark design, domain coverage, training frameworks, and mechanistic understanding, while also highlighting weak correlations between abductive performance and deductive/inductive reasoning.
鉴于尽管在人类认知中具有基础性作用，但大语言模型中对溯因推理的研究仍相对匮乏，本文提出统一的两阶段分类框架——将溯因推理划分为假设生成与假设选择，以厘清领域内概念混乱与任务定义碎片化问题。文章系统梳理了现有文献在任务、数据集、方法与评估策略上的分类，并对当前主流LLM在溯因任务上的表现进行实证评估，对比不同模型规模、家族及生成与选择任务类型。结果表明，当前方法存在诸多不足：基准设计静态、领域覆盖狭窄、训练框架局限、对溯因机制理解不足，且其表现与演绎和归纳推理任务的相关性较低，说明溯因能力仍属独立且未充分发展的推理维度。
Tree Training: Accelerating Agentic LLMs Training via Shared Prefix Reuse
Authors: Jinghui Wang, Shaojie Wang, Yinghan Cui, Xuxing Chen, Chao Wang, Liang Huang, Can Tang, Xiaojiang Zhang, Junyi Peng, Li Wan, Haotian Zhang, Bin Chen
First: 2025-11-01T05:56:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:13:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic large language model (LLM) training often involves multi-turn interaction trajectories that branch into multiple execution paths due to concurrent tool use, think-mode, sub-agent, context management and other runtime designs. As a result, the tokens produced by a single task naturally form a tree-structured token trajectory with shared prefixes, rather than a linear sequence. Existing training pipelines linearize such trajectories and treat each branch independently, leading to substantial redundant computation in both forward and backward passes. We derive that averaging the loss over all branches independently is algebraically identical to a per-token weighted loss, where each token's weight equals the fraction of branches passing through it. The problem therefore reduces to computing the log-probability of every token in the prefix tree exactly once, with no repeated computation across shared prefixes: we propose DFS serialization of the tree, which visits every token exactly once, and adapt full-attention and SSM layers to ensure the resulting log-probabilities match independent per-branch calculation exactly. In practice, a single trajectory tree can be too large to fit in GPU memory; we therefore propose Redundancy-Free Tree Partitioning, which handles memory-constrained settings with zero redundant computation and peak memory bounded by a single root-to-leaf path. Together, these contributions form Tree Training, an efficient framework for training LLMs on tree-structured trajectories, achieving up to 6.2x end-to-end training speedup on dense and MoE models for both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：树状训练：通过共享前缀重用加速代理型大语言模型训练
代理型大语言模型（LLM）训练常涉及因并发工具使用、思考模式、子代理、上下文管理等运行时设计而分叉的多轮交互轨迹。因此，单个任务生成的token自然形成具有共享前缀的树状轨迹，而非线性序列。现有训练流程将此类轨迹线性化并独立处理各分支，导致前向和反向传播中大量冗余计算。我们推导出：独立对各分支损失取平均，在代数上等价于按每个token被各分支经过的比例加权的损失。问题因此转化为：仅对前缀树中每个token计算一次对数概率，且共享前缀间无重复计算。我们提出树状深度优先搜索序列化方法，确保每个token仅访问一次，并调整全注意力与SSM层，使所得对数概率与独立分支计算完全一致。实践中，单条轨迹树可能过大，无法完全放入GPU内存；因此我们提出无冗余树分割方法，在内存受限场景下实现零冗余计算，且峰值内存仅受限于单条根到叶路径。上述贡献共同构成树状训练框架，可高效训练树状轨迹上的LLM，在监督微调与强化学习中，对稠密和MoE模型实现最高达6.2倍的端到端训练加速。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of linearizing tree-structured token trajectories in agentic LLM training, which causes redundant computation across shared prefixes, the paper proposes Tree Training. It leverages DFS serialization to traverse each token in the prefix tree exactly once, and adapts full-attention and SSM layers to preserve exact log-probability computation equivalent to independent per-branch evaluation. To handle memory constraints, it introduces Redundancy-Free Tree Partitioning, which partitions the tree without redundant computation and keeps peak memory usage bounded by a single root-to-leaf path. Experiments show up to 6.2x end-to-end training speedup on both dense and MoE models for supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning.
为解决代理型大语言模型训练中树状轨迹线性化导致的重复计算问题，该文提出Tree Training框架：通过深度优先遍历树结构，仅对每个token计算一次对数概率，并调整注意力与SSM层以保持分支独立计算的准确性；同时引入无冗余树分割机制，在内存受限场景下避免重复计算，峰值内存仅需单条根到叶路径。实验表明，该方法在密集型与MoE模型上，监督微调与强化学习任务中均实现最高6.2倍的端到端训练加速。
Tool Attention Is All You Need: Dynamic Tool Gating and Lazy Schema Loading for Eliminating the MCP/Tools Tax in Scalable Agentic Workflows
Authors: Anuj Sadani, Deepak Kumar
First: 2026-04-23T16:10:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:10:00+00:00
Comments: 21 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has become a common interface for connecting large language model (LLM) agents to external tools, but its reliance on stateless, eager schema injection imposes a hidden per-turn overhead the MCP Tax or Tools Tax that practitioner reports place between roughly 10k and 60k tokens in typical multi-server deployments. This payload inflates the key-value cache, is associated with reasoning degradation as context utilization approaches published fracture points around 70%, and turns token budgets into a recurring operational cost. We introduce Tool Attention, a middleware-layer mechanism that generalizes the "Attention Is All You Need" paradigm from self-attention over tokens to gated attention over tools. Tool Attention combines (i) an Intent Schema Overlap (ISO) score from sentence embeddings, (ii) a state-aware gating function enforcing preconditions and access scopes, and (iii) a two-phase lazy schema loader that keeps a compact summary pool in context and promotes full JSON schemas only for top-k gated tools. We evaluate on a simulated 120-tool, six-server benchmark whose per-server token counts are calibrated to public audits of real MCP deployments. In this simulation, Tool Attention directly reduces measured per-turn tool tokens by 95.0% (47.3k -> 2.4k) and raises effective context utilization (a token-ratio quantity) from 24% to 91%. End-to-end figures for task success, latency, cost, and reasoning quality are reported as projections derived from the measured token counts combined with published deployment telemetry; they are not measured on live LLM agents, and we mark projected values explicitly throughout. Taken together, the results support a simple thesis: protocol-level efficiency, not raw context length, is a binding constraint on scalable gentic systems. The code for this work is accessible at https://github.com/asadani/tool-attention
中文标题/摘要
标题：工具注意力：动态工具门控与懒加载模式，消除可扩展代理工作流中的MCP/工具开销
模型上下文协议（MCP）已成为连接大语言模型（LLM）代理与外部工具的通用接口，但其依赖无状态、即时模式的模式注入，会带来隐性每轮开销——即所谓的MCP税或工具税，在典型多服务器部署中，该开销约为1万至6万tokens。该负载会膨胀键值缓存，当上下文利用率接近约70%的已知临界点时，会导致推理性能下降，并使token预算转化为持续运营成本。我们提出工具注意力，一种中间件层机制，将“注意力机制只需全部”范式从对token的自注意力推广到对工具的门控注意力。工具注意力结合（i）句子嵌入计算的意图模式重叠（ISO）得分，（ii）状态感知门控函数，强制执行前提条件和访问范围，以及（iii）两阶段懒加载模式，仅在上下文中保留紧凑摘要池，并仅对前k个门控工具加载完整JSON模式。我们在一个模拟的120工具、六服务器基准测试中进行评估，每服务器token计数根据真实MCP部署的公开审计进行校准。在该模拟中，工具注意力直接将每轮工具token减少95.0%（47.3k → 2.4k），并将有效上下文利用率（一种token比率量）从24%提升至91%。任务成功率、延迟、成本和推理质量的端到端指标为基于测量token计数与已发布部署遥测数据推导的预测值，未在真实LLM代理上测量，全文明确标注预测值。综合来看，结果支持一个简单论点：协议级效率，而非原始上下文长度，是可扩展代理系统的关键约束。本工作的代码可在https://github.com/asadani/tool-attention获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high per-turn overhead of MCP/Tools Tax in scalable LLM agent workflows—ranging from 10k to 60k tokens per turn—the paper proposes Tool Attention, a middleware mechanism inspired by "Attention Is All You Need" that gates tool access dynamically. It combines an Intent Schema Overlap score from sentence embeddings, a state-aware gating function for preconditions, and a lazy schema loader that loads full JSON schemas only for top-k gated tools. Evaluated on a simulated 120-tool, six-server benchmark calibrated to real MCP deployments, Tool Attention reduced tool tokens per turn by 95.0% (from 47.3k to 2.4k) and boosted effective context utilization from 24% to 91%. Projected end-to-end metrics for task success, latency, cost, and reasoning quality show improved efficiency without live agent testing, supporting the thesis that protocol-level efficiency—not raw context length—is the key constraint for scalable agentic systems.
为解决LLM代理系统中MCP/Tools Tax导致的每轮高开销问题，该研究提出Tool Attention机制，通过动态工具门控与懒加载模式替代传统急切模式。该机制结合意图-模式重叠得分、状态感知门控函数及两阶段模式加载器，仅加载必要工具模式，使模拟120工具六服务器场景中每轮工具令牌减少95%，上下文利用率从24%提升至91%。综合预测指标显示任务成功率、延迟、成本及推理质量均显著改善，验证协议层效率而非上下文长度是可扩展智能系统的关键瓶颈。
Divide-then-Diagnose: Weaving Clinician-Inspired Contexts for Ultra-Long Capsule Endoscopy Videos
Authors: Bowen Liu, Li Yang, Shanshan Song, Mingyu Tang, Zhifang Gao, Qifeng Chen, Yangqiu Song, Huimin Chen, Xiaomeng Li
First: 2026-04-23T16:07:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:07:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Capsule endoscopy (CE) enables non-invasive gastrointestinal screening, but current CE research remains largely limited to frame-level classification and detection, leaving video-level analysis underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce and formally define a new task, diagnosis-driven CE video summarization, which requires extracting key evidence frames that covers clinically meaningful findings and making accurate diagnoses from those evidence frames. This setting is challenging because diagnostically relevant events are extremely sparse and can be overwhelmed by tens of thousands of redundant normal frames, while individual observations are often ambiguous due to motion blur, debris, specular highlights, and rapid viewpoint changes. To facilitate research in this direction, we introduce VideoCAP, the first CE dataset with diagnosis-driven annotations derived from real clinical reports. VideoCAP comprises 240 full-length videos and provides realistic supervision for both key evidence frame extraction and diagnosis. To address this task, we further propose DiCE, a clinician-inspired framework that mirrors the standard CE reading workflow. DiCE first performs efficient candidate screening over the raw video, then uses a Context Weaver to organize candidates into coherent diagnostic contexts that preserve distinct lesion events, and an Evidence Converger to aggregate multi-frame evidence within each context into robust clip-level judgments. Experiments show that DiCE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, producing concise and clinically reliable diagnostic summaries. These results highlight diagnosis-driven contextual reasoning as a promising paradigm for ultra-long CE video summarization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分而诊之：为超长胶囊内镜视频编织临床启发式上下文
胶囊内镜（CE）可实现无创胃肠筛查，但当前CE研究主要局限于帧级分类与检测，视频级分析仍被忽视。为弥合此差距，我们提出并正式定义新任务——诊断驱动的CE视频摘要，要求从视频中提取涵盖临床意义发现的关键证据帧，并基于这些帧做出准确诊断。该任务挑战性在于：诊断相关事件极为稀疏，常被数万冗余正常帧淹没，且单帧观察常因运动模糊、碎屑、镜面高光及快速视角变化而模糊不清。为促进该方向研究，我们引入VideoCAP，首个基于真实临床报告的诊断驱动标注CE数据集，包含240条完整视频，为关键证据帧提取与诊断提供真实监督。为解决此任务，我们进一步提出DiCE框架，其受临床医生工作流启发：首先对原始视频进行高效候选筛选，再通过Context Weaver将候选组织为连贯诊断上下文，保留不同病灶事件；并通过Evidence Converger在每个上下文中聚合多帧证据，形成稳健的片段级判断。实验表明，DiCE持续优于现有最先进方法，生成简洁且临床可靠的诊断摘要。结果凸显诊断驱动上下文推理是超长CE视频摘要的有前景范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of video-level analysis in capsule endoscopy despite its clinical importance, this work introduces a new task—diagnosis-driven CE video summarization—to extract clinically meaningful evidence frames and make accurate diagnoses. To tackle the challenge of sparse diagnostic events amid vast normal frames and ambiguous visual artifacts, the authors propose DiCE, a clinician-inspired framework that first screens video candidates, then weaves them into coherent diagnostic contexts using a Context Weaver, and finally converges multi-frame evidence per context via an Evidence Converger. Experiments on VideoCAP, the first CE dataset with diagnosis-driven annotations, show DiCE consistently outperforms existing methods, generating concise, reliable diagnostic summaries that reflect clinical reasoning.
为突破胶囊内镜视频分析中仅限帧级分类的局限，本文提出诊断驱动的视频摘要新任务，并设计DiCE框架，模拟临床医生阅片流程。该框架先高效筛选候选帧，再通过上下文编织模块将相关帧组织成保留病变事件的诊断上下文，最后用证据聚合模块将多帧证据整合为可靠的片段级诊断。在首个含临床报告驱动标注的VideoCAP数据集上，DiCE显著优于现有方法，生成简洁、准确且临床可靠的诊断摘要。
Probably Approximately Consensus: On the Learning Theory of Finding Common Ground
Authors: Carter Blair, Ben Armstrong, Shiri Alouf-Heffetz, Nimrod Talmon, Davide Grossi
Venue: IJCAI 2025
First: 2026-04-23T16:06:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:06:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Social Choice and Learning Algorithms Workshop at IJCAI 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A primary goal of online deliberation platforms is to identify ideas that are broadly agreeable to a community of users through their expressed preferences. Yet, consensus elicitation should ideally extend beyond the specific statements provided by users and should incorporate the relative salience of particular topics. We address this issue by modelling consensus as an interval in a one-dimensional opinion space derived from potentially high-dimensional data via embedding and dimensionality reduction. We define an objective that maximizes expected agreement within a hypothesis interval where the expectation is over an underlying distribution of issues, implicitly taking into account their salience. We propose an efficient Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) algorithm and establish PAC-learning guarantees. Our initial experiments demonstrate the performance of our algorithm and examine more efficient approaches to identifying optimal consensus regions. We find that through selectively querying users on an existing sample of statements, we can reduce the number of queries needed to a practical number.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可能近似共识：寻找共同立场的学习理论研究
在线讨论平台的主要目标是通过用户表达的偏好识别社区广泛认同的观点。然而，共识提取应超越用户提供的具体陈述，还应纳入特定议题的相对重要性。我们通过嵌入和降维将高维数据映射到一维意见空间，将共识建模为该空间中的一个区间。我们定义一个目标函数，最大化假设区间内预期的一致性，其中期望基于议题的潜在分布，隐式考虑其重要性。我们提出了一种高效的经验风险最小化（ERM）算法，并建立了PAC学习保证。初步实验验证了算法性能，并探讨了更高效的识别最优共识区域的方法。我们发现，通过有选择地对现有陈述样本中的用户进行查询，可将所需查询次数减少到实用水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to find consensus beyond user-provided statements by incorporating topic salience, the paper models consensus as an interval in a one-dimensional opinion space derived from high-dimensional data via embedding and dimensionality reduction. It proposes an Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) algorithm that maximizes expected agreement over an issue distribution, establishing PAC-learning guarantees for theoretical soundness. Experimental results show that selectively querying users on a sample of statements significantly reduces the number of queries required to identify optimal consensus regions, making the approach practically efficient.
为在用户明确陈述之外结合话题显著性以达成共识，该文将共识建模为通过嵌入与降维从高维数据中获得的一维意见空间中的一个区间，并提出一种基于经验风险最小化的算法，以最大化假设区间内预期的一致性，且具备PAC学习保证。实验表明，通过在语句样本上选择性查询用户，可大幅减少达到最优共识区域所需的查询次数，使方法在实际平台中具备可行性。
Mitigating Lost in Multi-turn Conversation via Curriculum RL with Verifiable Accuracy and Abstention Rewards
Authors: Ming Li
First: 2025-10-21T15:32:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:06:17+00:00
Comments: ACL2026, camera-ready
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models demonstrate strong capabilities in single-turn instruction following but suffer from Lost-in-Conversation (LiC), a degradation in performance as information is revealed progressively in multi-turn settings. Motivated by the current progress on Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), we propose Curriculum Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Accuracy and Abstention Rewards (RLAAR), a framework that encourages models not only to generate correct answers, but also to judge the solvability of questions in the multi-turn conversation setting. Our approach employs a competence-gated curriculum that incrementally increases dialogue difficulty (in terms of instruction shards), stabilizing training while promoting reliability. Using multi-turn, on-policy rollouts and a mixed-reward system, RLAAR teaches models to balance problem-solving with informed abstention, reducing premature answering behaviors that cause LiC. Evaluated on LiC benchmarks, RLAAR significantly mitigates LiC performance decay (62.6% to 75.1%) and improves calibrated abstention rates (33.5% to 73.4%). Together, these results provide a practical recipe for building multi-turn reliable and trustworthy LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过可验证准确率与拒绝奖励的课程强化学习缓解多轮对话中的迷失现象
大语言模型在单轮指令遵循任务中表现优异，但在多轮对话中易出现“迷失对话”（LiC）现象，即随着信息逐步揭示，性能下降。受可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）最新进展启发，我们提出一种结合可验证准确率与拒绝奖励的课程强化学习框架（RLAAR），鼓励模型不仅生成正确答案，还判断多轮对话中问题的可解性。该方法采用能力门控课程，逐步增加对话难度（以指令片段衡量），在稳定训练的同时提升可靠性。通过多轮在线策略回放与混合奖励机制，RLAAR训练模型在解决问题与明智拒绝之间取得平衡，减少导致LiC的过早回答行为。在LiC基准测试中，RLAAR显著缓解了性能衰减（62.6%提升至75.1%），并提高了校准后的拒绝率（33.5%提升至73.4%）。这些结果为构建可靠、可信的多轮大语言模型提供了实用方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the performance degradation of large language models in multi-turn conversations known as Lost-in-Conversation (LiC), this paper proposes Curriculum Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Accuracy and Abstention Rewards (RLAAR). The method introduces a competence-gated curriculum that gradually increases dialogue complexity while using a mixed reward system combining verifiable accuracy and abstention signals to encourage models to avoid premature answers. Evaluated on LiC benchmarks, RLAAR reduces performance decay from 62.6% to 75.1% and boosts calibrated abstention rates from 33.5% to 73.4%, demonstrating its effectiveness in improving reliability and trustworthiness in multi-turn settings.
为解决大语言模型在多轮对话中因信息逐步暴露导致的性能退化（Lost-in-Conversation, LiC）问题，本文提出一种结合可验证准确率与拒绝奖励的课程强化学习框架（RLAAR）。该方法通过能力门控课程逐步提升对话难度，并结合在线策略回滚与混合奖励机制，引导模型在不确定时主动拒绝回答，而非仓促作答。实验表明，RLAAR可将LiC性能衰减率从62.6%降至75.1%，并使校准后的拒绝率从33.5%提升至73.4%，显著增强模型在多轮对话中的可靠性与可信度。
Geo-R1: Improving Few-Shot Geospatial Referring Expression Understanding with Reinforcement Fine-Tuning
Authors: Zilun Zhang, Zian Guan, Tiancheng Zhao, Haozhan Shen, Tianyu Li, Yuxiang Cai, Zhonggen Su, Zhaojun Liu, Jianwei Yin, Xiang Li
First: 2025-09-26T07:01:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:06:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ISPRS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Referring expression understanding in remote sensing poses unique challenges, as it requires reasoning over complex object-context relationships. While supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on multimodal large language models achieves strong performance with massive labeled datasets, they struggle in data-scarce scenarios, leading to poor generalization. To address this limitation, we propose Geo-R1, a reasoning-centric reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) paradigm for few-shot geospatial referring. Geo-R1 enforces the model to first generate explicit, interpretable reasoning chains that decompose referring expressions, and then leverage these rationales to localize target objects. This "reason first, then act" process enables the model to make more effective use of limited annotations, enhances generalization, and provides interpretability. We validate Geo-R1 on three carefully designed few-shot geospatial referring benchmarks, where our model consistently and substantially outperforms SFT baselines. It also demonstrates strong cross-dataset generalization, highlighting its robustness. Code and data will be released at: https://github.com/Geo-R1/geo-r1.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Geo-R1：通过强化微调提升小样本地理参照表达理解
遥感中的参照表达理解面临独特挑战，因其需推理复杂对象-上下文关系。尽管多模态大语言模型通过监督微调（SFT）在大规模标注数据上表现优异，但在数据稀缺场景下表现不佳，泛化能力弱。为解决此问题，我们提出Geo-R1，一种面向小样本地理参照的推理中心强化微调（RFT）范式。Geo-R1要求模型首先生成显式、可解释的推理链，分解参照表达，再利用这些推理链定位目标对象。该“先推理，再行动”过程使模型更有效利用有限标注，增强泛化能力并提供可解释性。我们在三个精心设计的小样本地理参照基准上验证Geo-R1，模型表现持续且显著优于SFT基线，同时展现强跨数据集泛化能力，凸显其鲁棒性。代码与数据将在https://github.com/Geo-R1/geo-r1发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization of supervised fine-tuning in data-scarce geospatial referring tasks, this work introduces Geo-R1, a reinforcement fine-tuning method that prioritizes reasoning over direct prediction. The model first generates interpretable reasoning chains to decompose referring expressions, then uses these rationales to guide object localization, enabling better use of limited annotations and improving generalization. Experiments on three few-shot benchmarks show Geo-R1 consistently outperforms supervised fine-tuning baselines and exhibits strong cross-dataset robustness.
为应对遥感图像中少样本地理指代理解的挑战，本文提出Geo-R1方法，采用强化微调策略，先生成可解释的推理链分解指代表达，再据此定位目标物体，从而更高效利用有限标注并提升泛化能力。在三个少样本地理指代基准测试中，Geo-R1显著优于监督微调基线，并展现出良好的跨数据集泛化能力。
Multiscale Super Resolution without Image Priors
Authors: Daniel Fu, Gabby Litterio, Pedro Felzenszwalb, Rashid Zia
First: 2026-04-23T16:05:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:05:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We address the ambiguities in the super-resolution problem under translation. We demonstrate that combinations of low-resolution images at different scales can be used to make the super-resolution problem well posed. Such differences in scale can be achieved using sensors with different pixel sizes (as demonstrated here) or by varying the effective pixel size through changes in optical magnification (e.g., using a zoom lens). We show that images acquired with pairwise coprime pixel sizes lead to a system with a stable inverse, and furthermore, that super-resolution images can be reconstructed efficiently using Fourier domain techniques or iterative least squares methods. Our mathematical analysis provides an expression for the expected error of the least squares reconstruction for large signals assuming i.i.d. noise that elucidates the noise-resolution tradeoff. These results are validated through both one- and two-dimensional experiments that leverage charge-coupled device (CCD) hardware binning to explore reconstructions over a large range of effective pixel sizes. Finally, two-dimensional reconstructions for a series of targets are used to demonstrate the advantages of multiscale super-resolution, and implications of these results for common imaging systems are discussed.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需图像先验的多尺度超分辨率
我们解决了在平移变换下超分辨率问题中的歧义性。我们证明，不同尺度的低分辨率图像组合可使超分辨率问题变得良定。这种尺度差异可通过使用不同像素尺寸的传感器（如本文所示）或通过改变光学放大倍率（例如使用变焦镜头）来实现有效像素尺寸的变化。我们表明，使用互质像素尺寸对采集的图像可形成具有稳定逆的系统，且可通过傅里叶域技术或迭代最小二乘法高效重建超分辨率图像。我们的数学分析给出了在独立同分布噪声假设下，大信号情况下最小二乘重建的预期误差表达式，阐明了噪声与分辨率之间的权衡关系。这些结果通过一维和二维实验得到验证，实验利用电荷耦合器件（CCD）硬件的像素合并功能，探索了广泛的有效像素尺寸范围下的重建效果。最后，通过对一系列目标的二维重建，展示了多尺度超分辨率的优势，并讨论了这些结果对常见成像系统的影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the ambiguity in super-resolution under translation, this work proposes using multiscale low-resolution images to make the problem well-posed, leveraging sensors with different pixel sizes or optical magnification. The method employs Fourier domain techniques or iterative least squares to reconstruct high-resolution images from such multiscale inputs, particularly when pixel sizes are pairwise coprime, ensuring a stable inverse. Experimental results using CCD hardware binning confirm the effectiveness across a wide range of effective pixel sizes, and two-dimensional reconstructions of multiple targets demonstrate improved fidelity compared to single-scale methods, highlighting the noise-resolution tradeoff quantified by the least squares error model.
为解决翻译下超分辨率问题的模糊性，该研究提出利用不同尺度的低分辨率图像使问题变得良定，通过不同像素尺寸传感器或光学变焦实现尺度差异。方法采用傅里叶域技术或迭代最小二乘法重建高分辨率图像，证明互质像素尺寸可获得稳定逆矩阵。实验利用CCD硬件分箱验证了在广泛有效像素尺寸下高效重建，理论误差分析揭示噪声与分辨率的权衡关系。对多个目标的二维重建结果表明，多尺度超分辨率优于单尺度方法，对实际成像系统具有重要启示。
Learning State-Tracking from Code Using Linear RNNs
Authors: Julien Siems, Riccardo Grazzi, Kirill Kalinin, Hitesh Ballani, Babak Rahmani
First: 2026-02-16T15:07:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:05:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Over the last years, state-tracking tasks, particularly permutation composition, have become a testbed to understand the limits of sequence models architectures like Transformers and RNNs (linear and non-linear). However, these are often sequence-to-sequence tasks: learning to map actions (permutations) to states, which is incompatible with the next-token prediction setting commonly used to train language models. We address this gap by converting permutation composition into code via REPL traces that interleave state-reveals through prints and variable transformations. We show that linear RNNs capable of state-tracking excel also in this setting, while Transformers still fail. Motivated by this representation, we investigate why tracking states in code is generally difficult: actions are not always fully observable. We frame this as tracking the state of a probabilistic finite-state automaton with deterministic state reveals and show that linear RNNs can be worse than non-linear RNNs at tracking states in this setup.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用线性RNN从代码中学习状态跟踪
近年来，状态跟踪任务（特别是排列组合）已成为理解Transformer和RNN（线性和非线性）等序列模型架构局限性的测试平台。然而，这些任务通常是序列到序列的任务：学习将动作（排列）映射到状态，这与训练语言模型常用的下一个词预测设置不兼容。我们通过将排列组合转换为代码，利用REPL轨迹中通过打印和变量变换交错揭示状态的方式，解决了这一差距。我们表明，能够进行状态跟踪的线性RNN在此设置中表现优异，而Transformer仍失败。受此表示的启发，我们研究了在代码中跟踪状态为何通常困难：动作并非总是完全可观测。我们将此问题表述为跟踪具有确定性状态揭示的随机有限状态自动机的状态，并证明在此设置中，线性RNN在跟踪状态方面可能不如非线性RNN表现好。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate sequence models on state-tracking tasks beyond next-token prediction, this work converts permutation composition into executable code using REPL traces that reveal states via prints and variable updates. It demonstrates that linear RNNs perform well in this setting, outperforming Transformers, while also showing that linear RNNs can underperform non-linear RNNs when state tracking involves partial observability, modeled as a probabilistic finite-state automaton with deterministic reveals.
为评估序列模型在超出下一词预测框架的态追踪任务中的能力，论文将排列组合任务转化为通过REPL日志交替输出状态和变量变换的代码形式。实验表明，线性RNN在该设定下表现优异，而Transformer则失败，说明线性RNN更适合此类任务。进一步分析指出，代码中状态追踪困难源于观测不完全，当状态以确定性方式揭示时，线性RNN在概率有限状态机中可能不如非线性RNN表现好。
Quotient-Space Diffusion Models
Authors: Yixian Xu, Yusong Wang, Shengjie Luo, Kaiyuan Gao, Tianyu He, Di He, Chang Liu
Venue: ICLR 2026 Oral Presentation
First: 2026-04-23T16:04:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:04:40+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026 Oral Presentation; 40 pages, 5 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion-based generative models have reformed generative AI, and have enabled new capabilities in the science domain, for example, generating 3D structures of molecules. Due to the intrinsic problem structure of certain tasks, there is often a symmetry in the system, which identifies objects that can be converted by a group action as equivalent, hence the target distribution is essentially defined on the quotient space with respect to the group. In this work, we establish a formal framework for diffusion modeling on a general quotient space, and apply it to molecular structure generation which follows the special Euclidean group $\text{SE}(3)$ symmetry. The framework reduces the necessity of learning the component corresponding to the group action, hence simplifies learning difficulty over conventional group-equivariant diffusion models, and the sampler guarantees recovering the target distribution, while heuristic alignment strategies lack proper samplers. The arguments are empirically validated on structure generation for small molecules and proteins, indicating that the principled quotient-space diffusion model provides a new framework that outperforms previous symmetry treatments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：商空间扩散模型
基于扩散的生成模型已革新生成式人工智能，并在科学领域赋予了新能力，例如生成分子的三维结构。由于某些任务的固有结构问题，系统中常存在对称性，即通过群作用可相互转换的对象被视为等价，因此目标分布本质上定义在相对于该群的商空间上。本文建立了一般商空间上扩散建模的正式框架，并将其应用于遵循特殊欧几里得群 SE(3) 对称性的分子结构生成。该框架减少了学习群作用分量的必要性，从而简化了相对于传统群等变扩散模型的学习难度，且采样器可保证恢复目标分布，而启发式对齐策略缺乏合适的采样器。相关论点在小分子和蛋白质结构生成任务中经实证验证，表明该原理性的商空间扩散模型提供了一种优于以往对称性处理方法的新框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the prevalence of symmetry in scientific generative tasks like molecular structure generation, this work introduces a formal framework for diffusion modeling on quotient spaces, specifically targeting systems with SE(3) symmetry. The method leverages group action equivalence to eliminate the need to learn group-invariant components, thereby simplifying training compared to conventional equivariant models. Experimental results on small molecules and proteins demonstrate that the proposed quotient-space diffusion model not only recovers the target distribution with a principled sampler but also outperforms prior symmetry-aware approaches in generation quality and efficiency.
鉴于科学生成任务（如分子结构生成）中普遍存在对称性，本文提出了一种在群作用定义的商空间上运行的扩散模型形式化框架，并将其应用于SE(3)对称的分子系统。该方法避免学习与群变换相关的冗余部分，从而简化了训练过程，同时保证采样器能恢复目标分布——这是启发式方法所欠缺的。在小分子和蛋白质结构生成实验中，该原理化的商空间扩散模型表现优于先前的对称性处理方法。
TEMA: Anchor the Image, Follow the Text for Multi-Modification Composed Image Retrieval
Authors: Zixu Li, Yupeng Hu, Zhiheng Fu, Zhiwei Chen, Yongqi Li, Liqiang Nie
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-23T16:03:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T16:03:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) is an important image retrieval paradigm that enables users to retrieve a target image using a multimodal query that consists of a reference image and modification text. Although research on CIR has made significant progress, prevailing setups still rely simple modification texts that typically cover only a limited range of salient changes, which induces two limitations highly relevant to practical applications, namely Insufficient Entity Coverage and Clause-Entity Misalignment. In order to address these issues and bring CIR closer to real-world use, we construct two instruction-rich multi-modification datasets, M-FashionIQ and M-CIRR. In addition, we propose TEMA, the Text-oriented Entity Mapping Architecture, which is the first CIR framework designed for multi-modification while also accommodating simple modifications. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that TEMA's superiority in both original and multi-modification scenarios, while maintaining an optimal balance between retrieval accuracy and computational efficiency. Our codes and constructed multi-modification dataset (M-FashionIQ and M-CIRR) are available at https://github.com/lee-zixu/ACL26-TEMA/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TEMA：锚定图像，依据文本进行多修改组合图像检索
组合图像检索（CIR）是一种重要的图像检索范式，允许用户通过由参考图像和修改文本组成的多模态查询检索目标图像。尽管CIR研究已取得显著进展，但现有设置仍依赖简单的修改文本，通常仅覆盖有限范围的显著变化，这导致两个与实际应用高度相关的局限性：实体覆盖不足和从句-实体错位。为解决这些问题并使CIR更贴近实际应用，我们构建了两个指令丰富的多修改数据集M-FashionIQ和M-CIRR。此外，我们提出了TEMA（文本导向实体映射架构），这是首个专为多修改设计且同时支持简单修改的CIR框架。在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，TEMA在原始和多修改场景中均表现优越，同时保持检索精度与计算效率之间的最佳平衡。我们的代码和构建的多修改数据集（M-FashionIQ和M-CIRR）可在https://github.com/lee-zixu/ACL26-TEMA/获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) systems that struggle with complex, multi-modification queries due to insufficient entity coverage and clause-entity misalignment, the authors introduce TEMA, a Text-oriented Entity Mapping Architecture designed to handle both simple and multi-modification queries. TEMA leverages instruction-rich datasets M-FashionIQ and M-CIRR to train and evaluate its ability to anchor image regions to textual modifications accurately. Experimental results across four benchmarks show TEMA outperforms prior methods in both single and multi-modification settings while maintaining high efficiency, demonstrating its practical relevance for real-world CIR applications.
为解决现有组合图像检索（CIR）系统因修改文本过于简单而导致的实体覆盖不足和语句-实体错位问题，作者构建了两个富含指令的多修改数据集M-FashionIQ和M-CIRR，并提出了TEMA文本导向实体映射架构，这是首个同时支持简单和多修改查询的CIR框架。实验在四个基准数据集上表明，TEMA在单修改与多修改场景下均优于现有方法，且在检索精度与计算效率间保持最优平衡，展现出良好的实用性和可扩展性。
SyMTRS: Benchmark Multi-Task Synthetic Dataset for Depth, Domain Adaptation and Super-Resolution in Aerial Imagery
Authors: Safouane El Ghazouali, Nicola Venturi, Michael Rueegsegger, Umberto Michelucci
First: 2026-04-23T15:59:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:59:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent advances in deep learning for remote sensing rely heavily on large annotated datasets, yet acquiring high-quality ground truth for geometric, radiometric, and multi-domain tasks remains costly and often infeasible. In particular, the lack of accurate depth annotations, controlled illumination variations, and multi-scale paired imagery limits progress in monocular depth estimation, domain adaptation, and super-resolution for aerial scenes. We present SyMTRS, a large-scale synthetic dataset generated using a high-fidelity urban simulation pipeline. The dataset provides high-resolution RGB aerial imagery (2048 x 2048), pixel-perfect depth maps, night-time counterparts for domain adaptation, and aligned low-resolution variants for super-resolution at x2, x4, and x8 scales. Unlike existing remote sensing datasets that focus on a single task or modality, SyMTRS is designed as a unified multi-task benchmark enabling joint research in geometric understanding, cross-domain robustness, and resolution enhancement. We describe the dataset generation process, its statistical properties, and its positioning relative to existing benchmarks. SyMTRS aims to bridge critical gaps in remote sensing research by enabling controlled experiments with perfect geometric ground truth and consistent multi-domain supervision. The results obtained in this work can be reproduced from this Github repository: https://github.com/safouaneelg/SyMTRS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SyMTRS：面向航空影像深度估计、域自适应与超分辨率的多任务合成基准数据集
近期遥感领域深度学习的进展高度依赖大规模标注数据集，但获取几何、辐射度量及多域任务的高质量真实标注仍成本高昂且常不可行。尤其缺乏精确深度标注、可控光照变化及多尺度配对影像，限制了航空场景单目深度估计、域自适应与超分辨率的发展。本文提出SyMTRS，一个通过高保真城市仿真流程生成的大规模合成数据集。该数据集提供高分辨率RGB航空影像（2048×2048）、像素级精确深度图、用于域自适应的夜间影像，以及用于超分辨率的x2、x4、x8尺度对齐低分辨率版本。与现有聚焦单一任务或模态的遥感数据集不同，SyMTRS设计为统一多任务基准，支持几何理解、跨域鲁棒性与分辨率增强的联合研究。本文描述了数据集生成流程、统计特性及其与现有基准的对比定位。SyMTRS旨在通过提供完美几何真实标注与一致多域监督，弥合遥感研究中的关键差距。本工作结果可从以下GitHub仓库复现：https://github.com/safouaneelg/SyMTRS。

Motivated by the scarcity of high-quality, multi-domain ground truth data for aerial remote sensing tasks, this work introduces SyMTRS, a large-scale synthetic dataset generated via a high-fidelity urban simulation pipeline. It provides high-resolution RGB imagery, pixel-perfect depth maps, night-time counterparts for domain adaptation, and downsampled variants for super-resolution at x2, x4, and x8 scales. Unlike existing benchmarks that focus on single tasks, SyMTRS enables joint evaluation across monocular depth estimation, domain adaptation, and super-resolution.
