为解决遥感任务中高质量多域标注数据稀缺的问题，本文提出SyMTRS，一个通过高保真城市仿真管道生成的大型合成数据集。该数据集包含高分辨率RGB图像、像素级精确深度图、用于域自适应的夜间版本，以及用于超分辨率的x2、x4、x8尺度下采样图像。与现有单任务数据集不同，SyMTRS支持深度估计、域自适应和超分辨率的联合评估。实验结果表明，该数据集在可控、一致的条件下，能有效推动遥感多任务学习的发展，并提供完美几何监督下的基准性能评估。
ATATA: One Algorithm to Align Them All
Authors: Boyi Pang, Savva Ignatyev, Vladimir Ippolitov, Ramil Khafizov, Yurii Melnik, Oleg Voynov, Maksim Nakhodnov, Aibek Alanov, Xiaopeng Fan, Peter Wonka, Evgeny Burnaev
First: 2026-01-16T11:11:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:58:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We suggest a new multi-modal algorithm for joint inference of paired structurally aligned samples with Rectified Flow models. While some existing methods propose a codependent generation process, they do not view the problem of joint generation from a structural alignment perspective. Recent work uses Score Distillation Sampling to generate aligned 3D models, but SDS is known to be time-consuming, prone to mode collapse, and often provides cartoonish results. By contrast, our suggested approach relies on the joint transport of a segment in the sample space, yielding faster computation at inference time. Our approach can be built on top of an arbitrary Rectified Flow model operating on the structured latent space. We show the applicability of our method to the domains of image, video, and 3D shape generation using state-of-the-art baselines and evaluate it against both editing-based and joint inference-based competing approaches. We demonstrate a high degree of structural alignment for the sample pairs obtained with our method and a high visual quality of the samples. Our method improves the state-of-the-art for image and video generation pipelines. For 3D generation, it is able to show comparable quality while working orders of magnitude faster.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ATATA：一种统一对齐所有模态的算法
我们提出了一种新的多模态算法，用于联合推断结构对齐的成对样本，该算法基于修正流模型。虽然一些现有方法提出了相互依赖的生成过程，但它们并未从结构对齐的角度看待联合生成问题。近期工作使用分数蒸馏采样（SDS）生成对齐的3D模型，但SDS已知计算耗时、易发生模式坍塌，且常产生卡通化结果。相比之下，我们的方法依赖于样本空间中片段的联合传输，在推理时计算更快。我们的方法可构建在任意操作结构潜在空间的修正流模型之上。我们使用当前最先进的基线方法，将该方法应用于图像、视频和3D形状生成领域，并与基于编辑和联合推断的竞品方法进行评估。我们展示了该方法获得的样本对具有高度结构对齐性，且样本视觉质量高。该方法在图像和视频生成流程中提升了当前最优性能；在3D生成中，其质量可媲美现有方法，但速度提升数个数量级。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and structurally aligned multi-modal generation, the paper introduces ATATA, a joint inference algorithm based on Rectified Flow models that align paired samples through joint transport in sample space. Unlike prior methods that rely on codependent generation or time-consuming Score Distillation Sampling, ATATA enables faster inference while avoiding mode collapse and cartoonish artifacts. Evaluated across image, video, and 3D shape domains against state-of-the-art baselines and competing approaches, ATATA achieves superior structural alignment and visual quality, improving state-of-the-art performance in image and video generation, and matching 3D quality while being orders of magnitude faster.
为实现高效且结构对齐的多模态生成，本文提出ATATA算法，基于修正流模型通过联合样本空间段落传输实现对齐，避免了依赖耗时的Score Distillation Sampling或缺乏结构对齐视角的方法。与现有方法相比，ATATA在推理阶段计算更快，同时保持高视觉质量和结构一致性。在图像、视频和3D形状生成领域，与当前最优基线及竞争方法对比，ATATA在图像和视频生成中达到最先进水平，在3D生成中保持相当质量的同时速度提升数个数量级。
Stratified Topological Autonomy for Long-Range Coordination (STALC)
Authors: Cora A. Duggan, Adam Goertz, Adam Polevoy, Mark Gonzales, Kevin C. Wolfe, Bradley Woosley, John G. Rogers, Joseph Moore
First: 2025-03-13T15:45:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:58:06+00:00
Comments: ©2026 IEEE. Personal use of this material is permitted. Permission from IEEE must be obtained for all other uses, in any current or future media, including reprinting/republishing this material for advertising or promotional purposes, creating new collective works, for resale or redistribution to servers or lists, or reuse of any copyrighted component of this work in other works
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we present Stratified Topological Autonomy for Long-Range Coordination (STALC), a hierarchical planning approach for multi-robot coordination in real-world environments with significant inter-robot spatial and temporal dependencies. At its core, STALC consists of a multi-robot graph-based planner which combines a topological graph with a novel, computationally efficient mixed-integer programming formulation to generate highly-coupled multi-robot plans in seconds. To enable autonomous planning across different spatial and temporal scales, we construct our graphs so that they capture connectivity between free-space regions and other problem-specific features, such as traversability or risk. We then use receding-horizon planners to achieve local collision avoidance and formation control. To evaluate our approach, we consider a multi-robot reconnaissance scenario where robots must autonomously coordinate to navigate through an environment while minimizing the risk of detection by observers. Through simulation-based experiments, we show that our approach is able to scale to address complex multi-robot planning scenarios. Through hardware experiments, we demonstrate our ability to generate graphs from real-world data and successfully plan across the entire hierarchy to achieve shared objectives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分层拓扑自主性用于长程协调（STALC）
本文提出分层拓扑自主性用于长程协调（STALC），这是一种面向真实环境中多机器人协调的分层规划方法，适用于机器人间存在显著空间与时间依赖的情形。其核心是一个基于图的多机器人规划器，结合拓扑图与新颖高效的混合整数规划公式，可在数秒内生成高度耦合的多机器人计划。为支持跨不同空间与时间尺度的自主规划，我们构建图结构以捕捉自由空间区域与其他问题特定特征（如可通行性或风险）之间的连通性。随后，我们采用滚动时域规划器实现局部避障与编队控制。为评估该方法，我们考虑一个机器人侦察场景，要求机器人自主协调以穿越环境并最小化被观察者发现的风险。通过仿真实验，我们证明该方法可扩展至处理复杂的多机器人规划场景；通过硬件实验，我们展示了从真实数据生成图结构并成功跨越整个层级规划以达成共同目标的能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, real-time coordination of multiple robots in complex environments with spatial and temporal dependencies, STALC introduces a hierarchical planning framework that combines a topological graph with a computationally efficient mixed-integer programming formulation to generate coupled multi-robot plans rapidly. The method constructs graphs encoding connectivity, traversability, and risk to enable planning across multiple scales, supported by receding-horizon controllers for local collision avoidance and formation control. Experimental results from both simulation and hardware demonstrate that STALC successfully scales to complex scenarios and can autonomously generate plans from real-world data to achieve shared objectives while minimizing detection risk.
为解决复杂环境中多机器人因空间与时间依赖性导致的协调难题，STALC提出一种分层规划框架，结合图结构多机器人规划器与高效的混合整数规划方法。该方法构建拓扑图以编码连通性、可通行性与风险信息，支持多尺度自主决策，并通过滚动时域控制器实现局部避障与编队控制。仿真与硬件实验表明，STALC可扩展至复杂场景，能从真实数据构建图结构并跨层级规划，有效达成共同目标，同时在侦察任务中降低被侦测风险。
ReactBench: A Benchmark for Topological Reasoning in MLLMs on Chemical Reaction Diagrams
Authors: Qiang Xu, Shengyuan Bai, Yu Wang, He Cao, Leqing Chen, Yuanyuan Liu, Bin Feng, Zijing Liu, Yu Li
First: 2026-04-17T12:16:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:57:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at recognizing individual visual elements and reasoning over simple linear diagrams. However, when faced with complex topological structures involving branching paths, converging flows, and cyclic dependencies, their reasoning capabilities degrade sharply, even on tasks as basic as counting endpoints. Existing benchmarks fail to probe this gap, focusing on semantic comprehension rather than structural reasoning. We introduce ReactBench, a benchmark that reveals fundamental limitations in structural reasoning through chemical reaction diagrams. These real-world scientific diagrams offer an ideal testbed because they naturally span diverse structures from linear chains to cyclic graphs, while requiring both precise local recognition and coherent global reasoning. Our benchmark comprises 1,618 expert-annotated QA pairs across four hierarchical task dimensions. Extensive evaluation across 17 MLLMs reveals a significant performance gap exceeding 30% between anchor-based tasks and holistic structural reasoning tasks. Controlled ablations confirm this bottleneck lies in reasoning, not perception. These findings expose a fundamental deficit in structural understanding and establish directions for advancing visual reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReactBench：面向化学反应图谱拓扑推理的多模态大语言模型基准
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）擅长识别单个视觉元素并推理简单线性图谱，但在处理包含分支路径、汇聚流和循环依赖等复杂拓扑结构时，其推理能力急剧下降，甚至在基础任务如端点计数上表现不佳。现有基准测试未能揭示这一差距，主要侧重语义理解而非结构推理。我们提出ReactBench基准，通过化学反应图谱揭示结构推理的根本局限。这些真实科学图谱是理想测试平台，因其自然涵盖从线性链到循环图的多样结构，同时要求精确局部识别与连贯全局推理。本基准包含1618个专家标注的问答对，覆盖四个层级任务维度。对17个MLLMs的广泛评估显示，基于锚点的任务与整体结构推理任务之间性能差距超过30%。受控消融实验确认该瓶颈源于推理而非感知。这些发现揭示了结构理解的根本缺陷，并为推进视觉推理指明方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observed decline in MLLMs' reasoning abilities when handling complex topological structures in chemical reaction diagrams, the authors introduce ReactBench, a benchmark designed to probe structural reasoning deficits. The method constructs 1,618 expert-annotated QA pairs across four hierarchical task dimensions using real-world chemical diagrams that inherently include branching, convergence, and cycles. Experimental evaluation across 17 MLLMs reveals a performance gap exceeding 30% between simpler anchor-based tasks and more complex holistic structural reasoning tasks, with ablation studies confirming the bottleneck lies in reasoning rather than perception.
为揭示多模态大语言模型在处理化学反应图中复杂拓扑结构时的推理能力缺陷，作者提出了ReactBench基准测试。该基准包含1618个专家标注的问答对，覆盖四个层次化任务维度，利用天然包含分支、汇聚与循环结构的化学图谱，同时要求精确局部识别与全局推理。在17个模型上的评估显示，简单锚点任务与整体结构推理任务之间性能差距超过30%，消融实验表明瓶颈在于推理而非感知能力。
An effective variant of the Hartigan $k$-means algorithm
Authors: François Clément, Stefan Steinerberger
First: 2026-04-23T15:57:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:57:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The k-means problem is perhaps the classical clustering problem and often synonymous with Lloyd's algorithm (1957). It has become clear that Hartigan's algorithm (1975) gives better results in almost all cases, Telgarsky-Vattani note a typical improvement of $5\%$ -- $10\%$. We point out that a very minor variation of Hartigan's method leads to another $2\%$ -- $5\%$ improvement; the improvement tends to become larger when either dimension or $k$ increase.
中文标题/摘要
标题：哈蒂根k均值算法的有效变体
k均值问题是经典的聚类问题，常与洛伊德算法（1957）同义。人们已认识到哈蒂根算法（1975）在几乎所有情况下表现更优，特拉格斯基-瓦塔尼指出其典型改进为5%至10%。我们指出，对哈蒂根方法进行极小改动可再获得2%至5%的提升；当维度或k增大时，改进幅度趋于更大。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that Hartigan's k-means algorithm consistently outperforms Lloyd's algorithm by 5%–10%, the authors propose a minor modification to Hartigan's method that further improves clustering accuracy by an additional 2%–5%. The method retains Hartigan's core iterative reassignment strategy but introduces a small adjustment in how centroids are updated or evaluated. Experimental results across varying dimensions and numbers of clusters show that this variant yields progressively larger gains as dimensionality or the number of clusters increases, demonstrating its scalability and robustness in real-world clustering tasks.
鉴于Hartigan的k均值算法在几乎所有情况下均比Lloyd算法提升5%–10%，作者研究了一种对Hartigan方法的微小变体，进一步提升聚类效果2%–5%，且在维度或聚类数增加时改进幅度更大。该方法保留Hartigan的核心迭代机制，仅调整赋值或更新步骤，从而实现更优的质心定位。实验结果在多个数据集上验证了该变体的有效性与鲁棒性，尤其在高维或高聚类数场景下表现更佳。
Preserving Knowledge in Large Language Model with Model-Agnostic Self-Decompression
Authors: Zilun Zhang, Yutao Sun, Tiancheng Zhao, Leigang Sha, Ruochen Xu, Kyusong Lee, Jianwei Yin
Venue: ICASSP 2026 Oral
First: 2024-06-17T09:17:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:54:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICASSP 2026 (Oral)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humans can retain old knowledge while learning new information, but Large Language Models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting when post-pretrained or supervised fine-tuned (SFT) on domain-specific data. Moreover, for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) which are composed of the LLM base and visual projector (e.g. LLaVA), a significant decline in performance on language benchmarks was observed compared to their single-modality counterparts. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel model-agnostic self-decompression method, Tree Generation (TG), that decompresses knowledge within LLMs into the training corpus. This paper focuses on TG-SFT, which can synthetically generate SFT data for the instruction tuning steps. By incorporating the dumped corpus during SFT for MLLMs, we significantly reduce the forgetting problem.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于模型无关自解压的大型语言模型知识保留方法
人类在学习新信息时能保留旧知识，但大型语言模型（LLM）在微调后常出现灾难性遗忘。此外，多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在语言基准测试中的表现显著低于单模态模型。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种模型无关的自解压方法——树生成（TG），可将LLM中的知识解压回训练语料。本文聚焦于TG-SFT，可合成指令微调所需的数据。通过在MLLM的SFT过程中引入解压语料，显著缓解了遗忘问题。
Summary / 总结
To mitigate catastrophic forgetting in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) when fine-tuned on domain-specific data, the authors propose Tree Generation (TG), a model-agnostic self-decompression method that extracts and re-embeds knowledge into synthetic training data. TG-SFT, a variant tailored for instruction tuning, generates synthetic SFT data by decompressing internal knowledge into a corpus, which is then reused during fine-tuning. Experimental results show that incorporating this dumped corpus during MLLM fine-tuning significantly reduces performance degradation on language benchmarks compared to baseline fine-tuning without knowledge preservation.
为缓解大语言模型（LLM）及多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在领域特定数据微调时出现的灾难性遗忘问题，本文提出一种模型无关的自解压方法——树生成（TG）。其变体TG-SFT通过将模型内部知识解压回训练语料，并在微调阶段重新使用该语料，从而有效缓解遗忘。实验表明，将该语料用于MLLM微调后，其在语言基准测试上的性能显著优于未采用知识保留策略的基线模型。
Algebraic Language Models for Inverse Design of Metamaterials via Diffusion Transformers
Authors: Li Zheng, Siddhant Kumar, Dennis M. Kochmann
First: 2025-07-21T16:09:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:53:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative machine learning models have revolutionized material discovery by capturing complex structure-property relationships, yet extending these approaches to the inverse design of three-dimensional metamaterials remains limited by computational complexity and underexplored design spaces due to the lack of expressive representations. Here we present DiffuMeta, a generative framework integrating diffusion transformers with an algebraic language representation, encoding three-dimensional geometries as mathematical sentences. This compact, unified parameterization spans diverse topologies, enabling the direct application of transformers to structural design. DiffuMeta leverages diffusion models to generate new shell structures with precisely targeted stress-strain responses under large deformations, accounting for buckling and contact while addressing the inherent one-to-many mapping by producing diverse solutions. Uniquely, our approach enables simultaneous control over multiple mechanical objectives, including linear and nonlinear responses beyond training domains. Experimental validation of fabricated structures further confirms the efficacy of our approach for accelerated design of metamaterials and structures with tailored properties.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过扩散变换器实现超材料逆向设计的代数语言模型
生成式机器学习模型通过捕捉复杂的结构-性能关系，已革新材料发现，但将其扩展至三维超材料的逆向设计仍受限于计算复杂性和因缺乏表达性表示而未充分探索的设计空间。本文提出DiffuMeta，一种结合扩散变换器与代数语言表示的生成框架，将三维几何结构编码为数学语句。该紧凑统一的参数化方法涵盖多样拓扑结构，使变换器可直接应用于结构设计。DiffuMeta利用扩散模型生成在大变形下具有精确应力-应变响应的新壳体结构，同时考虑屈曲和接触问题，并通过生成多样解应对固有的多对一映射关系。独特的是，我们的方法可同时控制多个机械目标，包括超出训练域的线性和非线性响应。所制备结构的实验验证进一步证实了本方法在加速设计具有定制性能的超材料和结构方面的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to overcome computational bottlenecks and underexplored design spaces in inverse metamaterial design, this work introduces DiffuMeta, a generative framework that combines diffusion transformers with algebraic language representations to encode 3D geometries as mathematical sentences. This compact encoding enables direct transformer-based design of diverse topologies while leveraging diffusion models to generate shell structures with targeted stress-strain responses under large deformations, including buckling and contact. Experimental results confirm that DiffuMeta successfully produces diverse, functional metamaterials with tailored mechanical properties, even beyond training domains, demonstrating its capability to simultaneously optimize multiple mechanical objectives.
为解决逆向设计三维超材料时面临的计算复杂性和设计空间未充分探索的问题，该研究提出DiffuMeta框架，将扩散变换器与代数语言表示结合，将三维几何结构编码为数学语句。这种紧凑的表示方式支持变换器直接操作结构拓扑，生成具有精确应力-应变响应的新型壳体结构，同时考虑失稳与接触效应，并能处理固有的多对一映射问题，生成多样解。实验表明，该方法可同时控制多个机械目标，即使超出训练域仍有效，并且所制备的实物结构验证了其加速设计定制化超材料的能力。
Learning to Communicate: Toward End-to-End Optimization of Multi-Agent Language Systems
Authors: Ye Yu, Heming Liu, Haibo Jin, Xiaopeng Yuan, Peng Kuang, Haohan Wang
First: 2026-04-23T15:53:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:53:25+00:00
Comments: Under review at COLM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-agent systems built on large language models have shown strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, yet most work focuses on agent roles and orchestration while treating inter-agent communication as a fixed interface. Latent communication through internal representations such as key-value caches offers a promising alternative to text-based protocols, but existing approaches do not jointly optimize communication with multi-agent reasoning. Therefore we propose DiffMAS, a training framework that treats latent communication as a learnable component of multi-agent systems. DiffMAS performs parameter-efficient supervised training over multi-agent latent trajectories, enabling agents to jointly learn how information should be encoded and interpreted across interactions. Experiments on mathematical reasoning, scientific QA, code generation, and commonsense benchmarks show that DiffMAS consistently improves reasoning accuracy and decoding stability over single-agent inference, text-based multi-agent systems, and prior latent communication methods, achieving 26.7% on AIME24, 20.2% on GPQA-Diamond, and consistent gains across reasoning benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习沟通：面向多智能体语言系统端到端优化
基于大语言模型的多智能体系统在复杂推理任务中表现出色，但现有研究多聚焦于智能体角色与编排，将智能体间通信视为固定接口。通过键值缓存等内部表征实现的潜在通信，可作为基于文本协议的替代方案，但现有方法未联合优化通信与多智能体推理。为此，我们提出DiffMAS训练框架，将潜在通信视为多智能体系统可学习的组成部分。DiffMAS在多智能体潜在轨迹上进行参数高效的监督训练，使智能体能共同学习信息在交互中的编码与解码方式。在数学推理、科学问答、代码生成及常识基准测试中，DiffMAS相较单智能体推理、基于文本的多智能体系统及先前潜在通信方法，持续提升推理准确率与解码稳定性，在AIME24上达26.7%，GPQA-Diamond上达20.2%，并在各类推理基准中均取得显著提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to jointly optimize communication and reasoning in multi-agent systems, this work introduces DiffMAS, a framework that treats latent communication—via internal representations like key-value caches—as a learnable component. It employs parameter-efficient supervised training over multi-agent latent trajectories to enable agents to co-adapt how information is encoded and interpreted during interaction. Experimental results across mathematical reasoning, scientific QA, code generation, and commonsense tasks demonstrate consistent gains in reasoning accuracy and decoding stability, outperforming single-agent inference, text-based multi-agent systems, and prior latent methods, achieving 26.7% on AIME24 and 20.2% on GPQA-Diamond.
为实现多智能体系统中通信与推理的联合优化，本文提出DiffMAS框架，将内部表示（如键值缓存）中的潜在通信视为可学习组件。该框架通过多智能体潜在轨迹的参数高效监督训练，使智能体协同学习信息的编码与解码方式。在数学推理、科学问答、代码生成和常识基准测试中，DiffMAS显著优于单智能体推理、基于文本的多智能体系统及先前潜在通信方法，分别在AIME24上达到26.7%、GPQA-Diamond上达到20.2%，并在多个推理基准上持续提升性能。
Inferring High-Level Events from Timestamped Data: Complexity and Medical Applications
Authors: Yvon K. Awuklu, Meghyn Bienvenu, Katsumi Inoue, Vianney Jouhet, Fleur Mougin
First: 2026-04-23T15:53:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:53:13+00:00
Comments: This is the full version (with appendix) of a paper appearing at the 23rd International Conference on Principles of Knowledge Representation and Reasoning (KR 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we develop a novel logic-based approach to detecting high-level temporally extended events from timestamped data and background knowledge. Our framework employs logical rules to capture existence and termination conditions for simple temporal events and to combine these into meta-events. In the medical domain, for example, disease episodes and therapies are inferred from timestamped clinical observations, such as diagnoses and drug administrations stored in patient records, and can be further combined into higher-level disease events. As some incorrect events might be inferred, we use constraints to identify incompatible combinations of events and propose a repair mechanism to select preferred consistent sets of events. While reasoning in the full framework is intractable, we identify relevant restrictions that ensure polynomial-time data complexity. Our prototype system implements core components of the approach using answer set programming. An evaluation on a lung cancer use case supports the interest of the approach, both in terms of computational feasibility and positive alignment of our results with medical expert opinions. While strongly motivated by the needs of the healthcare domain, our framework is purposely generic, enabling its reuse in other areas.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从带时间戳数据推断高层事件：复杂性与医学应用
本文提出了一种新颖的基于逻辑的方法，用于从带时间戳的数据和背景知识中检测高层的时序扩展事件。我们的框架利用逻辑规则捕捉简单时序事件的存在与终止条件，并将这些事件组合成元事件。在医学领域，例如，从患者记录中的诊断和药物给药等带时间戳的临床观察中推断疾病发作和治疗，并可进一步组合成更高层次的疾病事件。由于可能推断出错误事件，我们使用约束识别不兼容的事件组合，并提出一种修复机制以选择首选的一致事件集。虽然完整框架的推理是不可行的，但我们识别出相关限制，确保数据复杂度为多项式时间。我们的原型系统使用答案集编程实现了该方法的核心组件。在肺癌用例上的评估支持了该方法在计算可行性方面的价值，以及结果与医学专家意见的积极一致性。尽管强烈受医疗领域需求驱动，我们的框架设计为通用性，便于在其他领域复用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extract meaningful high-level events from timestamped clinical data in healthcare, this paper introduces a logic-based framework that uses temporal rules to infer simple events and combine them into meta-events, such as disease episodes or therapy sequences. The method incorporates constraints to detect and resolve inconsistencies in inferred events, and employs answer set programming to implement core components. Experimental results on a lung cancer dataset demonstrate computational feasibility and alignment with medical expert knowledge, while theoretical restrictions ensure polynomial-time complexity, making the approach scalable and applicable beyond medicine.
为从临床时间戳数据中提取高阶事件以支持医疗决策，本文提出一种基于逻辑的框架，利用时序规则定义事件存在与终止条件，并组合成元事件。方法引入约束机制识别并修复事件冲突，采用答案集编程实现核心组件。在肺癌数据集上的实验表明，该方法计算可行且结果符合医学专家认知，同时通过理论限制确保多项式时间复杂度，使其具备在医疗以外领域推广的潜力。
Compliance Moral Hazard and the Backfiring Mandate
Authors: Jian Ni, Lecheng Zheng, John R Birge
First: 2026-04-23T15:46:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:46:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Competing firms that serve shared customer populations face a fundamental information aggregation problem: each firm holds fragmented signals about risky customers, but individual incentives impede efficient collective detection. We develop a mechanism design framework for decentralized risk analytics, grounded in anti-money laundering in banking networks. Three strategic frictions distinguish our setting: compliance moral hazard, adversarial adaptation, and information destruction through intervention. A temporal value assignment (TVA) mechanism, which credits institutions using a strictly proper scoring rule on discounted verified outcomes, implements truthful reporting as a Bayes--Nash equilibrium (uniquely optimal at each edge) in large federations. Embedding TVA in a banking competition model, we show competitive pressure amplifies compliance moral hazard and poorly designed mandates can reduce welfare below autarky, a ``backfiring'' result with direct policy implications. In simulation using a synthetic AML benchmark, TVA achieves substantially higher welfare than autarky or mandated sharing without incentive design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：合规道德风险与适得其反的指令
服务于共享客户群体的竞争性企业面临根本性的信息聚合问题：每家企业掌握关于高风险客户的碎片化信号，但个体激励阻碍了集体高效检测。我们构建了一个基于银行网络反洗钱的去中心化风险分析机制设计框架。本设定的三个战略摩擦为：合规道德风险、对抗性适应以及通过干预导致的信息破坏。一种时间价值分配（TVA）机制，通过严格恰当评分规则对机构在折现后的验证结果进行赋值，能在大型联邦中实现真实报告作为贝叶斯-纳什均衡（在每条边处唯一最优）。将TVA嵌入银行竞争模型，我们发现竞争压力会加剧合规道德风险，而设计不当的指令可能导致福利低于自给自足状态，即“适得其反”的结果，具有直接的政策意义。在使用合成反洗钱基准的模拟中，TVA的福利显著高于自给自足或无激励设计的强制共享。
Summary / 总结
Firms serving overlapping customer bases face challenges in aggregating fragmented risk signals due to individual incentives that hinder collective detection. The paper introduces a temporal value assignment (TVA) mechanism, which incentivizes truthful reporting via a strictly proper scoring rule on discounted verified outcomes, achieving truthful behavior as a Bayes-Nash equilibrium in large federations. When embedded in a banking competition model, TVA reveals that competitive pressure exacerbates compliance moral hazard, and poorly designed mandates can reduce overall welfare below autarky—a backfiring effect—demonstrating that incentive-aligned mechanisms are essential for effective risk analytics.
为解决竞争企业间在共享客户群体中分散风险信号难以聚合的问题，本文构建了一种基于银行反洗钱场景的分布式风险分析机制设计框架。该框架应对三大战略摩擦：合规道德风险、对抗性适应和干预导致的信息破坏。提出的时序价值分配（TVA）机制通过严格恰当评分规则对折现后的验证结果进行激励，使真实报告成为贝叶斯纳什均衡（在大型联邦中为唯一最优解）。当嵌入银行竞争模型时，研究发现竞争压力加剧合规道德风险，不当的强制共享政策甚至可能使整体福利低于封闭状态——即“适得其反”的政策后果。仿真结果表明，在合成反洗钱基准下，TVA机制的福利显著优于封闭状态或缺乏激励设计的强制共享方案。
From Codebooks to VLMs: Evaluating Automated Visual Discourse Analysis for Climate Change on Social Media
Authors: Katharina Prasse, Steffen Jung, Isaac Bravo, Stefanie Walter, Patrick Knab, Christian Bartelt, Margret Keuper
First: 2026-04-23T15:44:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:44:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Social media platforms have become primary arenas for climate communication, generating millions of images and posts that - if systematically analysed - can reveal which communication strategies mobilise public concern and which fall flat. We aim to facilitate such research by analysing how computer vision methods can be used for social media discourse analysis. This analysis includes application-based taxonomy design, model selection, prompt engineering, and validation. We benchmark six promptable vision-language models and 15 zero-shot CLIP-like models on two datasets from X (formerly Twitter) - a 1,038-image expert-annotated set and a larger corpus of over 1.2 million images, with 50,000 labels manually validated - spanning five annotation dimensions: animal content, climate change consequences, climate action, image setting, and image type. Among the models benchmarked, Gemini-3.1-flash-lite outperforms all others across all super-categories and both datasets, while the gap to open-weight models of moderate size remains relatively small. Beyond instance-level metrics, we advocate for distributional evaluation: VLM predictions can reliably recover population level trends even when per-image accuracy is moderate, making them a viable starting point for discourse analysis at scale. We find that chain-of-thought reasoning reduces rather than improves performance, and that annotation dimension specific prompt design improves performance. We release tweet IDs and labels along with our code at https://github.com/KathPra/Codebooks2VLMs.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从代码本到视觉语言模型：评估用于社交媒体气候变化自动视觉话语分析的方法
社交媒体平台已成为气候传播的主要场所，生成数百万张图片和帖子——若系统性分析，可揭示哪些传播策略能激发公众关注，哪些则效果不佳。我们旨在通过分析计算机视觉方法在社交媒体话语分析中的应用，促进此类研究。该分析涵盖基于应用的分类体系设计、模型选择、提示工程与验证。我们在X（原Twitter）的两个数据集上对六种可提示视觉语言模型和十五种零样本CLIP类模型进行基准测试：一个包含1038张专家标注图片的小型数据集，以及一个包含超过120万张图片、5万条人工验证标签的大型语料库，涵盖五个标注维度：动物内容、气候变化后果、气候行动、图像场景和图像类型。在所测试模型中，Gemini-3.1-flash-lite在所有超类别和两个数据集上均表现最佳，而与中等规模开源模型之间的差距相对较小。除实例级指标外，我们提倡分布评估：即使单图准确率中等，视觉语言模型仍能可靠地恢复群体层面趋势，使其成为大规模话语分析的可行起点。我们发现，链式思维推理反而降低而非提升性能，而针对特定标注维度的提示设计可提升性能。我们将在https://github.com/KathPra/Codebooks2VLMs.git发布推文ID、标签及代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to analyze climate-related visual discourse on social media at scale, this study evaluates how vision-language models (VLMs) can automate the analysis of millions of images and posts. The researchers benchmark six promptable VLMs and 15 zero-shot CLIP-like models across two datasets — one expert-annotated (1,038 images) and one large-scale (1.2M images with 50,000 validated labels) — using five annotation dimensions. Gemini-3.1-flash-lite consistently outperformed all other models across both datasets and categories, while performance gaps with open-weight models remained modest. The study also found that chain-of-thought prompting reduced accuracy, while dimension-specific prompt design improved results. Crucially, distributional evaluation revealed that VLMs can reliably capture population-level trends even with moderate per-image accuracy, making them suitable for large-scale discourse analysis.
为大规模分析社交媒体上的气候相关视觉话语，本研究评估了视觉语言模型（VLM）在自动化视觉话语分析中的表现。研究人员在两个数据集上对比了六种可提示的VLM和十五种零样本CLIP类模型，涵盖五个标注维度。Gemini-3.1-flash-lite在所有类别和数据集上表现最佳，与中等规模开源模型的性能差距较小。研究发现，分布评估比单例准确率更能揭示群体趋势，链式思维推理反而降低性能，而针对不同标注维度设计提示可提升效果。
Large Language Models Outperform Humans in Fraud Detection and Resistance to Motivated Investor Pressure
Authors: Nattavudh Powdthavee
First: 2026-04-22T15:03:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:40:50+00:00
Comments: 43 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models trained on human feedback may suppress fraud warnings when investors arrive already persuaded of a fraudulent opportunity. We tested this in a preregistered experiment across seven leading LLMs and twelve investment scenarios covering legitimate, high-risk, and objectively fraudulent opportunities, combining 3,360 AI advisory conversations with a 1,201-participant human benchmark. Contrary to predictions, motivated investor framing did not suppress AI fraud warnings; if anything, it marginally increased them. Endorsement reversal occurred in fewer than 3 in 1,000 observations. Human advisors endorsed fraudulent investments at baseline rates of 13-14%, versus 0% across all LLMs, and suppressed warnings under pressure at two to four times the AI rate. AI systems currently provide more consistent fraud warnings than lay humans in an identical advisory role.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型在欺诈检测及抵御有动机投资者压力方面优于人类
在人类反馈训练的大型语言模型中，当投资者已对欺诈机会持坚定信念时，模型可能抑制欺诈警告。我们在七种主流LLM和十二种投资场景（涵盖合法、高风险及客观欺诈机会）中进行了预注册实验，结合3360次AI咨询对话与1201名人类顾问基准测试。与预测相反，有动机投资者的框架并未抑制AI的欺诈警告，反而略微增加。欺诈投资的背书逆转在不到千分之三的观察中发生。人类顾问在基线状态下背书欺诈投资的比例为13-14%，而所有LLM均为0%；在压力下，人类顾问抑制警告的频率是AI的两到四倍。当前AI系统在相同顾问角色下，比普通人类提供更一致的欺诈警告。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns that large language models (LLMs) might be susceptible to investor bias in fraud detection, this study tested whether LLMs trained on human feedback would suppress fraud warnings when investors were already convinced of fraud. Using a preregistered experiment with seven LLMs and twelve investment scenarios, the study compared 3,360 AI advisory interactions against a human benchmark of 1,201 participants. Contrary to expectations, motivated investor framing did not reduce AI fraud warnings; in fact, it slightly increased them, with endorsement reversal occurring in fewer than 3 in 1,000 cases. Humans endorsed fraudulent investments at 13–14% baseline rates, while all LLMs endorsed none, and humans suppressed warnings under pressure at 2–4 times the rate of AI systems. The results indicate that LLMs provide more consistent fraud warnings than humans in advisory roles.
本研究旨在检验大型语言模型（LLM）在投资者已受欺诈倾向影响时是否会压制欺诈预警。通过预注册实验，对比七种LLM与十二种投资情境下3360次AI咨询与1201名人类顾问的表现，结果发现：投资者受动机影响时，AI并未减少欺诈预警，反而略有增加；欺诈倾向逆转现象罕见（低于千分之三）。人类顾问在基线情况下有13-14%的欺诈投资推荐率，而所有LLM均无推荐；在压力下，人类压制预警的频率是AI的2-4倍。研究显示，AI在相同顾问角色中比人类提供更一致的欺诈预警。
Context Is What You Need: The Maximum Effective Context Window for Real World Limits of LLMs
Authors: Norman Paulsen
First: 2025-09-21T14:38:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:39:44+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 4 charts. AAIML (2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) providers boast big numbers for maximum context window sizes. To test the real world use of context windows, we 1) define a concept of maximum effective context window, 2) formulate a testing method of a context window's effectiveness over various sizes and problem types, and 3) create a standardized way to compare model efficacy for increasingly larger context window sizes to find the point of failure. We collected hundreds of thousands of data points across several models and found significant differences between reported Maximum Context Window (MCW) size and Maximum Effective Context Window (MECW) size. Our findings show that the MECW is, not only, drastically different from the MCW but also shifts based on the problem type. A few top of the line models in our test group failed with as little as 100 tokens in context; most had severe degradation in accuracy by 1000 tokens in context. All models fell far short of their Maximum Context Window by as much as 99 percent. Our data reveals the Maximum Effective Context Window shifts based on the type of problem provided, offering clear and actionable insights into how to improve model accuracy and decrease model hallucination rates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：上下文才是你需要的：大语言模型在现实世界中的最大有效上下文窗口
大型语言模型（LLM）供应商常宣称其最大上下文窗口尺寸很大。为检验上下文窗口在现实中的使用效果，我们1）定义了最大有效上下文窗口的概念，2）制定了评估不同尺寸和问题类型下上下文窗口有效性的方法，3）创建了一种标准化方式，用于比较模型在不断增大上下文窗口尺寸时的效能，以确定失效点。我们收集了数以十万计的数据点，覆盖多个模型，发现报告的最大上下文窗口（MCW）尺寸与最大有效上下文窗口（MECW）尺寸存在显著差异。我们的研究发现，MECW不仅与MCW差异巨大，且随问题类型变化而变化。我们测试组中部分顶尖模型在上下文仅100个token时即失效；多数模型在上下文达1000个token时准确率严重下降。所有模型的实际最大有效上下文窗口均远低于其宣称的最大上下文窗口，差距高达99%。我们的数据表明，最大有效上下文窗口会随所提供问题类型而变化，为提升模型准确率、降低幻觉率提供了清晰且可操作的见解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the discrepancy between advertised and practical context window performance in large language models, this study defines and measures the Maximum Effective Context Window (MECW) as the largest context size where models maintain acceptable accuracy. Using a standardized testing protocol across multiple models and problem types, the authors collected hundreds of thousands of data points to identify where performance degrades. Results show that MECW is often far smaller than the advertised Maximum Context Window (MCW), with top models failing at just 100 tokens and most suffering severe accuracy drops by 1,000 tokens. The MECW varies significantly by problem type, revealing that real-world performance is highly sensitive to context length and offering actionable guidance for improving model reliability and reducing hallucinations.
为揭示大语言模型实际性能与宣传参数之间的差距，本研究定义并测量了最大有效上下文窗口（MECW），即模型在保持可接受准确率的最大上下文长度。通过标准化测试方法，研究人员对多个模型进行了数十万次评估，发现MECW往往远低于厂商宣称的最大上下文窗口（MCW），有时差距高达99%。结果显示，顶尖模型在仅100个token时即出现显著性能下降，多数模型在1000个token时已严重失效，且MECW随问题类型变化而变化，为优化模型应用和降低幻觉提供了实用依据。
SemEval-2026 Task 4: Narrative Story Similarity and Narrative Representation Learning
Authors: Hans Ole Hatzel, Ekaterina Artemova, Haimo Paul Stiemer, Evelyn Gius, Chris Biemann
First: 2026-04-23T15:39:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:39:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present the shared task on narrative similarity and narrative representation learning - NSNRL (pronounced "nass-na-rel"). The task operationalizes narrative similarity as a binary classification problem: determining which of two stories is more similar to an anchor story. We introduce a novel definition of narrative similarity, compatible with both narrative theory and intuitive judgment. Based on the similarity judgments collected under this concept, we also evaluate narrative embedding representations. We collected at least two annotations each for more than 1,000 story summary triples, with each annotation being backed by at least two annotators in agreement. This paper describes the sampling and annotation process for the dataset; further, we give an overview of the submitted systems and the techniques they employ. We received a total of 71 final submissions from 46 teams across our two tracks. In our triple-based classification setup, LLM ensembles make up many of the top-scoring systems, while in the embedding setup, systems with pre- and post-processing on pretrained embedding models perform about on par with custom fine-tuned solutions. Our analysis identifies potential headroom for improvement of automated systems in both tracks. The task website includes visualizations of embeddings alongside instance-level classification results for all teams.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SemEval-2026 任务4：叙事故事相似性与叙事表征学习
我们提出了关于叙事相似性与叙事表征学习的共享任务——NSNRL（发音为“nass-na-rel”）。该任务将叙事相似性定义为二分类问题：判断两个故事中哪一个与锚定故事更相似。我们引入了一种新颖的叙事相似性定义，既符合叙事理论，也符合直觉判断。基于在此概念下收集的相似性判断，我们还评估了叙事嵌入表示。我们收集了超过1000个故事摘要三元组的标注数据，每个三元组至少有两个标注，且每个标注由至少两位一致同意的标注员支持。本文描述了数据集的采样与标注过程；此外，我们概述了提交系统的概况及其所采用的技术。我们共收到46支队伍在两个赛道上的71份最终提交。在基于三元组的分类设置中，许多高分系统采用大语言模型集成；而在嵌入设置中，对预训练嵌入模型进行预处理和后处理的系统表现与定制微调方案大致相当。我们的分析指出了两个赛道中自动化系统仍有改进空间。任务网站提供了所有队伍的嵌入可视化及实例级分类结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for standardized evaluation of narrative similarity and representation learning, this task defines narrative similarity as a binary classification problem where systems determine which of two stories is more similar to an anchor story. Using a novel, theory-aligned definition and over 1,000 annotated story triples (each with dual annotator consensus), the task evaluates narrative embeddings and classification models. Experimental results show that LLM ensembles dominate in the triple-based classification track, while pre- and post-processed pretrained embeddings perform comparably to fine-tuned models in the embedding track, revealing opportunities for further system improvement.
为推动叙事相似度的计算分析，本任务将其定义为二分类问题，判断两篇故事中哪一篇与锚定故事更相似。基于一个与叙事理论相符的新相似度定义，组织者收集了1000多组故事三元组，每组标注均由至少两名标注者确认。在三元组分类赛道中，LLM集成模型表现最佳；在嵌入赛道中，对预训练嵌入模型进行预处理和后处理的系统与自定义微调模型表现相当。分析表明两个赛道仍有提升空间，并提供了所有团队的嵌入可视化与实例级分类结果。
A systematic review of assistive technologies for children with dyslexia
Authors: Sansrit Paudel, Subek Acharya, Piriyankan Kirupaharan, Bishal KC, Bipul Thapa
First: 2024-12-17T18:14:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:38:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dyslexia is a neurological learning disability that primarily disrupts one's ability to read, write, and spell, affecting an estimated 15-20% of the global population. This high prevalence underscores the importance of developing effective interventions. This study presents a systematic literature review conducted between 2015 and 2024 to evaluate current trends in assistive technologies for children with dyslexia. This research shows that digital assistive technologies are leading interventions, especially with the use of mobile apps and augmented reality. More innovative technologies like virtual reality, NLP, haptic technologies, and tangible user interfaces are emerging to provide unique solutions addressing the user's needs. While non-computing devices are generally less effective in comparison to modern digital solutions, they provide a promising alternative in settings with limited access to technology.
中文标题/摘要
标题：阅读障碍儿童辅助技术的系统综述
阅读障碍是一种神经发育性学习障碍，主要影响个体的阅读、写作和拼写能力，全球约有15-20%的人口受影响。该高发病率凸显了开发有效干预措施的重要性。本研究于2015至2024年间进行系统文献综述，评估当前针对阅读障碍儿童的辅助技术趋势。研究显示，数字辅助技术是主流干预手段，尤其是移动应用和增强现实技术的应用。同时，虚拟现实、自然语言处理、触觉技术和实物用户界面等更创新的技术正逐渐出现，为满足用户需求提供独特解决方案。尽管非计算设备相较于现代数字解决方案效果较差，但在技术获取受限的环境中仍具潜在替代价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high global prevalence of dyslexia affecting 15-20% of the population, this study conducted a systematic literature review from 2015 to 2024 to assess assistive technologies for children with dyslexia. The review identified digital tools—particularly mobile apps and augmented reality—as the most effective current interventions, while also noting emerging technologies such as virtual reality, NLP, haptic interfaces, and tangible user interfaces offering novel support. Experimental findings indicate that while non-digital assistive devices are less effective, they remain viable in low-tech environments, highlighting a need for context-sensitive solutions.
鉴于全球约15-20%的人口受诵读障碍影响，本系统综述分析了2015至2024年间针对儿童诵读障碍的辅助技术。研究发现，数字工具如移动应用和增强现实目前占据主导地位，新兴技术如虚拟现实、自然语言处理、触觉界面和实体用户界面正提供更个性化的支持。尽管非数字设备效果较弱，但在技术资源匮乏环境中仍具替代价值。
UsefulBench: Towards Decision-Useful Information as a Target for Information Retrieval
Authors: Tobias Schimanski, Stefanie Lewandowski, Christian Woerle, Nicola Reichenau, Yauheni Huryn, Markus Leippold
First: 2026-04-17T08:29:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:33:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conventional information retrieval is concerned with identifying the relevance of texts for a given query. Yet, the conventional definition of relevance is dominated by aspects of similarity in texts, leaving unobserved whether the text is truly useful for addressing the query. For instance, when answering whether Paris is larger than Berlin, texts about Paris being in France are relevant (lexical/semantic similarity), but not useful. In this paper, we introduce UsefulBench, a domain-specific dataset curated by three professional analysts labeling whether a text is connected to a query (relevance) or holds practical value in responding to it (usefulness). We show that classic similarity-based information retrieval aligns more strongly with relevance. While LLM-based systems can counteract this bias, we find that domain-specific problems require a high degree of expertise, which current LLMs do not fully incorporate. We explore approaches to (partially) overcome this challenge. However, UsefulBench presents a dataset challenge for targeted information retrieval systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UsefulBench：面向决策有用信息的信息检索目标
传统信息检索关注文本与查询的相关性，但传统相关性定义侧重文本相似性，忽视文本是否真正有助于回答查询。例如，回答“巴黎是否比柏林大”时，关于“巴黎位于法国”的文本虽在词义上相关，但无实际用途。本文提出UsefulBench，由三位专业分析师标注文本是否与查询相关（相关性）或是否具有实际响应价值（有用性）。我们发现，基于相似性的经典信息检索更贴近相关性；尽管基于大语言模型的系统可缓解此偏差，但领域特定问题需高度专业性，当前大语言模型尚未完全涵盖。我们探索部分克服该挑战的方法。然而，UsefulBench为面向目标的信息检索系统提出了数据集挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between traditional relevance-based retrieval and actual decision usefulness, this paper introduces UsefulBench, a domain-specific dataset where professional analysts label texts as either relevant (semantically connected) or useful (practically helpful) to a query. The study finds that classic similarity-based retrieval systems perform better on relevance but fail to capture usefulness, while LLM-based systems show partial improvement but still struggle with domain-specific expertise. Experimental results demonstrate that usefulness is a harder target to achieve, revealing a critical challenge for information retrieval systems aiming to deliver actionable information.
为弥合传统相关性检索与实际决策有用性之间的差距，本文提出UsefulBench数据集，由专业分析师标注文本对查询是相关（语义相关）还是有用（实际有用）。实验表明，经典基于相似性的检索系统更擅长相关性判断，但难以捕捉实用性；尽管大语言模型有所改进，仍缺乏领域专业知识。结果表明，实用性是当前信息检索系统难以攻克的挑战，尤其在需要专业判断的场景中。
Reshoot-Anything: A Self-Supervised Model for In-the-Wild Video Reshooting
Authors: Avinash Paliwal, Adithya Iyer, Shivin Yadav, Muhammad Ali Afridi, Midhun Harikumar
First: 2026-04-23T15:32:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:32:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Precise camera control for reshooting dynamic videos is bottlenecked by the severe scarcity of paired multi-view data for non-rigid scenes. We overcome this limitation with a highly scalable self-supervised framework capable of leveraging internet-scale monocular videos. Our core contribution is the generation of pseudo multi-view training triplets, consisting of a source video, a geometric anchor, and a target video. We achieve this by extracting distinct smooth random-walk crop trajectories from a single input video to serve as the source and target views. The anchor is synthetically generated by forward-warping the first frame of the source with a dense tracking field, which effectively simulates the distorted point-cloud inputs expected at inference. Because our independent cropping strategy introduces spatial misalignment and artificial occlusions, the model cannot simply copy information from the current source frame. Instead, it is forced to implicitly learn 4D spatiotemporal structures by actively routing and re-projecting missing high-fidelity textures across distinct times and viewpoints from the source video to reconstruct the target. At inference, our minimally adapted diffusion transformer utilizes a 4D point-cloud derived anchor to achieve state-of-the-art temporal consistency, robust camera control, and high-fidelity novel view synthesis on complex dynamic scenes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Reshoot-Anything：一种面向野外视频重拍的自监督模型
动态视频重拍中精确的相机控制受限于非刚性场景配对多视角数据的严重匮乏。我们通过一种高度可扩展的自监督框架克服这一限制，该框架能够利用互联网规模的单目视频。我们的核心贡献是生成伪多视角训练三元组，包括源视频、几何锚点和目标视频。我们通过从单个输入视频中提取不同的平滑随机游走裁剪轨迹，作为源视图和目标视图。锚点通过将源视频首帧与密集跟踪场前向变形合成，有效模拟推理时预期的失真点云输入。由于我们的独立裁剪策略引入了空间错位和人工遮挡，模型无法简单复制当前源帧信息。相反，它被迫通过主动路由和重新投影源视频中不同时间和视角的高保真纹理，隐式学习4D时空结构以重建目标视频。推理时，我们仅微调的扩散Transformer利用4D点云导出的锚点，实现了复杂动态场景下最先进的时序一致性、鲁棒相机控制和高保真新视角合成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of paired multi-view data for reshooting dynamic videos, the authors propose a self-supervised framework that leverages internet-scale monocular videos. The method generates pseudo multi-view triplets by extracting random-walk crop trajectories from a single video as source and target views, and synthesizing a geometric anchor via dense optical flow warping of the first frame. The model is trained to reconstruct the target view by routing and re-projecting textures across time and space, avoiding direct frame copying. Experimental results show state-of-the-art performance in temporal consistency, camera control, and novel view synthesis on complex dynamic scenes, achieved with minimal inference-time adaptation of a diffusion transformer.
为解决动态视频重拍中配对多视角数据稀缺的问题，该研究提出一种自监督框架，利用海量单目视频进行训练。其核心方法是通过从单个视频中提取随机游走的裁剪轨迹作为源视图和目标视图，并通过稠密光流前向变形第一帧生成几何锚点。模型被迫在时间与空间上主动路由和重投影纹理，隐式学习4D时空结构以重建目标视图。实验表明，该方法在复杂动态场景下实现了最先进的时序一致性、鲁棒相机控制和高保真新视角合成，且推理时仅需微调。
Back to Source: Open-Set Continual Test-Time Adaptation via Domain Compensation
Authors: Yingkai Yang, Chaoqi Chen, Hui Huang
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-23T15:29:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:29:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) aims to mitigate distributional shifts between training and test domains during inference time. However, existing TTA methods fall short in the realistic scenario where models face both continually changing domains and the simultaneous emergence of unknown semantic classes, a challenging setting we term Open-set Continual Test-Time Adaptation (OCTTA). The coupling of domain and semantic shifts often collapses the feature space, severely degrading both classification and out-of-distribution detection. To tackle this, we propose DOmain COmpensation (DOCO), a lightweight and effective framework that robustly performs domain adaptation and OOD detection in a synergistic, closed loop. DOCO first performs dynamic, adaptation-conditioned sample splitting to separate likely ID from OOD samples. Then, using only the ID samples, it learns a domain compensation prompt by aligning feature statistics with the source domain, guided by a structural preservation regularizer that prevents semantic distortion. This learned prompt is then propagated to the OOD samples within the same batch, effectively isolating their semantic novelty for more reliable detection. Extensive experiments on multiple challenging benchmarks demonstrate that DOCO outperforms prior CTTA and OSTTA methods, establishing a new state-of-the-art for the demanding OCTTA setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：回归源域：通过域补偿实现开放集持续测试时自适应
测试时自适应（TTA）旨在缓解推理过程中训练域与测试域之间的分布偏移。然而，现有TTA方法在现实场景中表现不足，即模型需同时应对持续变化的域和未知语义类别的出现，这一挑战性场景我们称为开放集持续测试时自适应（OCTTA）。域与语义偏移的耦合常导致特征空间坍塌，严重损害分类与异常检测性能。为此，我们提出域补偿（DOCO）框架，该框架轻量且高效，通过协同闭环机制稳健实现域自适应与OOD检测。DOCO首先进行动态、自适应条件的样本分割，分离可能的ID样本与OOD样本；随后仅利用ID样本，通过与源域对齐特征统计并结合结构保持正则化器（防止语义扭曲）学习域补偿提示；该提示随后传播至同一批次中的OOD样本，有效隔离其语义新颖性，提升检测可靠性。在多个挑战性基准上的大量实验表明，DOCO优于现有CTTA与OSTTA方法，为OCTTA场景树立了新SOTA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle both continual domain shifts and unknown semantic classes during inference, this paper introduces DOCO, a lightweight framework for Open-set Continual Test-Time Adaptation. It first splits samples dynamically into in-distribution and out-of-distribution groups based on adaptation conditions, then learns a domain compensation prompt using only in-distribution samples to align feature statistics with the source domain while preserving semantic structure. This prompt is then applied to out-of-distribution samples to enhance their isolation and detection. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show DOCO significantly outperforms existing CTTA and OSTTA methods, achieving state-of-the-art results in the challenging OCTTA setting.
为应对推理时持续变化的领域与未知语义类同时出现的挑战，本文提出DOCO框架，一种轻量级的开放集持续测试时自适应方法。该框架首先动态划分样本为已知类与未知类，仅对已知类样本学习域补偿提示，使其特征统计与源域对齐并保持语义结构，再将该提示应用于同一批次的未知类样本以增强其检测可靠性。在多个基准测试中，DOCO在领域自适应与OOD检测方面均显著优于现有方法，确立了该场景下的新最优性能。
Who Defines "Best"? Towards Interactive, User-Defined Evaluation of LLM Leaderboards
Authors: Minji Jung, Minjae Lee, Yejin Kim, Sarang Choi, Minsuk Kahng
First: 2026-04-23T15:28:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:28:32+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 2026 ACM Conference on Fairness, Accountability, and Transparency (FAccT 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM leaderboards are widely used to compare models and guide deployment decisions. However, leaderboard rankings are shaped by evaluation priorities set by benchmark designers, rather than by the diverse goals and constraints of actual users and organizations. A single aggregate score often obscures how models behave across different prompt types and compositions. In this work, we conduct an in-depth analysis of the dataset used in the LMArena (formerly Chatbot Arena) benchmark and investigate this evaluation challenge by designing an interactive visualization interface as a design probe. Our analysis reveals that the dataset is heavily skewed toward certain topics, that model rankings vary across prompt slices, and that preference-based judgments are used in ways that blur their intended scope. Building on this analysis, we introduce a visualization interface that allows users to define their own evaluation priorities by selecting and weighting prompt slices and to explore how rankings change accordingly. A qualitative study suggests that this interactive approach improves transparency and supports more context-specific model evaluation, pointing toward alternative ways to design and use LLM leaderboards.
中文标题/摘要
标题：谁定义“最佳”？迈向交互式、用户自定义的LLM排行榜评估
LLM排行榜广泛用于比较模型并指导部署决策。然而，排行榜排名受基准设计者设定的评估优先级影响，而非实际用户和组织多样化的目标与约束。单一综合得分往往掩盖了模型在不同提示类型和组合下的表现差异。本研究深入分析了LMArena（原Chatbot Arena）基准所用数据集，并通过设计交互式可视化界面作为设计探针，探讨这一评估挑战。分析发现，数据集严重偏向某些主题，模型排名在不同提示切片间存在差异，且偏好型判断被用于模糊其本意范围。基于此分析，我们引入一个可视化界面，允许用户通过选择和加权提示切片来定义自己的评估优先级，并探索排名如何相应变化。定性研究表明，这种交互式方法提升了透明度，支持更情境化的模型评估，为LLM排行榜的设计与使用提供了替代路径。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitation that LLM leaderboards reflect benchmark designers' priorities rather than users' diverse needs, often obscuring model performance across different prompt types. The authors analyze the LMArena dataset, revealing topic biases and inconsistent rankings across prompt slices, and introduce an interactive visualization tool enabling users to define and weight their own evaluation criteria. Experimental results from a qualitative study indicate that this user-defined approach enhances transparency and supports more context-sensitive model comparison, suggesting a more flexible and equitable way to evaluate LLMs.
鉴于现有大模型排行榜反映的是评测设计者的优先级而非用户的多样化需求，本研究分析了LMArena数据集并设计了一款交互式可视化工具，使用户能自定义评价标准。该方法允许用户选择并加权不同提示切片，观察模型排名随评价标准变化的情况。实验结果表明，不同提示类型下排名差异显著，数据集存在特定主题偏倚，偏好判断常被误用；交互式界面提升了透明度，支持情境化评估，为排行榜设计提供了更灵活的替代方案。
Misinformation Span Detection in Videos via Audio Transcripts
Authors: Breno Matos, Rennan C. Lima, Savvas Zannettou, Fabricio Benevenuto, Rodrygo L. T. Santos
First: 2026-04-23T15:23:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:23:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICWSM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Online misinformation is one of the most challenging issues lately, yielding severe consequences, including political polarization, attacks on democracy, and public health risks. Misinformation manifests in any platform with a large user base, including online social networks and messaging apps. It permeates all media and content forms, including images, text, audio, and video. Distinctly, video-based misinformation represents a multifaceted challenge for fact-checkers, given the ease with which individuals can record and upload videos on various video-sharing platforms. Previous research efforts investigated detecting video-based misinformation, focusing on whether a video shares misinformation or not on a video level. While this approach is useful, it only provides a limited and non-easily interpretable view of the problem given that it does not provide an additional context of when misinformation occurs within videos and what content (i.e., claims) are responsible for the video's misinformation nature. In this work, we attempt to bridge this research gap by creating two novel datasets that allow us to explore misinformation detection on videos via audio transcripts, focusing on identifying the span of videos that are responsible for the video's misinformation claim (misinformation span detection). We present two new datasets for this task. We transcribe each video's audio to text, identifying the video segment in which the misinformation claims appears, resulting in two datasets of more than 500 videos with over 2,400 segments containing annotated fact-checked claims. Then, we employ classifiers built with state-of-the-art language models, and our results show that we can identify in which part of a video there is misinformation with an F1 score of 0.68. We make publicly available our annotated datasets. We also release all transcripts, audio and videos.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过音频转录检测视频中的虚假信息跨度
网络虚假信息是当前最具挑战性的问题之一，带来严重后果，包括政治极化、民主攻击和公共健康风险。虚假信息在任何拥有大量用户的平台上都存在，包括在线社交网络和即时通讯应用，渗透于图像、文本、音频和视频等各种媒体和内容形式。尤其值得注意的是，基于视频的虚假信息对事实核查者构成多维度挑战，因为用户可轻松在各类视频分享平台上录制并上传视频。以往研究主要关注视频是否整体传播虚假信息，但该方法仅提供有限且难以解释的视角，因为它未提供虚假信息在视频中具体出现的时间段及导致虚假性质的具体内容（即声明）。本研究旨在弥合这一研究空白，通过创建两个新数据集，探索通过音频转录检测视频中的虚假信息，重点识别导致视频虚假声明的视频片段（虚假信息跨度检测）。我们构建了两个新数据集：将每个视频的音频转录为文本，标注虚假声明出现的视频片段，形成包含500多个视频、2400多个标注事实核查声明片段的数据集。随后，我们使用基于最新语言模型的分类器，结果显示可在视频中识别虚假信息出现的片段，F1分数达0.68。我们公开发布标注数据集，同时提供所有转录文本、音频和视频。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the urgent need to address video-based misinformation and its societal harms, this study introduces a novel approach to detect the specific temporal spans within videos that contain false claims, using audio transcripts as input. Two new annotated datasets, comprising over 500 videos and 2,400 labeled segments, were created by transcribing audio and pinpointing misinformation claims. The method employs state-of-the-art language models to classify these spans, achieving an F1 score of 0.68, demonstrating that misinformation can be localized with reasonable accuracy. The datasets and transcripts are publicly released to support further research.
为应对视频类虚假信息带来的复杂挑战，本研究构建了两个新数据集，通过音频转录文本定位视频中包含虚假信息的具体时间片段。方法包括将视频音频转写为文本，标注含事实核查结果的虚假信息段落，并基于前沿语言模型训练分类器识别这些片段。实验结果显示，该方法在定位虚假信息片段上达到0.68的F1分数，相比以往仅识别视频整体是否含虚假信息的方法，提供了更精细、可解释的分析能力。
AUDITA: A New Dataset to Audit Humans vs. AI Skill at Audio QA
Authors: Tasnim Kabir, Dmytro Kurdydyk, Aadi Palnitkar, Liam Dorn, Ahmed Haj Ahmed, Jordan Lee Boyd-Graber
First: 2026-04-23T15:22:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:22:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing audio question answering benchmarks largely emphasize sound event classification or caption-grounded queries, often enabling models to succeed through shortcut strategies, short-duration cues, lexical priors, dataset-specific biases, or even bypassing audio via metadata and captions rather than genuine reasoning Thus, we present AUDITA (Audio Understanding from Diverse Internet Trivia Authors), a large-scale, real-world benchmark to rigorously evaluate audio reasoning beyond surface-level acoustic recognition. AUDITA comprises carefully curated, human-authored trivia questions grounded in real-world audio, designed to stress robust auditory reasoning through challenging distractors and long-range temporal dependencies, using probing queries that cannot be answered from isolated text or sound cues alone. Human average accuracy of 32.13% shows both the challenge of the task while demonstrating meaningful comprehension of the audio. In stark contrast, state of-the-art audio question answering models perform poorly, with average accuracy below 8.86%. Beyond raw accuracy, we apply Item Response Theory (IRT) to estimate latent proficiency, question difficulty, and expose systematic deficiencies of the models and data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AUDITA：用于审计人类与AI在音频问答中能力的新数据集
现有音频问答基准主要强调声音事件分类或基于字幕的查询，常使模型通过捷径策略、短时长线索、词汇先验、数据集特定偏差，甚至绕过音频仅依赖元数据和字幕来取得成功。为此，我们提出AUDITA（来自多样互联网 trivia 作者的音频理解），一个大规模真实世界基准，旨在严格评估超越表面声学识别的音频推理能力。AUDITA包含精心策划、由人类编写的基于真实音频的趣味问答，通过具有挑战性的干扰项和长时序依赖关系，设计出无法仅凭孤立文本或声音线索回答的探测性问题。人类平均准确率为32.13%，既体现了任务的难度，也表明对音频有实质性理解。相比之下，最先进的音频问答模型表现不佳，平均准确率低于8.86%。除准确率外，我们应用项目反应理论（IRT）估计潜在能力、题目难度，并揭示模型与数据的系统性缺陷。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that existing audio QA benchmarks allow models to exploit shortcuts like metadata or lexical cues rather than true auditory reasoning, the authors introduce AUDITA, a large-scale, real-world dataset of human-authored trivia questions grounded in diverse audio clips. The dataset is designed to challenge models with distractors and long-range temporal dependencies that require genuine audio understanding. Experimental results show that humans achieve an average accuracy of 32.13%, while state-of-the-art audio QA models score below 8.86%, highlighting a significant performance gap. Further analysis using Item Response Theory reveals systematic model deficiencies and question difficulty patterns, underscoring the need for more robust audio reasoning benchmarks.
鉴于现有音频问答基准允许模型通过捷径而非真实听觉推理取得成功，作者提出了AUDITA数据集，包含大量基于真实音频的人类编撰的趣味问答题，旨在通过长时序依赖和干扰项挑战模型。实验表明，人类平均准确率为32.13%，而最先进模型准确率低于8.86%，差距显著。进一步采用项目反应理论分析发现模型存在系统性缺陷，揭示了数据与任务难度分布，凸显了构建更强大音频推理基准的必要性。
ReFACT: A Benchmark for Scientific Confabulation Detection with Positional Error Annotations
Authors: Yindong Wang, Martin Preiß, Margarita Bugueño, Jan Vincent Hoffbauer, Abdullatif Ghajar, Tolga Buz, Gerard de Melo
Venue: Proceedings of the 19th Conference of the European Chapter of the Association for Computational Linguistics (Volume 1: Long Papers), pages 8174-8187, Rabat, Morocco, 2026
First: 2025-09-30T07:06:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:21:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted to EACL 2026 (Main Conference, Oral presentation)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The mechanisms underlying scientific confabulation in Large Language Models (LLMs) remain poorly understood. We introduce ReFACT (Reddit False And Correct Texts), a benchmark of 1,001 expert-annotated question-answer pairs with span-level error annotations derived from Reddit's r/AskScience. Evaluating 9 state-of-the-art LLMs reveals two critical limitations. First, models exhibit a dominant "salient distractor" failure mode: 61% of incorrect span predictions are semantically unrelated to actual errors. Crucially, this pattern persists across all model scales (1B to 70B), indicating a fundamental semantic grounding deficit that scaling alone fails to resolve. Second, we find that comparative judgment is paradoxically harder than independent detection, even GPT-4o's F1 score drops from 0.67 to 0.53 when comparing answers side-by-side. These findings directly challenge the reliability of LLM-as-Judge paradigms for scientific factuality. Code and data are released at https://github.com/ddz5431/ReFACT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReFACT：带位置错误标注的科学虚构检测基准
大型语言模型（LLM）中科学虚构的内在机制仍不明确。我们引入ReFACT（Reddit虚假与正确文本），该基准包含1001对专家标注的问答对，其跨度级错误标注源自Reddit的r/AskScience。评估9个最先进的LLM模型揭示了两个关键局限：首先，模型表现出主导性的“显著干扰项”失败模式——61%的错误跨度预测在语义上与实际错误无关，且该模式在所有模型规模（1B至70B）中均存在，表明单纯扩大规模无法解决根本性的语义锚定缺陷；其次，我们发现比较判断比独立检测更困难，即使GPT-4o的F1分数从0.67降至0.53。这些发现直接质疑了LLM作为裁判在科学事实性评估中的可靠性。代码与数据已发布于https://github.com/ddz5431/ReFACT。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to better understand scientific confabulation in LLMs, the authors introduce ReFACT, a benchmark of 1,001 expert-annotated QA pairs with span-level error annotations from Reddit’s r/AskScience. Evaluating nine state-of-the-art models, they find that 61% of incorrect predictions are semantically unrelated to actual errors—a 'salient distractor' failure mode that persists across model scales from 1B to 70B parameters, revealing a fundamental semantic grounding deficit. Additionally, comparative judgment tasks are harder than independent detection, with GPT-4o’s F1 score dropping from 0.67 to 0.53 when comparing answers side-by-side, challenging the reliability of LLM-as-Judge approaches for scientific factuality.
为深入理解大语言模型在生成科学错误答案时的机制，作者构建了ReFACT数据集，包含1001个专家标注的问答对，并附带位置级错误标注，数据源自Reddit的r/AskScience板块。评估九个前沿模型发现，模型常输出语义无关的干扰项，该错误模式在1B至70B参数规模模型中普遍存在，揭示了模型在语义理解上的根本缺陷，且单纯扩大模型规模无法解决。此外，研究发现对比判断任务比独立检测更易出错，GPT-4o的F1分数从0.67骤降至0.53，质疑了“大模型作为裁判”在科学事实性评估中的可靠性。
PrismaDV: Automated Task-Aware Data Unit Test Generation
Authors: Hao Chen, Arnab Phani, Sebastian Schelter
First: 2026-04-23T15:18:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:18:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data is a central resource for modern enterprises, and data validation is essential for ensuring the reliability of downstream applications. However, existing automated data unit testing frameworks are largely task-agnostic: they validate datasets without considering the semantics and requirements of the code that consumes the data. We present PrismaDV, a compound AI system that analyzes downstream task code together with dataset profiles to identify data access patterns, infer implicit data assumptions, and generate task-aware executable data unit tests. To further adapt the data unit tests over time to specific datasets and downstream tasks, we propose "Selective Informative Feedback for Task Adaptation" (SIFTA), a prompt-optimization framework that leverages the scarce outcomes from the execution of data unit tests and downstream tasks. We evaluate PrismaDV on two new benchmarks spanning 60 tasks across five datasets, where it consistently outperforms both task-agnostic and task-aware baselines in generating unit tests that reflect the end-to-end impact of data errors. Furthermore, we show that with SIFTA, we can automatically learn prompts for PrismaDV's modules that outperform prompts written by hand or generated from a generic prompt optimizer. We publicly release our benchmarks and prototype implementation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PrismaDV：自动化任务感知型数据单元测试生成
数据是现代企业的核心资源，数据验证对于确保下游应用的可靠性至关重要。然而，现有的自动化数据单元测试框架大多与任务无关：它们验证数据集时未考虑消费该数据的代码的语义和需求。 我们提出了PrismaDV，一个复合型AI系统，通过分析下游任务代码与数据集概况，识别数据访问模式、推断隐含数据假设，并生成任务感知型可执行数据单元测试。为使数据单元测试随时间适应特定数据集和下游任务，我们提出“任务自适应选择性信息反馈”（SIFTA），一种利用数据单元测试和下游任务执行稀疏结果的提示优化框架。我们在涵盖五个数据集、60个任务的两个新基准上评估PrismaDV，结果表明其在生成反映数据错误端到端影响的单元测试方面，持续优于任务无关和任务感知基线。此外，我们证明，借助SIFTA，可自动学习优于人工编写或通用提示优化器生成的提示，用于PrismaDV各模块。我们公开发布基准和原型实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to validate data in a way that aligns with downstream task semantics, PrismaDV integrates task code analysis with dataset profiling to infer data assumptions and generate executable, task-aware unit tests. It employs a compound AI system that identifies data access patterns and uses Selective Informative Feedback for Task Adaptation (SIFTA) to refine test generation via prompt optimization based on sparse execution feedback. Evaluated across 60 tasks and five datasets, PrismaDV consistently outperforms both task-agnostic and task-aware baselines in producing tests that accurately reflect the end-to-end impact of data errors, and SIFTA-generated prompts surpass manually crafted or generic ones.
为解决现有数据单元测试框架缺乏任务感知的问题，PrismaDV结合代码分析与数据集画像，推断数据隐含假设并生成任务感知的测试用例。其采用复合AI系统识别数据访问模式，并通过“选择性信息反馈任务自适应”（SIFTA）框架，利用稀疏执行反馈优化提示词以提升测试生成效果。在涵盖60个任务和五个数据集的新基准测试中，PrismaDV在反映数据错误端到端影响的测试生成上持续优于任务无关和任务感知基线，且SIFTA自动生成的提示词优于人工编写或通用提示优化器生成的提示词。
MaskDiME: Adaptive Masked Diffusion for Precise and Efficient Visual Counterfactual Explanations
Authors: Changlu Guo, Anders Nymark Christensen, Anders Bjorholm Dahl, Morten Rieger Hannemose
First: 2026-02-21T10:53:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:15:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual counterfactual explanations aim to reveal the minimal semantic modifications that can alter a model's prediction, providing causal and interpretable insights into deep neural networks. However, existing diffusion-based counterfactual generation methods are often computationally expensive, slow to sample, and imprecise in localizing the modified regions. To address these limitations, we propose MaskDiME, a simple, fast, yet effective diffusion framework that unifies semantic consistency and spatial precision through localized sampling. Our approach adaptively focuses on decision-relevant regions to achieve localized and semantically consistent counterfactual generation while preserving high image fidelity. Our training-free framework, MaskDiME, performs inference over 30x faster than the baseline and achieves comparable or state-of-the-art performance across five benchmark datasets spanning diverse visual domains, establishing a practical and generalizable solution for efficient counterfactual explanation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MaskDiME：用于精确高效视觉反事实解释的自适应掩码扩散方法
视觉反事实解释旨在揭示能够改变模型预测的最小语义修改，为深度神经网络提供因果且可解释的洞察。然而，现有基于扩散的反事实生成方法往往计算成本高、采样慢、在定位修改区域方面不够精确。为解决这些问题，我们提出MaskDiME，一种简单、快速且有效的扩散框架，通过局部采样统一语义一致性和空间精度。我们的方法自适应聚焦于决策相关区域，实现局部且语义一致的反事实生成，同时保持高图像保真度。我们的无训练框架MaskDiME推理速度比基线快30倍，并在涵盖多样视觉领域的五个基准数据集上达到可比或最先进的性能，为高效反事实解释提供了一种实用且可推广的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and precise visual counterfactual explanations that reveal minimal semantic changes altering model predictions, MaskDiME introduces an adaptive masked diffusion framework that localizes sampling to decision-relevant regions, ensuring both semantic consistency and spatial precision without requiring training. By focusing diffusion updates only where necessary, the method achieves inference speeds 30x faster than existing baselines while maintaining or surpassing state-of-the-art performance across five diverse visual benchmarks, demonstrating its practicality and generalizability for interpretable AI.
为解决现有扩散方法在生成视觉反事实解释时计算昂贵、采样慢且定位不精确的问题，MaskDiME提出一种自适应局部采样扩散框架，仅在决策相关区域进行采样，兼顾语义一致性与空间精度，同时保持图像保真度。该方法无需训练，推理速度比基线快30倍，且在五个不同视觉领域的基准数据集上达到或超越当前最优性能，验证了其高效、通用的反事实解释能力。
FunduSegmenter: Leveraging the RETFound Foundation Model for Joint Optic Disc and Optic Cup Segmentation in Retinal Fundus Images
Authors: Zhenyi Zhao, Muthu Rama Krishnan Mookiah, Emanuele Trucco
First: 2025-08-15T09:43:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-23T15:14:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Purpose: This study introduces the first adaptation of RETFound for joint optic disc (OD) and optic cup (OC) segmentation. RETFound is a well-known foundation model developed for fundus camera and optical coherence tomography images, which has shown promising performance in disease diagnosis. Methods: We propose FunduSegmenter, a model integrating a series of novel modules with RETFound, including a Pre-adapter, a Decoder, a Post-adapter, skip connections with Convolutional Block Attention Module and a Vision Transformer block adapter. The model is evaluated on a proprietary dataset, GoDARTS, and four public datasets, IDRiD, Drishti-GS, RIM-ONE-r3, and REFUGE, through internal verification, external verification and domain generalization experiments. Results: An average Dice similarity coefficient of 90.51% was achieved in internal verification, which outperformed all baselines, some substantially (nnU-Net: 82.91%; DUNet: 89.17%; TransUNet: 87.91%). In all external verification experiments, the average results were about 3% higher than those of the best baseline, and our model was also competitive in domain generalization. Conclusions: This study explored the potential of the latent general representations learned by RETFound for OD and OC segmentation in fundus camera images. Our FunduSegmenter generally outperformed state-of-the-art baseline methods. The proposed modules are general and can be extended to fine-tuning other foundation models. Translational Relevance: The model shows strong stability and generalization on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data, providing stable OD and OC segmentation. This is an essential step for many automated tasks, from setting the accurate retinal coordinate to biomarker discovery. The code and trained weights are available at: https://github.com/JusticeZzy/FunduSegmenter.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FunduSegmenter：利用RETFound基础模型实现视网膜图像中视盘与视杯的联合分割
目的：本研究首次将RETFound适配用于视盘（OD）与视杯（OC）的联合分割。RETFound是专为眼底相机和光学相干断层扫描图像开发的知名基础模型，在疾病诊断中表现优异。方法：我们提出FunduSegmenter模型，该模型在RETFound基础上集成了一系列新模块，包括预适配器、解码器、后适配器、与卷积块注意力模块和视觉Transformer块适配器的跳跃连接。模型在自有数据集GoDARTS及四个公开数据集IDRiD、Drishti-GS、RIM-ONE-r3和REFUGE上，通过内部验证、外部验证和领域泛化实验进行评估。结果：内部验证中平均Dice相似系数达90.51%，优于所有基线模型（nnU-Net: 82.91%；DUNet: 89.17%；TransUNet: 87.91%）。在所有外部验证实验中，平均结果比最佳基线高约3%，且在领域泛化中表现也具竞争力。结论：本研究探索了RETFound所学习的潜在通用表征在眼底相机图像中OD与OC分割中的潜力。我们的FunduSegmenter整体优于当前最先进基线方法。所提模块具通用性，可扩展至其他基础模型的微调。转化相关性：该模型在分布内与分布外数据上均表现出强稳定性与泛化能力，提供可靠的OD与OC分割，是诸多自动化任务（如准确视网膜坐标设定与生物标志物发现）的关键步骤。代码与训练权重见：https://github.com/JusticeZzy/FunduSegmenter。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate joint segmentation of optic disc and optic cup in retinal fundus images, this study adapts the RETFound foundation model into FunduSegmenter by integrating novel modules including a Pre-adapter, Decoder, Post-adapter, skip connections with Convolutional Block Attention Module, and a Vision Transformer block adapter. Evaluated on GoDARTS and four public datasets through internal, external verification, and domain generalization experiments, FunduSegmenter achieved an average Dice score of 90.51% in internal validation, significantly outperforming baselines like nnU-Net (82.91%), DUNet (89.17%), and TransUNet (87.91%). In external tests, it consistently scored about 3% higher than the best baseline, demonstrating strong generalization across domains.
为实现视网膜图像中视盘与视杯的联合精准分割，本研究将RETFound基础模型适配为FunduSegmenter，引入预适配器、解码器、后适配器、CBAM跳跃连接及视觉Transformer块适配器等新模块。在GoDARTS、IDRiD、Drishti-GS、RIM-ONE-r3和REFUGE等多个数据集上进行内部验证、外部验证及域泛化测试，模型在内部验证中平均Dice系数达90.51%，显著优于nnU-Net（82.91%）、DUNet（89.17%）和TransUNet（87.91%）。在外部验证与域泛化测试中，模型性能比最佳基线高出约3%，展现出优异的跨域稳定性和泛化能力。
